import {
  getTaskIntelligenceEngine,
  type TaskDomain,
  type TaskIntelligenceProfile,
  type TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
} from "./taskIntelligence";

export type InputModality =
  | "typed"
  | "spoken";

export type InterpretedIntentKind =
  | "ask"
  | "plan"
  | "create"
  | "modify"
  | "execute"
  | "continue"
  | "remember"
  | "recall"
  | "discuss"
  | "unknown";

export interface RawHumanInput {
  modality: InputModality;
  text: string;

  turnId?: string;
  receivedAt?: string;
}

export interface InterpretedConstraint {
  kind:
    | "explicit"
    | "inferred";

  text: string;
  sourceText?: string;
}

export interface InterpretedQuestion {
  id: string;
  question: string;
  blocking: boolean;
  reason: string;
}

export interface SemanticInterpretationDraft {
  intentKind?: InterpretedIntentKind;
  goal?: string;
  desiredOutcome?: string;

  explicitConstraints?: string[];
  inferredConstraints?: string[];

  unresolvedQuestions?: Array<
    Omit<
      InterpretedQuestion,
      "id"
    >
  >;

  domains?: TaskDomain[];
  requiredTools?: string[];

  estimatedSteps?: number;
  dependencyCount?: number;
  ambiguityLevel?: number;
  noveltyLevel?: number;

  creativeGeneration?: number;
  styleSensitivity?: number;

  factualVerification?: number;
  evidenceDependency?: number;
  consequenceSeverity?: number;

  reasoningSteps?: number;
  crossDomainReasoning?: boolean;

  containsPersonalData?: boolean;
  containsSecrets?: boolean;
  containsFinancialData?: boolean;
  containsHealthData?: boolean;
  containsCredentials?: boolean;

  requestedLatencyMs?: number;
  canRunAsynchronously?: boolean;

  minimizeCost?: boolean;
  costCeilingRelative?: number;

  confidence?: number;
}

export interface InputInterpretation {
  raw: RawHumanInput;

  normalizedText: string;

  intent: {
    kind: InterpretedIntentKind;
    confidence: number;
  };

  goal: {
    summary: string;
    confidence: number;
  };

  desiredOutcome: {
    summary: string;
    confidence: number;
  };

  constraints: InterpretedConstraint[];

  unresolvedQuestions: InterpretedQuestion[];
  clarificationRequired: boolean;

  completeness: number;
  ambiguity: number;

  taskSignals: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput;
  taskIntelligence: TaskIntelligenceProfile;

  interpretedAt: string;
}

export type InputSemanticAnalyzer =
  (
    input: RawHumanInput,
    normalizedText: string,
  ) =>
    Promise<SemanticInterpretationDraft>
    | SemanticInterpretationDraft;

function clamp01(
  value: number | undefined,
  fallback = 0.5,
): number {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function requireText(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function uniqueText(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (values ?? [])
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value
              .replace(
                /\s+/g,
                " ",
              )
              .trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function normalizeSpokenText(
  value: string,
): string {
  return value
    .replace(
      /\b(?:um+|uh+|erm+|hmm+)\b[,.]?\s*/gi,
      " ",
    )
    .replace(
      /\b(\w+)(?:\s+\1){1,2}\b/gi,
      "$1",
    )
    .replace(
      /\s+([,.!?;:])/g,
      "$1",
    )
    .replace(
      /\s+/g,
      " ",
    )
    .trim();
}

function normalizeTypedText(
  value: string,
): string {
  return value
    .replace(
      /\r\n/g,
      "\n",
    )
    .replace(
      /[ \t]+/g,
      " ",
    )
    .replace(
      /\n{3,}/g,
      "\n\n",
    )
    .trim();
}

export function normalizeHumanInput(
  input: RawHumanInput,
): string {
  const raw =
    requireText(
      input.text,
      "raw input text",
    );

  return input.modality ===
    "spoken"
      ? normalizeSpokenText(
          raw,
        )
      : normalizeTypedText(
          raw,
        );
}

function detectIntentKind(
  text: string,
): InterpretedIntentKind {
  const lower =
    text.toLowerCase();

  if (
    /\b(?:continue|carry on|resume|keep going|pick up)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "continue";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:remember|save this|keep this in mind)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "remember";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:what do you remember|recall|do you know|what did i say)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "recall";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:plan|roadmap|outline|break down|sequence)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "plan";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:create|build|write|draft|generate|make|design)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "create";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:change|edit|modify|update|repair|fix|replace|rewrite|refactor)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "modify";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:run|execute|send|open|delete|deploy|publish|install|apply)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "execute";
  }

  if (
    /\?$/.test(
      text,
    ) ||
    /^(?:what|why|how|when|where|who|which|can|could|should|would|is|are|do|does)\b/i.test(
      text,
    )
  ) {
    return "ask";
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:talk about|discuss|thoughts on|tell me about)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    return "discuss";
  }

  return "unknown";
}

function inferDomains(
  text: string,
  kind: InterpretedIntentKind,
): TaskDomain[] {
  const lower =
    text.toLowerCase();

  const domains:
    TaskDomain[] =
      [];

  const add =
    (
      domain:
        TaskDomain,
      pattern:
        RegExp,
    ) => {
      if (
        pattern.test(
          lower,
        )
      ) {
        domains.push(
          domain,
        );
      }
    };

  add(
    "coding",
    /\b(?:code|typescript|javascript|python|repo|repository|api|function|class|compile|typecheck|bug|refactor|patch)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "research",
    /\b(?:research|investigate|sources?|search|look up|compare evidence|study)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "factual",
    /\b(?:verify|fact|accurate|current|latest|evidence|citation|check whether)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "creative",
    /\b(?:story|chapter|character|fiction|scene|poem|creative|art|image|design|worldbuilding)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "planning",
    /\b(?:plan|roadmap|milestone|schedule|sequence|strategy)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "personal-assistance",
    /\b(?:calendar|email|reminder|appointment|shift|schedule me|my day|my tasks?)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "system-operations",
    /\b(?:deploy|server|service|system|process|install|runtime|restart|terminal|powershell)\b/,
  );

  add(
    "data-analysis",
    /\b(?:analy[sz]e|dataset|spreadsheet|csv|statistics|chart|metrics)\b/,
  );

  if (
    domains.length ===
      0 &&
    kind ===
      "plan"
  ) {
    domains.push(
      "planning",
    );
  }

  if (
    domains.length ===
      0 &&
    (
      kind ===
        "discuss" ||
      kind ===
        "ask"
    )
  ) {
    domains.push(
      "conversation",
    );
  }

  if (
    domains.length ===
    0
  ) {
    domains.push(
      "general",
    );
  }

  return [
    ...new Set(
      domains,
    ),
  ];
}

function inferTools(
  text: string,
): string[] {
  const lower =
    text.toLowerCase();

  const tools:
    string[] =
      [];

  if (
    /\b(?:web|internet|search|look up|latest|current|sources?)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    tools.push(
      "web",
    );
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:file|document|pdf|spreadsheet|repo|repository|attachment|folder)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    tools.push(
      "files",
    );
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:calendar|meeting|appointment|schedule)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    tools.push(
      "calendar",
    );
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:email|inbox|mail|reply|message)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    tools.push(
      "email",
    );
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:run|execute|terminal|powershell|shell|install|typecheck|test suite)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    tools.push(
      "runtime",
    );
  }

  return [
    ...new Set(
      tools,
    ),
  ].sort();
}

function extractExplicitConstraints(
  text: string,
): string[] {
  const constraints:
    string[] =
      [];

  const patterns = [
    /\b(?:must|must not|don't|do not|only|without|keep|preserve|avoid|never|no)\b[^.!?\n]*/gi,
    /\b(?:under|within|at most|at least)\s+[^.!?\n]*/gi,
  ];

  for (
    const pattern
    of patterns
  ) {
    for (
      const match
      of text.matchAll(
        pattern,
      )
    ) {
      const value =
        match[0]
          ?.trim();

      if (value) {
        constraints.push(
          value,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  return uniqueText(
    constraints,
  );
}

function inferAmbiguity(
  text: string,
  kind: InterpretedIntentKind,
): {
  level: number;
  questions:
    Array<
      Omit<
        InterpretedQuestion,
        "id"
      >
    >;
} {
  const lower =
    text.toLowerCase();

  const questions:
    Array<
      Omit<
        InterpretedQuestion,
        "id"
      >
    > =
      [];

  let level =
    0;

  const ambiguousReference =
    /\b(?:it|that|that thing|the thing|this|those|them)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  const concreteContext =
    /\b(?:file|document|repo|repository|project|chapter|model|website|book|calendar|email|task|code|function|class|roadmap|milestone)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  if (
    ambiguousReference &&
    !concreteContext &&
    text.split(
      /\s+/,
    ).length <=
      12
  ) {
    level =
      Math.max(
        level,
        0.85,
      );

    questions.push({
      question:
        "What specific item or prior subject does the request refer to?",
      blocking:
        kind ===
          "modify" ||
        kind ===
          "execute" ||
        kind ===
          "continue",
      reason:
        "The input contains an unresolved reference without a concrete target.",
    });
  }

  if (
    /(?:\b(?:and|but|because|so|to|the|a|an)\b)\s*[.!?]?$/.test(
      lower,
    )
  ) {
    level =
      Math.max(
        level,
        0.8,
      );

    questions.push({
      question:
        "Was the input cut off before the request was complete?",
      blocking:
        true,
      reason:
        "The utterance ends with a syntactically incomplete connector or article.",
    });
  }

  if (
    kind ===
      "unknown"
  ) {
    level =
      Math.max(
        level,
        0.6,
      );

    questions.push({
      question:
        "What outcome should Chernobog produce from this input?",
      blocking:
        true,
      reason:
        "No clear request type could be determined.",
    });
  }

  return {
    level,
    questions,
  };
}

function estimateSteps(
  text: string,
  kind: InterpretedIntentKind,
): number {
  let steps =
    1;

  const connectors =
    text.match(
      /\b(?:then|after that|next|finally|and then)\b/gi,
    )?.length ??
    0;

  steps +=
    connectors;

  if (
    kind ===
      "plan"
  ) {
    steps =
      Math.max(
        steps,
        3,
      );
  }

  if (
    /\b(?:end[- ]to[- ]end|full|complete|entire|migration|overhaul)\b/i.test(
      text,
    )
  ) {
    steps =
      Math.max(
        steps,
        5,
      );
  }

  return steps;
}

function defaultSemanticAnalyzer(
  input: RawHumanInput,
  normalizedText: string,
): SemanticInterpretationDraft {
  const kind =
    detectIntentKind(
      normalizedText,
    );

  const ambiguity =
    inferAmbiguity(
      normalizedText,
      kind,
    );

  const domains =
    inferDomains(
      normalizedText,
      kind,
    );

  const lower =
    normalizedText.toLowerCase();

  const creative =
    domains.includes(
      "creative",
    )
      ? 0.9
      : /\b(?:write|design|generate|create)\b/.test(
            lower,
          )
        ? 0.45
        : 0.05;

  const style =
    /\b(?:style|voice|tone|aesthetic|match|feel)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
      ? 0.85
      : creative >
          0.5
        ? 0.5
        : 0.05;

  const factual =
    domains.includes(
      "factual",
    ) ||
    domains.includes(
      "research",
    )
      ? 0.9
      : /\b(?:check|verify|accurate|correct|current|latest)\b/.test(
            lower,
          )
        ? 0.8
        : 0.25;

  const evidence =
    /\b(?:research|sources?|citation|evidence|verify|latest|current)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
      ? 0.9
      : 0.1;

  const consequence =
    /\b(?:production|deploy|delete|send|publish|payment|security|credentials?|medical|financial)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
      ? 0.85
      : 0.25;

  const containsCredentials =
    /\b(?:password|api key|secret key|token|credential)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  const containsHealthData =
    /\b(?:health|medical|doctor|diagnosis|medication|symptom)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  const containsFinancialData =
    /\b(?:bank|card|payment|salary|tax|financial)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  const containsPersonalData =
    /\b(?:my calendar|my email|my schedule|my account|my address|my phone|my shift)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  const requestedLatencyMs =
    /\b(?:right now|immediately|asap)\b/.test(
      lower,
    )
      ? 1_500
      : /\b(?:quickly|fast|soon)\b/.test(
            lower,
          )
        ? 10_000
        : undefined;

  const minimizeCost =
    /\b(?:free|cheap|cheapest|minimi[sz]e cost|low cost)\b/.test(
      lower,
    );

  return {
    intentKind:
      kind,
    goal:
      normalizedText,
    desiredOutcome:
      normalizedText,
    explicitConstraints:
      extractExplicitConstraints(
        normalizedText,
      ),
    inferredConstraints:
      [],
    unresolvedQuestions:
      ambiguity.questions,
    domains,
    requiredTools:
      inferTools(
        normalizedText,
      ),
    estimatedSteps:
      estimateSteps(
        normalizedText,
        kind,
      ),
    dependencyCount:
      /\b(?:depends on|dependency|blocked by|requires)\b/.test(
        lower,
      )
        ? 1
        : 0,
    ambiguityLevel:
      ambiguity.level,
    noveltyLevel:
      /\b(?:new|novel|first time|unknown|experimental)\b/.test(
        lower,
      )
        ? 0.7
        : 0.25,
    creativeGeneration:
      creative,
    styleSensitivity:
      style,
    factualVerification:
      factual,
    evidenceDependency:
      evidence,
    consequenceSeverity:
      consequence,
    reasoningSteps:
      estimateSteps(
        normalizedText,
        kind,
      ),
    crossDomainReasoning:
      domains.length >
      1,
    containsPersonalData,
    containsSecrets:
      containsCredentials,
    containsFinancialData,
    containsHealthData,
    containsCredentials,
    requestedLatencyMs,
    canRunAsynchronously:
      /\b(?:background|later|overnight|batch)\b/.test(
        lower,
      ),
    minimizeCost,
    costCeilingRelative:
      minimizeCost
        ? 0.1
        : undefined,
    confidence:
      Math.max(
        0.35,
        1 -
          ambiguity.level *
            0.6,
      ),
  };
}

function toConstraintRecords(
  draft:
    SemanticInterpretationDraft,
): InterpretedConstraint[] {
  const explicit =
    uniqueText(
      draft.explicitConstraints,
    ).map(
      (
        text,
      ): InterpretedConstraint => ({
        kind:
          "explicit",
        text,
        sourceText:
          text,
      }),
    );

  const inferred =
    uniqueText(
      draft.inferredConstraints,
    ).map(
      (
        text,
      ): InterpretedConstraint => ({
        kind:
          "inferred",
        text,
      }),
    );

  return [
    ...explicit,
    ...inferred,
  ];
}

function toQuestions(
  draft:
    SemanticInterpretationDraft,
): InterpretedQuestion[] {
  return (
    draft.unresolvedQuestions ??
    []
  ).map(
    (
      question,
      index,
    ) => ({
      id:
        `q-${String(
          index + 1,
        ).padStart(
          2,
          "0",
        )}`,
      question:
        requireText(
          question.question,
          "unresolved question",
        ),
      blocking:
        question.blocking,
      reason:
        requireText(
          question.reason,
          "unresolved question reason",
        ),
    }),
  );
}

function toTaskSignals(
  draft:
    SemanticInterpretationDraft,
): TaskIntelligenceSignalInput {
  return {
    domains:
      draft.domains,
    estimatedSteps:
      draft.estimatedSteps,
    dependencyCount:
      draft.dependencyCount,
    ambiguityLevel:
      draft.ambiguityLevel,
    noveltyLevel:
      draft.noveltyLevel,
    creativeGeneration:
      draft.creativeGeneration,
    styleSensitivity:
      draft.styleSensitivity,
    factualVerification:
      draft.factualVerification,
    evidenceDependency:
      draft.evidenceDependency,
    consequenceSeverity:
      draft.consequenceSeverity,
    reasoningSteps:
      draft.reasoningSteps,
    crossDomainReasoning:
      draft.crossDomainReasoning,
    containsPersonalData:
      draft.containsPersonalData,
    containsSecrets:
      draft.containsSecrets,
    containsFinancialData:
      draft.containsFinancialData,
    containsHealthData:
      draft.containsHealthData,
    containsCredentials:
      draft.containsCredentials,
    requestedLatencyMs:
      draft.requestedLatencyMs,
    canRunAsynchronously:
      draft.canRunAsynchronously,
    minimizeCost:
      draft.minimizeCost,
    costCeilingRelative:
      draft.costCeilingRelative,
    requiredTools:
      draft.requiredTools,
  };
}

export class InputInterpreter {
  constructor(
    private readonly analyzer:
      InputSemanticAnalyzer =
        defaultSemanticAnalyzer,
  ) {}

  async interpret(
    input:
      RawHumanInput,
  ): Promise<InputInterpretation> {
    const normalizedText =
      normalizeHumanInput(
        input,
      );

    const draft =
      await this.analyzer(
        structuredClone(
          input,
        ),
        normalizedText,
      );

    const semanticConfidence =
      clamp01(
        draft.confidence,
        0.6,
      );

    const intentKind =
      draft.intentKind ??
      "unknown";

    const goal =
      draft.goal
        ?.trim() ||
      normalizedText;

    const desiredOutcome =
      draft.desiredOutcome
        ?.trim() ||
      goal;

    const ambiguity =
      clamp01(
        draft.ambiguityLevel,
        intentKind ===
          "unknown"
          ? 0.6
          : 0.25,
      );

    const questions =
      toQuestions(
        draft,
      );

    const clarificationRequired =
      questions.some(
        (question) =>
          question.blocking,
      );

    const constraintRecords =
      toConstraintRecords(
        draft,
      );

    const taskSignals =
      toTaskSignals(
        draft,
      );

    const taskIntelligence =
      getTaskIntelligenceEngine()
        .classify(
          taskSignals,
        );

    const completeness =
      clamp01(
        (
          (
            goal
              ? 1
              : 0
          ) +
          (
            desiredOutcome
              ? 1
              : 0
          ) +
          (
            intentKind !==
              "unknown"
              ? 1
              : 0
          ) +
          (
            clarificationRequired
              ? 0
              : 1
          )
        ) /
          4,
        0,
      );

    return {
      raw:
        structuredClone(
          input,
        ),
      normalizedText,
      intent: {
        kind:
          intentKind,
        confidence:
          semanticConfidence,
      },
      goal: {
        summary:
          goal,
        confidence:
          semanticConfidence,
      },
      desiredOutcome: {
        summary:
          desiredOutcome,
        confidence:
          semanticConfidence,
      },
      constraints:
        constraintRecords,
      unresolvedQuestions:
        questions,
      clarificationRequired,
      completeness,
      ambiguity,
      taskSignals:
        structuredClone(
          taskSignals,
        ),
      taskIntelligence,
      interpretedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
    };
  }
}

const defaultInputInterpreter =
  new InputInterpreter();

export function getInputInterpreter():
  InputInterpreter {
  return defaultInputInterpreter;
}

export type TaskDomain =
  | "general"
  | "conversation"
  | "planning"
  | "coding"
  | "research"
  | "factual"
  | "creative"
  | "personal-assistance"
  | "system-operations"
  | "data-analysis"
  | "other";

export type TaskComplexity =
  | "trivial"
  | "simple"
  | "moderate"
  | "complex"
  | "very-complex";

export type TaskCreativity =
  | "none"
  | "low"
  | "medium"
  | "high";

export type TaskFactuality =
  | "low"
  | "medium"
  | "high"
  | "critical";

export type TaskReasoningDepth =
  | "shallow"
  | "moderate"
  | "deep"
  | "very-deep";

export type TaskPrivacySensitivity =
  | "public"
  | "internal"
  | "personal"
  | "sensitive"
  | "highly-sensitive";

export type TaskLatencyTolerance =
  | "immediate"
  | "interactive"
  | "flexible"
  | "batch";

export type TaskCostTolerance =
  | "minimal"
  | "low"
  | "standard"
  | "high";

export interface TaskIntelligenceSignalInput {
  domains?: TaskDomain[];

  estimatedSteps?: number;
  dependencyCount?: number;
  ambiguityLevel?: number;
  noveltyLevel?: number;

  creativeGeneration?: number;
  styleSensitivity?: number;

  factualVerification?: number;
  evidenceDependency?: number;
  consequenceSeverity?: number;

  reasoningSteps?: number;
  crossDomainReasoning?: boolean;

  containsPersonalData?: boolean;
  containsSecrets?: boolean;
  containsFinancialData?: boolean;
  containsHealthData?: boolean;
  containsCredentials?: boolean;

  requestedLatencyMs?: number;
  canRunAsynchronously?: boolean;

  minimizeCost?: boolean;
  costCeilingRelative?: number;

  requiredTools?: string[];
}

export interface TaskDimensionAssessment<TValue> {
  value: TValue;
  confidence: number;
  reasons: string[];
}

export interface TaskIntelligenceProfile {
  primaryDomain: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskDomain>;
  secondaryDomains: TaskDomain[];

  complexity: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskComplexity>;
  creativity: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskCreativity>;
  factuality: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskFactuality>;
  reasoningDepth: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskReasoningDepth>;
  privacySensitivity: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskPrivacySensitivity>;
  latencyTolerance: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskLatencyTolerance>;
  costTolerance: TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskCostTolerance>;

  requiredTools: string[];

  derived: {
    multiDomain: boolean;
    requiresExternalEvidence: boolean;
    highRiskIncorrectness: boolean;
    prefersLocalExecution: boolean;
    toolUseRequired: boolean;
  };

  classifiedAt: string;
}

function clamp01(
  value: number | undefined,
  fallback = 0,
): number {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function nonNegativeInteger(
  value: number | undefined,
): number {
  if (
    value === undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.trunc(value),
  );
}

function normalizedTools(
  tools: string[] | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (tools ?? [])
        .map(
          (tool) =>
            tool.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ].sort();
}

function normalizedDomains(
  domains: TaskDomain[] | undefined,
): TaskDomain[] {
  const normalized =
    [
      ...new Set(
        domains ?? [],
      ),
    ];

  return normalized.length > 0
    ? normalized
    : [
        "general",
      ];
}

function assessment<TValue>(
  value: TValue,
  confidence: number,
  reasons: string[],
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TValue> {
  return {
    value,
    confidence:
      clamp01(
        confidence,
        0.5,
      ),
    reasons:
      reasons.length > 0
        ? reasons
        : [
            "No strong signal; default classification applied.",
          ],
  };
}

function classifyComplexity(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskComplexity> {
  const steps =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      input.estimatedSteps,
    );

  const dependencies =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      input.dependencyCount,
    );

  const ambiguity =
    clamp01(
      input.ambiguityLevel,
    );

  const novelty =
    clamp01(
      input.noveltyLevel,
    );

  const score =
    Math.min(
      1,
      (
        Math.min(
          steps,
          12,
        ) /
        12
      ) *
        0.4 +
      (
        Math.min(
          dependencies,
          8,
        ) /
        8
      ) *
        0.2 +
      ambiguity *
        0.2 +
      novelty *
        0.2,
    );

  const reasons = [
    `estimatedSteps=${steps}`,
    `dependencyCount=${dependencies}`,
    `ambiguity=${ambiguity.toFixed(2)}`,
    `novelty=${novelty.toFixed(2)}`,
  ];

  if (
    score < 0.12
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "trivial",
      0.85,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.3
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "simple",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.55
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "moderate",
      0.8,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.78
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "complex",
      0.84,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "very-complex",
    0.88,
    reasons,
  );
}

function classifyCreativity(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskCreativity> {
  const generation =
    clamp01(
      input.creativeGeneration,
    );

  const style =
    clamp01(
      input.styleSensitivity,
    );

  const score =
    generation *
      0.7 +
    style *
      0.3;

  const reasons = [
    `creativeGeneration=${generation.toFixed(2)}`,
    `styleSensitivity=${style.toFixed(2)}`,
  ];

  if (
    score < 0.12
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "none",
      0.9,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.4
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "low",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.72
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "medium",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "high",
    0.9,
    reasons,
  );
}

function classifyFactuality(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskFactuality> {
  const verification =
    clamp01(
      input.factualVerification,
    );

  const evidence =
    clamp01(
      input.evidenceDependency,
    );

  const severity =
    clamp01(
      input.consequenceSeverity,
    );

  const score =
    verification *
      0.4 +
    evidence *
      0.35 +
    severity *
      0.25;

  const reasons = [
    `factualVerification=${verification.toFixed(2)}`,
    `evidenceDependency=${evidence.toFixed(2)}`,
    `consequenceSeverity=${severity.toFixed(2)}`,
  ];

  if (
    score < 0.2
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "low",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.5
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "medium",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.8
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "high",
      0.86,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "critical",
    0.92,
    reasons,
  );
}

function classifyReasoningDepth(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
  domains: readonly TaskDomain[],
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskReasoningDepth> {
  const steps =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      input.reasoningSteps,
    );

  const crossDomain =
    input.crossDomainReasoning ===
      true ||
    domains.length > 1;

  const score =
    Math.min(
      1,
      Math.min(
        steps,
        10,
      ) /
        10 *
        0.75 +
      (
        crossDomain
          ? 0.25
          : 0
      ),
    );

  const reasons = [
    `reasoningSteps=${steps}`,
    `crossDomain=${String(
      crossDomain,
    )}`,
  ];

  if (
    score < 0.18
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "shallow",
      0.84,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.48
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "moderate",
      0.82,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    score < 0.78
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "deep",
      0.86,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "very-deep",
    0.9,
    reasons,
  );
}

function classifyPrivacy(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskPrivacySensitivity> {
  const reasons:
    string[] = [];

  if (
    input.containsCredentials ===
    true
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "containsCredentials=true",
    );

    return assessment(
      "highly-sensitive",
      0.99,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    input.containsHealthData ===
      true ||
    input.containsFinancialData ===
      true ||
    input.containsSecrets ===
      true
  ) {
    if (
      input.containsHealthData
    ) {
      reasons.push(
        "containsHealthData=true",
      );
    }

    if (
      input.containsFinancialData
    ) {
      reasons.push(
        "containsFinancialData=true",
      );
    }

    if (
      input.containsSecrets
    ) {
      reasons.push(
        "containsSecrets=true",
      );
    }

    return assessment(
      "sensitive",
      0.96,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    input.containsPersonalData ===
    true
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "containsPersonalData=true",
    );

    return assessment(
      "personal",
      0.94,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    normalizedDomains(
      input.domains,
    ).some(
      (domain) =>
        domain ===
          "system-operations" ||
        domain ===
          "personal-assistance",
    )
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "task domain commonly operates on non-public user/system context",
    );

    return assessment(
      "internal",
      0.72,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "public",
    0.7,
    [
      "No sensitive-data signal supplied.",
    ],
  );
}

function classifyLatency(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskLatencyTolerance> {
  const requested =
    input.requestedLatencyMs;

  const reasons:
    string[] = [];

  if (
    requested !== undefined &&
    Number.isFinite(
      requested,
    ) &&
    requested >= 0
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      `requestedLatencyMs=${Math.trunc(
        requested,
      )}`,
    );

    if (
      requested <= 1_500
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "immediate",
        0.95,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    if (
      requested <= 10_000
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "interactive",
        0.92,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    if (
      requested <= 120_000
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "flexible",
        0.9,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    return assessment(
      "batch",
      0.9,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  if (
    input.canRunAsynchronously ===
    true
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "batch",
      0.8,
      [
        "canRunAsynchronously=true",
      ],
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "interactive",
    0.65,
    [
      "No explicit latency deadline; defaulting to interactive.",
    ],
  );
}

function classifyCost(
  input: TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
): TaskDimensionAssessment<TaskCostTolerance> {
  const ceiling =
    input.costCeilingRelative;

  if (
    input.minimizeCost ===
    true
  ) {
    return assessment(
      "minimal",
      0.95,
      [
        "minimizeCost=true",
      ],
    );
  }

  if (
    ceiling !== undefined &&
    Number.isFinite(
      ceiling,
    )
  ) {
    const normalized =
      clamp01(
        ceiling,
      );

    const reasons = [
      `costCeilingRelative=${normalized.toFixed(2)}`,
    ];

    if (
      normalized < 0.2
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "minimal",
        0.9,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    if (
      normalized < 0.45
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "low",
        0.88,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    if (
      normalized < 0.8
    ) {
      return assessment(
        "standard",
        0.86,
        reasons,
      );
    }

    return assessment(
      "high",
      0.88,
      reasons,
    );
  }

  return assessment(
    "standard",
    0.65,
    [
      "No explicit cost constraint; defaulting to standard.",
    ],
  );
}

export class TaskIntelligenceEngine {
  classify(
    input:
      TaskIntelligenceSignalInput,
  ): TaskIntelligenceProfile {
    const domains =
      normalizedDomains(
        input.domains,
      );

    const privacy =
      classifyPrivacy(
        input,
      );

    const factuality =
      classifyFactuality(
        input,
      );

    const requiredTools =
      normalizedTools(
        input.requiredTools,
      );

    return {
      primaryDomain:
        assessment(
          domains[0],
          input.domains
            ?.length
            ? 0.92
            : 0.55,
          input.domains
            ?.length
            ? [
                "Primary domain supplied by normalized task signals.",
              ]
            : [
                "No domain supplied; defaulting to general.",
              ],
        ),
      secondaryDomains:
        domains.slice(1),
      complexity:
        classifyComplexity(
          input,
        ),
      creativity:
        classifyCreativity(
          input,
        ),
      factuality,
      reasoningDepth:
        classifyReasoningDepth(
          input,
          domains,
        ),
      privacySensitivity:
        privacy,
      latencyTolerance:
        classifyLatency(
          input,
        ),
      costTolerance:
        classifyCost(
          input,
        ),
      requiredTools,
      derived: {
        multiDomain:
          domains.length > 1,
        requiresExternalEvidence:
          clamp01(
            input.evidenceDependency,
          ) >= 0.5 ||
          clamp01(
            input.factualVerification,
          ) >= 0.75,
        highRiskIncorrectness:
          factuality.value ===
            "critical" ||
          clamp01(
            input.consequenceSeverity,
          ) >= 0.8,
        prefersLocalExecution:
          privacy.value ===
            "sensitive" ||
          privacy.value ===
            "highly-sensitive",
        toolUseRequired:
          requiredTools.length >
          0,
      },
      classifiedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
    };
  }
}

const defaultTaskIntelligenceEngine =
  new TaskIntelligenceEngine();

export function getTaskIntelligenceEngine():
  TaskIntelligenceEngine {
  return defaultTaskIntelligenceEngine;
}

import {
  buildChernobogWorldModelContext,
} from "../../../lib/chernobog/pipeline/worldModelContext";
import {
  buildChernobogWorldStateContext,
} from "../../../lib/chernobog/pipeline/worldStateContext";
import type {
  InputInterpretation,
} from "./inputInterpreter";

export type ContextCategory =
  | "project-state"
  | "memory"
  | "file"
  | "recent-decision"
  | "tool-state"
  | "policy"
  | "world-state"
  | "world-model"
  | "other";

export type ContextFreshness =
  | "live"
  | "fresh"
  | "aging"
  | "stale"
  | "historical"
  | "unknown";

export interface ContextProvenance {
  sourceId: string;
  sourceType: string;

  reference?: string;
  eventId?: string;
  filePath?: string;
  recordKey?: string;

  retrievedAt: string;
}

export interface ContextItem {
  id: string;
  category: ContextCategory;

  title: string;
  content: string;

  provenance: ContextProvenance;

  observedAt?: string;
  freshness: ContextFreshness;
  confidence: number;

  sensitive: boolean;

  relevanceTags: string[];
  metadata?: Record<
    string,
    unknown
  >;
}

export interface ContextSourceRequest {
  interpretation: InputInterpretation;

  projectId?: string;
  requestedCategories?: ContextCategory[];
  requestedSourceIds?: string[];

  maxItems?: number;
  maxChars?: number;
}

export interface ContextSource {
  id: string;
  categories: ContextCategory[];

  retrieve(
    request: ContextSourceRequest,
  ):
    Promise<ContextItem[]>
    | ContextItem[];
}

export interface ContextSourceDiagnostic {
  sourceId: string;

  status:
    | "queried"
    | "skipped"
    | "failed";

  itemCount: number;
  reason: string;
}

export interface SelectedContextItem
  extends ContextItem {
  relevance: number;
  relevanceReasons: string[];

  originalChars: number;
  includedChars: number;
  truncated: boolean;
}

export interface ContextPacket {
  generatedAt: string;

  items: SelectedContextItem[];

  systemText: string;

  diagnostics:
    ContextSourceDiagnostic[];

  metrics: {
    retrievedItems: number;
    selectedItems: number;
    excludedItems: number;

    originalChars: number;
    includedChars: number;

    truncatedItems: number;
    totalSources: number;
    queriedSources: number;
    failedSources: number;
  };
}

export interface ContextEngineOptions {
  defaultMaxItems?: number;
  defaultMaxChars?: number;
  maxItemChars?: number;
  minimumRelevance?: number;
}

function requireText(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function clamp01(
  value: number,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function normalizeStringList(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (values ?? [])
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value
              .trim()
              .toLowerCase(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function normalizedItem(
  source:
    ContextSource,
  item:
    ContextItem,
): ContextItem {
  const category =
    item.category;

  if (
    !source.categories.includes(
      category,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Context source ${source.id} returned unsupported category ${category}.`,
    );
  }

  return {
    ...structuredClone(
      item,
    ),
    id:
      requireText(
        item.id,
        "context item id",
      ),
    category,
    title:
      requireText(
        item.title,
        "context item title",
      ),
    content:
      requireText(
        item.content,
        "context item content",
      ),
    provenance: {
      ...structuredClone(
        item.provenance,
      ),
      sourceId:
        requireText(
          item.provenance
            .sourceId,
          "context provenance sourceId",
        ),
      sourceType:
        requireText(
          item.provenance
            .sourceType,
          "context provenance sourceType",
        ),
      retrievedAt:
        new Date(
          item.provenance
            .retrievedAt,
        ).toISOString(),
    },
    confidence:
      clamp01(
        item.confidence,
      ),
    sensitive:
      item.sensitive ===
      true,
    relevanceTags:
      normalizeStringList(
        item.relevanceTags,
      ),
  };
}

const CONTEXT_RELEVANCE_STOP_WORDS =
  new Set([
    // OH4K_RELEVANCE_STOP_WORDS_V1
    "a",
    "an",
    "and",
    "are",
    "as",
    "at",
    "be",
    "been",
    "being",
    "but",
    "by",
    "can",
    "could",
    "did",
    "do",
    "does",
    "for",
    "from",
    "had",
    "has",
    "have",
    "he",
    "her",
    "here",
    "him",
    "his",
    "how",
    "i",
    "if",
    "in",
    "into",
    "is",

    "its",
    "me",
    "my",
    "of",

    "which",
    "who",
    "why",
    "will",
    "with",
    "would",
    "you",
    "your",
  ]);

function tokenize(
  value: string,
): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    value
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /[^a-z0-9_.-]+/g,
        " ",
      )
      .split(
        /\s+/,
      )
      .map(
        (token) =>
          token.trim(),
      )
      .filter(
        (token) =>
          token.length >=
            3 &&
          !CONTEXT_RELEVANCE_STOP_WORDS.has(
            token,
          ),
      ),
  );
}

function lexicalOverlap(
  item:
    ContextItem,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): number {
  const taskTokens =
    tokenize(
      [
        interpretation
          .normalizedText,
        interpretation
          .goal.summary,
        interpretation
          .desiredOutcome.summary,
        interpretation
          .constraints
          .map(
            (constraint) =>
              constraint.text,
          )
          .join(" "),
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .primaryDomain
          .value,
        ...interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .secondaryDomains,
        ...(
          interpretation
            .taskSignals
            .requiredTools ??
          []
        ),
      ].join(" "),
    );

  const itemTokens =
    tokenize(
      [
        item.title,
        item.content.slice(
          0,
          2_000,
        ),
        ...item
          .relevanceTags,
      ].join(" "),
    );

  if (
    taskTokens.size ===
      0 ||
    itemTokens.size ===
      0
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  let overlap =
    0;

  for (
    const token
    of taskTokens
  ) {
    if (
      itemTokens.has(
        token,
      )
    ) {
      overlap += 1;
    }
  }

  return Math.min(
    1,
    overlap /
      Math.max(
        3,
        Math.min(
          taskTokens.size,
          12,
        ),
      ),
  );
}

function freshnessScore(
  freshness:
    ContextFreshness,
): number {
  switch (
    freshness
  ) {
    case "live":
      return 1;
    case "fresh":
      return 0.9;
    case "aging":
      return 0.65;
    case "historical":
      return 0.45;
    case "stale":
      return 0.25;
    case "unknown":
    default:
      return 0.4;
  }
}

function categoryNeedScore(
  category:
    ContextCategory,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const reasons:
    string[] =
      [];

  const intent =
    interpretation
      .intent.kind;

  const domain =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .primaryDomain
      .value;

  const secondary =
    new Set(
      interpretation
        .taskIntelligence
        .secondaryDomains,
    );

  const tools =
    new Set(
      interpretation
        .taskSignals
        .requiredTools ??
        [],
    );

  let score =
    0.2;

  switch (
    category
  ) {
    case "memory":
      if (
        intent ===
          "recall" ||
        intent ===
          "continue" ||
        intent ===
          "remember"
      ) {
        score =
          1;
        reasons.push(
          "intent requires continuity or memory",
        );
      } else if (
        domain ===
          "personal-assistance"
      ) {
        score =
          0.75;
        reasons.push(
          "personal-assistance commonly needs user context",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "project-state":
      if (
        [
          "planning",
          "coding",
          "system-operations",
          "creative",
        ].includes(
          domain,
        )
      ) {
        score =
          0.85;
        reasons.push(
          "primary domain depends on active project state",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "file":
      if (
        tools.has(
          "files",
        ) ||
        domain ===
          "coding" ||
        domain ===
          "research"
      ) {
        score =
          0.95;
        reasons.push(
          "task signals require file-backed evidence",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "recent-decision":
      if (
        intent ===
          "continue" ||
        domain ===
          "planning" ||
        domain ===
          "coding"
      ) {
        score =
          0.8;
        reasons.push(
          "task may depend on recent decisions or continuation state",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "tool-state":
      if (
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .derived
          .toolUseRequired ||
        domain ===
          "system-operations"
      ) {
        score =
          0.95;
        reasons.push(
          "task requires tools or operational state",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "policy":
      if (
        intent ===
          "execute" ||
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .derived
          .highRiskIncorrectness ||
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .privacySensitivity
          .value ===
          "sensitive" ||
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .privacySensitivity
          .value ===
          "highly-sensitive"
      ) {
        score =
          0.95;
        reasons.push(
          "execution/risk/privacy requires applicable policy context",
        );
      } else {
        score =
          0.55;
        reasons.push(
          "policy context may constrain cognitive work",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "world-state":
      if (
        domain ===
          "system-operations" ||
        tools.has(
          "runtime",
        )
      ) {
        score =
          0.9;
        reasons.push(
          "task requires current runtime/system state",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "world-model":
      if (
        domain ===
          "planning" ||
        domain ===
          "system-operations" ||
        secondary.has(
          "planning",
        )
      ) {
        score =
          0.7;
        reasons.push(
          "dependency/impact context may improve reasoning",
        );
      }
      break;

    case "other":
    default:
      score =
        0.25;
      break;
  }

  return {
    score,
    reasons,
  };
}

function scoreItem(
  item:
    ContextItem,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
  explicitlyRequested:
    boolean,
): {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const category =
    categoryNeedScore(
      item.category,
      interpretation,
    );

  const lexical =
    lexicalOverlap(
      item,
      interpretation,
    );

  const freshness =
    freshnessScore(
      item.freshness,
    );

  const explicitBoost =
    explicitlyRequested
      ? 0.2
      : 0;

  let score =
    clamp01(
      category.score *
        0.45 +
      lexical *
        0.25 +
      freshness *
        0.15 +
      item.confidence *
        0.15 +
      explicitBoost,
    );

  const contentBoundCategories =
    new Set<ContextCategory>([
      "project-state",
      "memory",
      "file",
      "recent-decision",
      "other",
    ]);

  /*
   * Category relevance alone is not enough for content-heavy sources.
   *
   * Example:
   * a coding task may need files, but that must not make every available
   * file relevant. Without topical overlap, a file/memory/project item is
   * capped below the default selection threshold unless the caller
   * explicitly requested that source/category.
   */
  if (
    !explicitlyRequested &&
    lexical === 0 &&
    contentBoundCategories.has(
      item.category,
    )
  ) {
    score =
      Math.min(
        score,
        0.3,
      );
  }

  const reasons = [
    ...category.reasons,
    `categoryNeed=${category.score.toFixed(
      2,
    )}`,
    `lexicalOverlap=${lexical.toFixed(
      2,
    )}`,
    `freshness=${item.freshness}`,
    `confidence=${item.confidence.toFixed(
      2,
    )}`,
  ];

  if (
    explicitlyRequested
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "source/category explicitly requested",
    );
  }

  if (
    !explicitlyRequested &&
    lexical === 0 &&
    contentBoundCategories.has(
      item.category,
    )
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "content-heavy context had zero topical overlap and was capped below automatic selection threshold",
    );
  }

  return {
    score,
    reasons,
  };
}

function clippedContent(
  content: string,
  limit: number,
): {
  content: string;
  truncated: boolean;
} {
  if (
    content.length <=
    limit
  ) {
    return {
      content,
      truncated:
        false,
    };
  }

  const marker =
    "\n\n[context item truncated]\n\n";

  const available =
    Math.max(
      0,
      limit -
        marker.length,
    );

  const head =
    Math.ceil(
      available *
      0.75,
    );

  const tail =
    Math.max(
      0,
      available -
        head,
    );

  return {
    content: [
      content.slice(
        0,
        head,
      ),
      marker,
      tail > 0
        ? content.slice(
            -tail,
          )
        : "",
    ].join(""),
    truncated:
      true,
  };
}

function formatSelectedItem(
  item:
    SelectedContextItem,
): string {
  return [
    `## CONTEXT ITEM: ${item.title}`,
    `id=${item.id}`,
    `category=${item.category}`,
    `source=${item.provenance.sourceId}`,
    `freshness=${item.freshness}`,
    `confidence=${item.confidence.toFixed(
      2,
    )}`,
    `relevance=${item.relevance.toFixed(
      2,
    )}`,
    `sensitive=${String(
      item.sensitive,
    )}`,
    `reference=${item.provenance.reference ?? "none"}`,
    "",
    item.content,
  ].join("\n");
}

/*
 * OH4K_EXPLICIT_BUDGET_AUTHORITY_V1
 *
 * Caller-supplied context budgets are authoritative.
 * The engine may enforce a positive integer floor, but it must not
 * silently increase an explicit maxChars or maxItemChars value.
 */
export class ContextEngine {
  private readonly sources =
    new Map<
      string,
      ContextSource
    >();

  private readonly defaultMaxItems:
    number;

  private readonly defaultMaxChars:
    number;

  private readonly maxItemChars:
    number;

  private readonly minimumRelevance:
    number;

  constructor(
    options:
      ContextEngineOptions = {},
  ) {
    this.defaultMaxItems =
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.trunc(
          options
            .defaultMaxItems ??
          24,
        ),
      );

    this.defaultMaxChars =
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.trunc(
          options
            .defaultMaxChars ??
          24_000,
        ),
      );

    this.maxItemChars =
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.trunc(
          options
            .maxItemChars ??
          8_000,
        ),
      );

    this.minimumRelevance =
      clamp01(
        options
          .minimumRelevance ??
        0.35,
      );
  }

  registerSource(
    source:
      ContextSource,
  ): void {
    const id =
      requireText(
        source.id,
        "context source id",
      );

    if (
      source.categories
        .length ===
      0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Context source ${id} must declare at least one category.`,
      );
    }

    if (
      this.sources.has(
        id,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Context source is already registered: ${id}`,
      );
    }

    this.sources.set(
      id,
      source,
    );
  }

  upsertSource(
    source:
      ContextSource,
  ): void {
    const id =
      requireText(
        source.id,
        "context source id",
      );

    if (
      source.categories
        .length ===
      0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Context source ${id} must declare at least one category.`,
      );
    }

    this.sources.set(
      id,
      source,
    );
  }

  removeSource(
    id: string,
  ): boolean {
    return this.sources.delete(
      id.trim(),
    );
  }

  listSources(): Array<{
    id: string;
    categories: ContextCategory[];
  }> {
    return [
      ...this.sources.values(),
    ]
      .map(
        (source) => ({
          id:
            source.id,
          categories: [
            ...source
              .categories,
          ],
        }),
      )
      .sort(
        (left, right) =>
          left.id.localeCompare(
            right.id,
          ),
      );
  }

  async assemble(
    request:
      ContextSourceRequest,
  ): Promise<ContextPacket> {
    const diagnostics:
      ContextSourceDiagnostic[] =
        [];

    const retrieved:
      ContextItem[] =
        [];

    const requestedCategories =
      new Set(
        request.requestedCategories ??
        [],
      );

    const requestedSourceIds =
      new Set(
        request.requestedSourceIds ??
        [],
      );

    const candidateSources =
      [
        ...this.sources.values(),
      ];

    for (
      const source
      of candidateSources
    ) {
      if (
        requestedSourceIds.size >
          0 &&
        !requestedSourceIds.has(
          source.id,
        )
      ) {
        diagnostics.push({
          sourceId:
            source.id,
          status:
            "skipped",
          itemCount:
            0,
          reason:
            "not explicitly requested",
        });
        continue;
      }

      if (
        requestedCategories.size >
          0 &&
        !source.categories.some(
          (category) =>
            requestedCategories.has(
              category,
            ),
        )
      ) {
        diagnostics.push({
          sourceId:
            source.id,
          status:
            "skipped",
          itemCount:
            0,
          reason:
            "source categories not requested",
        });
        continue;
      }

      try {
        const items =
          await source.retrieve(
            request,
          );

        const normalized =
          items.map(
            (item) =>
              normalizedItem(
                source,
                item,
              ),
          );

        retrieved.push(
          ...normalized,
        );

        diagnostics.push({
          sourceId:
            source.id,
          status:
            "queried",
          itemCount:
            normalized.length,
          reason:
            "retrieval completed",
        });
      } catch (error) {
        diagnostics.push({
          sourceId:
            source.id,
          status:
            "failed",
          itemCount:
            0,
          reason:
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : String(error),
        });
      }
    }

    const deduplicated =
      new Map<
        string,
        ContextItem
      >();

    for (
      const item
      of retrieved
    ) {
      const key =
        [
          item.category,
          item.provenance
            .sourceId,
          item.provenance
            .reference ??
            "",
          item.id,
        ].join("|");

      const existing =
        deduplicated.get(
          key,
        );

      if (
        !existing ||
        item.confidence >
          existing.confidence
      ) {
        deduplicated.set(
          key,
          item,
        );
      }
    }

    const contentBoundCategories =
      new Set<ContextCategory>([
        "project-state",
        "memory",
        "file",
        "recent-decision",
        "other",
      ]);

    const scored =
      [
        ...deduplicated.values(),
      ]
        .map(
          (item) => {
            const explicit =
              requestedCategories.has(
                item.category,
              ) ||
              requestedSourceIds.has(
                item.provenance
                  .sourceId,
              );

            const topicalOverlap =
              lexicalOverlap(
                item,
                request
                  .interpretation,
              );

            const score =
              scoreItem(
                item,
                request
                  .interpretation,
                explicit,
              );

            return {
              item,
              explicit,
              topicalOverlap,
              ...score,
            };
          },
        )
        .filter(
          (entry) => {
            if (
              !entry.explicit &&
              contentBoundCategories.has(
                entry.item.category,
              ) &&
              entry.topicalOverlap === 0
            ) {
              return false;
            }

            return (
              entry.score >=
              this.minimumRelevance
            );
          },
        )
        .sort(
          (left, right) => {
            if (
              left.score !==
              right.score
            ) {
              return (
                right.score -
                left.score
              );
            }

            if (
              left.item
                .confidence !==
              right.item
                .confidence
            ) {
              return (
                right.item
                  .confidence -
                left.item
                  .confidence
              );
            }

            return left.item.id
              .localeCompare(
                right.item.id,
              );
          },
        );

    const maxItems =
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.trunc(
          request.maxItems ??
          this.defaultMaxItems,
        ),
      );

    const maxChars =
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.trunc(
          request.maxChars ??
          this.defaultMaxChars,
        ),
      );

    const selected:
      SelectedContextItem[] =
        [];

    let remainingChars =
      maxChars;

    for (
      const entry
      of scored
    ) {
      if (
        selected.length >=
          maxItems ||
        remainingChars <=
          0
      ) {
        break;
      }

      const itemLimit =
        Math.min(
          this.maxItemChars,
          remainingChars,
        );

      const clipped =
        clippedContent(
          entry.item.content,
          itemLimit,
        );

      selected.push({
        ...structuredClone(
          entry.item,
        ),
        content:
          clipped.content,
        relevance:
          entry.score,
        relevanceReasons:
          entry.reasons,
        originalChars:
          entry.item
            .content.length,
        includedChars:
          clipped.content
            .length,
        truncated:
          clipped.truncated,
      });

      remainingChars -=
        clipped.content
          .length;
    }

    const systemText =
      [
        "CHERNOBOG TASK CONTEXT PACKET",
        "Only the selected context below should be treated as retrieved task context.",
        "Preserve provenance, freshness, confidence, and sensitivity distinctions.",
        "Missing context must not be invented.",
        "",
        ...selected.map(
          formatSelectedItem,
        ),
      ].join("\n\n");

    const originalChars =
      retrieved.reduce(
        (
          total,
          item,
        ) =>
          total +
          item.content.length,
        0,
      );

    const includedChars =
      selected.reduce(
        (
          total,
          item,
        ) =>
          total +
          item.includedChars,
        0,
      );

    return {
      generatedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      items:
        selected,
      systemText,
      diagnostics,
      metrics: {
        retrievedItems:
          retrieved.length,
        selectedItems:
          selected.length,
        excludedItems:
          Math.max(
            0,
            deduplicated
              .size -
            selected.length,
          ),
        originalChars,
        includedChars,
        truncatedItems:
          selected.filter(
            (item) =>
              item.truncated,
          ).length,
        totalSources:
          candidateSources
            .length,
        queriedSources:
          diagnostics.filter(
            (diagnostic) =>
              diagnostic.status ===
              "queried",
          ).length,
        failedSources:
          diagnostics.filter(
            (diagnostic) =>
              diagnostic.status ===
              "failed",
          ).length,
      },
    };
  }
}

function runtimeWorldStateSource():
  ContextSource {
  return {
    id:
      "runtime-world-state",
    categories: [
      "world-state",
      "tool-state",
      "project-state",
    ],
    async retrieve(
      request,
    ) {
      const context =
        await buildChernobogWorldStateContext({
          projectId:
            request.projectId,
        });

      if (
        context.status ===
          "empty"
      ) {
        return [];
      }

      return [
        {
          id:
            "runtime-world-state",
          category:
            "world-state",
          title:
            "Canonical Runtime World State",
          content:
            context.systemText,
          provenance: {
            sourceId:
              "runtime-world-state",
            sourceType:
              "chernobog-world-state",
            reference:
              "11G",
            retrievedAt:
              new Date()
                .toISOString(),
          },
          freshness:
            context.status ===
            "available"
              ? "live"
              : "unknown",
          confidence:
            context.status ===
            "available"
              ? 0.95
              : 0.2,
          sensitive:
            true,
          relevanceTags: [
            "runtime",
            "system",
            "project",
            "state",
          ],
          metadata: {
            status:
              context.status,
            count:
              context.count,
            keys:
              context.keys,
          },
        },
      ];
    },
  };
}

function runtimeWorldModelSource():
  ContextSource {
  return {
    id:
      "runtime-world-model",
    categories: [
      "world-model",
    ],
    async retrieve() {
      const context =
        await buildChernobogWorldModelContext();

      if (
        context.status ===
          "empty"
      ) {
        return [];
      }

      return [
        {
          id:
            "runtime-world-model",
          category:
            "world-model",
          title:
            "Canonical World Model",
          content:
            context.systemText,
          provenance: {
            sourceId:
              "runtime-world-model",
            sourceType:
              "chernobog-world-model",
            reference:
              "11J",
            retrievedAt:
              new Date()
                .toISOString(),
          },
          freshness:
            context.status ===
            "available"
              ? "fresh"
              : "unknown",
          confidence:
            context.status ===
            "available"
              ? 0.9
              : 0.2,
          sensitive:
            true,
          relevanceTags: [
            "dependencies",
            "relationships",
            "impact",
            "predictions",
            "planning",
          ],
          metadata: {
            status:
              context.status,
            entityCount:
              context.entityCount,
            relationshipCount:
              context.relationshipCount,
            predictionCount:
              context.predictionCount,
            causalHypothesisCount:
              context
                .causalHypothesisCount,
          },
        },
      ];
    },
  };
}

const defaultContextEngine =
  new ContextEngine();

defaultContextEngine.registerSource(
  runtimeWorldStateSource(),
);

defaultContextEngine.registerSource(
  runtimeWorldModelSource(),
);

export function getContextEngine():
  ContextEngine {
  return defaultContextEngine;
}
