import type {
  CognitivePlan,
  CognitivePlanStep,
} from "./cognitivePlanningEngine";

export const PRIORITY_ATTENTION_ENGINE_VERSION =
  "oh4r.v1";

export type UserAttentionAvailability =
  | "available"
  | "limited"
  | "unavailable";

export type PlanAttentionReasonCode =
  | "ready"
  | "dependency-wait"
  | "blocked"
  | "completed"
  | "importance"
  | "urgency"
  | "user-pinned"
  | "verification"
  | "evidence-needed"
  | "tool-needed"
  | "continuity"
  | "user-attention-fit";

export interface PlanPriorityHint {
  importance?: number;
  urgency?: number;

  userPinned?: boolean;

  requiresUserAttention?: boolean;

  reason?: string;
}

export interface PlanAttentionProgress {
  completedStepIds?: string[];
  blockedStepIds?: string[];
}

export interface PlanAttentionPolicy {
  baseReadyScore: number;

  importanceWeight: number;
  urgencyWeight: number;

  verificationWeight: number;
  evidenceWeight: number;
  toolWeight: number;

  userPinnedWeight: number;
  continuityWeight: number;

  limitedAttentionPenalty: number;
  unavailableAttentionPenalty: number;

  minimumFocusScore: number;
  switchMargin: number;
}

export interface PlanAttentionReason {
  code:
    PlanAttentionReasonCode;

  weight: number;
  detail: string;
}

export interface PlanAttentionCandidate {
  rank: number;

  stepId: string;
  goalId: string;

  eligible: boolean;

  readiness:
    | "ready"
    | "waiting"
    | "blocked"
    | "completed";

  score: number;

  importance: number;
  urgency: number;

  reasons:
    PlanAttentionReason[];

  unmetDependencies:
    string[];

  blockers:
    string[];

  step:
    CognitivePlanStep;
}

export type PlanFocusReason =
  | "no-candidates"
  | "no-eligible-step"
  | "initial-focus"
  | "retained-focus"
  | "switched-focus";

export interface PlanAttentionDecision {
  version: string;
  generatedAt: string;

  reason:
    PlanFocusReason;

  changed: boolean;

  previousStepId?:
    string;

  selected?:
    PlanAttentionCandidate;

  candidates:
    PlanAttentionCandidate[];

  planStatus:
    CognitivePlan["status"];

  userAttention:
    UserAttentionAvailability;

  policy:
    PlanAttentionPolicy;

  invariants: {
    dependenciesCanBeBypassed: false;
    blockedStepsCanBeSelected: false;
    schedulingPerformed: false;
    executionPerformed: false;
    planMutated: false;
  };

  warnings:
    string[];
}

export const DEFAULT_PLAN_ATTENTION_POLICY:
  PlanAttentionPolicy = {
    baseReadyScore:
      30,

    importanceWeight:
      25,
    urgencyWeight:
      25,

    verificationWeight:
      8,
    evidenceWeight:
      6,
    toolWeight:
      4,

    userPinnedWeight:
      20,
    continuityWeight:
      10,

    limitedAttentionPenalty:
      15,
    unavailableAttentionPenalty:
      40,

    minimumFocusScore:
      25,
    switchMargin:
      10,
  };

function clampUnit(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  fallback:
    number,
): number {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Priority values must be finite numbers.",
    );
  }

  if (
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Priority values must be between 0 and 1.",
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function clampScore(
  value: number,
): number {
  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      100,
      Math.round(
        value,
      ),
    ),
  );
}

function validatePolicy(
  policy:
    PlanAttentionPolicy,
): void {
  const nonNegativeFields: Array<
    keyof PlanAttentionPolicy
  > = [
    "baseReadyScore",
    "importanceWeight",
    "urgencyWeight",
    "verificationWeight",
    "evidenceWeight",
    "toolWeight",
    "userPinnedWeight",
    "continuityWeight",
    "limitedAttentionPenalty",
    "unavailableAttentionPenalty",
    "minimumFocusScore",
    "switchMargin",
  ];

  for (
    const field
    of nonNegativeFields
  ) {
    const value =
      policy[field];

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(
        value,
      ) ||
      value <
        0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Plan attention policy ${field} must be a non-negative finite number.`,
      );
    }
  }

  if (
    policy.minimumFocusScore >
      100 ||
    policy.switchMargin >
      100
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Plan attention policy minimumFocusScore and switchMargin must not exceed 100.",
    );
  }
}

function setOf(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    values ?? [],
  );
}

function reason(
  reasons:
    PlanAttentionReason[],
  code:
    PlanAttentionReasonCode,
  weight:
    number,
  detail:
    string,
): number {
  reasons.push({
    code,
    weight,
    detail,
  });

  return weight;
}

function assessCandidate(
  input: {
    step:
      CognitivePlanStep;

    completed:
      Set<string>;

    blocked:
      Set<string>;

    hint:
      PlanPriorityHint;

    previousStepId?:
      string;

    userAttention:
      UserAttentionAvailability;

    policy:
      PlanAttentionPolicy;

    planBlocked:
      boolean;
  },
): Omit<
  PlanAttentionCandidate,
  "rank"
> {
  const step =
    input.step;

  const reasons:
    PlanAttentionReason[] =
      [];

  const importance =
    clampUnit(
      input.hint
        .importance,
      0.5,
    );

  const urgency =
    clampUnit(
      input.hint
        .urgency,
      0.5,
    );

  const completed =
    input.completed.has(
      step.id,
    );

  const explicitlyBlocked =
    input.blocked.has(
      step.id,
    );

  const unmetDependencies =
    step.dependsOnStepIds
      .filter(
        (dependency) =>
          !input.completed
            .has(
              dependency,
            ),
      );

  const blockers = [
    ...step.blockers,
    ...(
      explicitlyBlocked
        ? [
            "Step is marked blocked by live progress.",
          ]
        : []
    ),
    ...(
      input.planBlocked
        ? [
            "Parent cognitive plan is blocked.",
          ]
        : []
    ),
  ];

  let readiness:
    PlanAttentionCandidate[
      "readiness"
    ];

  if (
    completed
  ) {
    readiness =
      "completed";

    reason(
      reasons,
      "completed",
      0,
      "Step is already completed and is not eligible for focus.",
    );
  } else if (
    blockers.length >
      0
  ) {
    readiness =
      "blocked";

    reason(
      reasons,
      "blocked",
      0,
      blockers.join(
        " ",
      ),
    );
  } else if (
    unmetDependencies.length >
      0
  ) {
    readiness =
      "waiting";

    reason(
      reasons,
      "dependency-wait",
      0,
      `Waiting on dependencies: ${unmetDependencies.join(", ")}.`,
    );
  } else {
    readiness =
      "ready";

    reason(
      reasons,
      "ready",
      input.policy
        .baseReadyScore,
      "All dependencies are satisfied and the step is eligible for cognitive focus.",
    );
  }

  const eligible =
    readiness ===
    "ready";

  let rawScore =
    eligible
      ? input.policy
          .baseReadyScore
      : 0;

  if (
    eligible
  ) {
    rawScore +=
      reason(
        reasons,
        "importance",
        importance *
          input.policy
            .importanceWeight,
        `Importance=${importance.toFixed(2)}.`,
      );

    rawScore +=
      reason(
        reasons,
        "urgency",
        urgency *
          input.policy
            .urgencyWeight,
        `Urgency=${urgency.toFixed(2)}.`,
      );

    if (
      input.hint
        .userPinned
    ) {
      rawScore +=
        reason(
          reasons,
          "user-pinned",
          input.policy
            .userPinnedWeight,
          input.hint
            .reason
            ?.trim() ||
          "User explicitly pinned this step for attention.",
        );
    }

    if (
      step.kind ===
        "verification" ||
      step.verification
        .required
    ) {
      rawScore +=
        reason(
          reasons,
          "verification",
          input.policy
            .verificationWeight,
          "Step contains a required verification checkpoint.",
        );
    }

    if (
      step.evidenceNeeds
        .some(
          (need) =>
            need.required,
        )
    ) {
      rawScore +=
        reason(
          reasons,
          "evidence-needed",
          input.policy
            .evidenceWeight,
          "Step requires evidence before it can be considered complete.",
        );
    }

    if (
      step.requiredTools
        .length >
      0
    ) {
      rawScore +=
        reason(
          reasons,
          "tool-needed",
          input.policy
            .toolWeight,
          `Step references required tools: ${step.requiredTools.join(", ")}.`,
        );
    }

    if (
      input.previousStepId ===
      step.id
    ) {
      rawScore +=
        reason(
          reasons,
          "continuity",
          input.policy
            .continuityWeight,
          "Current focus receives a continuity boost to avoid unnecessary attention thrashing.",
        );
    }

    if (
      input.hint
        .requiresUserAttention
    ) {
      if (
        input.userAttention ===
        "limited"
      ) {
        rawScore -=
          reason(
            reasons,
            "user-attention-fit",
            input.policy
              .limitedAttentionPenalty,
            "Step requires user attention while user availability is limited.",
          );
      } else if (
        input.userAttention ===
        "unavailable"
      ) {
        rawScore -=
          reason(
            reasons,
            "user-attention-fit",
            input.policy
              .unavailableAttentionPenalty,
            "Step requires user attention while user is unavailable.",
          );
      } else {
        reason(
          reasons,
          "user-attention-fit",
          0,
          "Required user attention is currently available.",
        );
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    stepId:
      step.id,
    goalId:
      step.goalId,
    eligible,
    readiness,
    score:
      eligible
        ? clampScore(
            rawScore,
          )
        : 0,
    importance,
    urgency,
    reasons,
    unmetDependencies,
    blockers,
    step:
      structuredClone(
        step,
      ),
  };
}

export class PriorityAttentionEngine {
  prioritize(
    input: {
      plan:
        CognitivePlan;

      progress?:
        PlanAttentionProgress;

      priorityHints?:
        Record<
          string,
          PlanPriorityHint
        >;

      userAttention?:
        UserAttentionAvailability;

      previousStepId?:
        string;

      policy?:
        Partial<
          PlanAttentionPolicy
        >;
    },
  ): PlanAttentionDecision {
    const policy: PlanAttentionPolicy = {
      ...DEFAULT_PLAN_ATTENTION_POLICY,
      ...input.policy,
    };

    validatePolicy(
      policy,
    );

    const completed =
      setOf(
        input.progress
          ?.completedStepIds,
      );

    const blocked =
      setOf(
        input.progress
          ?.blockedStepIds,
      );

    const userAttention =
      input.userAttention ??
      "available";

    const planBlocked =
      input.plan
        .status ===
        "blocked" ||
      !input.plan
        .planningReady;

    const candidates =
      input.plan
        .steps
        .map(
          (step) =>
            assessCandidate({
              step,
              completed,
              blocked,
              hint:
                input
                  .priorityHints
                  ?.[step.id] ??
                {},
              previousStepId:
                input
                  .previousStepId,
              userAttention,
              policy,
              planBlocked,
            }),
        )
        .sort(
          (
            left,
            right,
          ) => {
            if (
              left.eligible !==
              right.eligible
            ) {
              return left.eligible
                ? -1
                : 1;
            }

            if (
              left.score !==
              right.score
            ) {
              return (
                right.score -
                left.score
              );
            }

            if (
              left.step.stage !==
              right.step.stage
            ) {
              return (
                left.step.stage -
                right.step.stage
              );
            }

            if (
              left.step.sequence !==
              right.step.sequence
            ) {
              return (
                left.step.sequence -
                right.step.sequence
              );
            }

            return left.stepId
              .localeCompare(
                right.stepId,
              );
          },
        )
        .map(
          (
            candidate,
            index,
          ): PlanAttentionCandidate => ({
            rank:
              index +
              1,
            ...candidate,
          }),
        );

    const eligible =
      candidates.filter(
        (candidate) =>
          candidate.eligible &&
          candidate.score >=
            policy
              .minimumFocusScore,
      );

    const warnings:
      string[] =
        [];

    if (
      planBlocked
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Parent cognitive plan is blocked; no plan step may receive executable attention.",
      );
    }

    if (
      userAttention ===
        "unavailable"
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "User attention is unavailable; steps that explicitly require user attention are deprioritized.",
      );
    }

    if (
      candidates.some(
        (candidate) =>
          candidate.step
            .requiredTools
            .length >
          0,
      )
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Tool requirements affect attention only; OH-4R does not invoke tools.",
      );
    }

    let selected:
      PlanAttentionCandidate
      | undefined;

    let reasonCode:
      PlanFocusReason;

    if (
      candidates.length ===
      0
    ) {
      reasonCode =
        "no-candidates";
    } else if (
      eligible.length ===
      0
    ) {
      reasonCode =
        "no-eligible-step";
    } else {
      const challenger =
        eligible[0];

      const incumbent =
        input.previousStepId
          ? eligible.find(
              (candidate) =>
                candidate.stepId ===
                input
                  .previousStepId,
            )
          : undefined;

      if (
        !input.previousStepId ||
        !incumbent
      ) {
        selected =
          challenger;

        reasonCode =
          input.previousStepId
            ? "switched-focus"
            : "initial-focus";
      } else if (
        challenger.stepId ===
        incumbent.stepId
      ) {
        selected =
          incumbent;

        reasonCode =
          "retained-focus";
      } else if (
        challenger.score <
        incumbent.score +
          policy.switchMargin
      ) {
        selected =
          incumbent;

        reasonCode =
          "retained-focus";
      } else {
        selected =
          challenger;

        reasonCode =
          "switched-focus";
      }
    }

    return {
      version:
        PRIORITY_ATTENTION_ENGINE_VERSION,
      generatedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      reason:
        reasonCode,
      changed:
        (
          input.previousStepId ??
          undefined
        ) !==
        selected?.stepId,
      previousStepId:
        input.previousStepId,
      selected:
        selected
          ? structuredClone(
              selected,
            )
          : undefined,
      candidates:
        structuredClone(
          candidates,
        ),
      planStatus:
        input.plan.status,
      userAttention,
      policy:
        structuredClone(
          policy,
        ),
      invariants: {
        dependenciesCanBeBypassed:
          false,
        blockedStepsCanBeSelected:
          false,
        schedulingPerformed:
          false,
        executionPerformed:
          false,
        planMutated:
          false,
      },
      warnings,
    };
  }
}

const defaultPriorityAttentionEngine =
  new PriorityAttentionEngine();

export function getPriorityAttentionEngine():
  PriorityAttentionEngine {
  return defaultPriorityAttentionEngine;
}

export const COGNITIVE_PROFILES_VERSION =
  "oh4ac.v1";

export type CognitiveProfileName =
  | "fast"
  | "deep"
  | "private"
  | "cheap"
  | "experimental";

export type CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort =
  | "standard"
  | "deep"
  | "very-deep"
  | "maximum";

export type CognitiveProfileVerificationMode =
  | "standard"
  | "strict";

export interface CognitiveProfilePriorities {
  quality: number;
  latency: number;
  cost: number;
  privacy: number;
  exploration: number;
}

export interface CognitiveProfileDefinition {
  name:
    CognitiveProfileName;

  displayName: string;

  description: string;

  priorities:
    CognitiveProfilePriorities;

  routing: {
    preferLocal:
      boolean;

    requireLocal:
      boolean;

    preferLowCost:
      boolean;

    allowExperimentalCandidates:
      boolean;

    candidateBreadth:
      number;
  };

  strategy: {
    preferred:
      string[];

    maxPasses:
      number;

    allowMultiModel:
      boolean;

    allowCritic:
      boolean;

    allowCompetition:
      boolean;

    allowRefinement:
      boolean;
  };

  reasoningEffort:
    CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort;

  verification:
    CognitiveProfileVerificationMode;
}

export interface CognitiveProfileTaskConstraints {
  privacySensitivity?:
    string;

  localOnly?:
    boolean;

  remoteAllowed?:
    boolean;

  allowExperimental?:
    boolean;

  validationRequired?:
    boolean;

  maxPasses?:
    number;

  reasoningDepth?:
    string;

  costSensitive?:
    boolean;

  latencySensitive?:
    boolean;
}

export interface CognitiveProfileRoutingHints {
  profile:
    CognitiveProfileName;

  priorities:
    CognitiveProfilePriorities;

  requireLocal:
    boolean;

  allowRemote:
    boolean;

  preferLocal:
    boolean;

  preferLowCost:
    boolean;

  allowExperimentalCandidates:
    boolean;

  candidateBreadth:
    number;

  preferredStrategies:
    string[];

  maxPasses:
    number;

  reasoningEffort:
    CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort;

  verification:
    CognitiveProfileVerificationMode;

  validationRequired:
    boolean;

  warnings:
    string[];

  invariants: {
    taskPrivacyCanBeWeakened:
      false;

    taskPassBudgetCanBeExceeded:
      false;

    requiredReasoningCanBeReduced:
      false;

    registryEligibilityBypassed:
      false;

    authorityModified:
      false;

    safetyModified:
      false;

    verificationDisabled:
      false;

    workflowRewritten:
      false;
  };
}

function weight(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function positiveInteger(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a positive integer.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function validateDefinition(
  profile:
    CognitiveProfileDefinition,
): CognitiveProfileDefinition {
  for (
    const [
      key,
      value,
    ]
    of Object.entries(
      profile.priorities,
    )
  ) {
    weight(
      value,
      `profile.${profile.name}.priorities.${key}`,
    );
  }

  positiveInteger(
    profile.routing
      .candidateBreadth,
    `profile.${profile.name}.routing.candidateBreadth`,
  );

  positiveInteger(
    profile.strategy
      .maxPasses,
    `profile.${profile.name}.strategy.maxPasses`,
  );

  if (
    profile.strategy
      .preferred.length ===
    0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `profile.${profile.name}.strategy.preferred must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return structuredClone(
    profile,
  );
}

for (
  const profile
  of Object.values(
    PROFILES,
  )
) {
  validateDefinition(
    profile,
  );
}

function requiredReasoningEffort(
  depth:
    string
    | undefined,
): CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort {
  switch (
    depth
      ?.trim()
      .toLowerCase()
  ) {
    case "very-deep":
    case "very_deep":
    case "maximum":
      return "very-deep";

    case "deep":
      return "deep";

    default:
      return "standard";
  }
}

const REASONING_ORDER:
  CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort[] = [
    "standard",
    "deep",
    "very-deep",
    "maximum",
  ];

function maxReasoningEffort(
  left:
    CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort,
  right:
    CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort,
): CognitiveProfileReasoningEffort {
  return (
    REASONING_ORDER
      .indexOf(
        left,
      ) >=
    REASONING_ORDER
      .indexOf(
        right,
      )
  )
    ? left
    : right;
}

function privacyRequiresLocal(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
): boolean {
  const normalized =
    value
      ?.trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  return (
    normalized ===
      "highly-sensitive" ||
    normalized ===
      "highly_sensitive" ||
    normalized ===
      "local-only" ||
    normalized ===
      "local_only"
  );
}

function effectivePriorities(
  profile:
    CognitiveProfileDefinition,
  task:
    CognitiveProfileTaskConstraints,
): CognitiveProfilePriorities {
  const priorities =
    structuredClone(
      profile.priorities,
    );

  if (
    task.costSensitive
  ) {
    priorities.cost =
      Math.max(
        priorities.cost,
        0.9,
      );
  }

  if (
    task.latencySensitive
  ) {
    priorities.latency =
      Math.max(
        priorities.latency,
        0.9,
      );
  }

  if (
    privacyRequiresLocal(
      task.privacySensitivity,
    ) ||
    task.localOnly
  ) {
    priorities.privacy =
      1;
  }

  return priorities;
}

function effectivePassBudget(
  profileBudget: number,
  taskBudget:
    number
    | undefined,
): number {
  if (
    taskBudget ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return profileBudget;
  }

  positiveInteger(
    taskBudget,
    "task.maxPasses",
  );

  return Math.min(
    profileBudget,
    taskBudget,
  );
}

export function listCognitiveProfiles():
  CognitiveProfileDefinition[] {
  return Object.values(
    PROFILES,
  )
    .map(
      (profile) =>
        structuredClone(
          profile,
        ),
    );
}

export function getCognitiveProfile(
  name:
    CognitiveProfileName,
): CognitiveProfileDefinition {
  const profile =
    PROFILES[name];

  if (!profile) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unknown cognitive profile: ${String(name)}`,
    );
  }

  return structuredClone(
    profile,
  );
}

export function resolveCognitiveProfile(
  name:
    CognitiveProfileName,
  task:
    CognitiveProfileTaskConstraints =
      {},
): CognitiveProfileRoutingHints {
  const profile =
    getCognitiveProfile(
      name,
    );

  const taskForcesLocal =
    task.localOnly ===
      true ||
    privacyRequiresLocal(
      task.privacySensitivity,
    );

  const requireLocal =
    profile.routing
      .requireLocal ||
    taskForcesLocal;

  const allowRemote =
    !requireLocal &&
    task.remoteAllowed !==
      false;

  const allowExperimentalCandidates =
    profile.routing
      .allowExperimentalCandidates &&
    task.allowExperimental !==
      false;

  const validationRequired =
    task.validationRequired ??
    false;

  const minimumEffort =
    requiredReasoningEffort(
      task.reasoningDepth,
    );

  const reasoningEffort =
    maxReasoningEffort(
      profile.reasoningEffort,
      minimumEffort,
    );

  const maxPasses =
    effectivePassBudget(
      profile.strategy
        .maxPasses,
      task.maxPasses,
    );

  const verification:
    CognitiveProfileVerificationMode =
      validationRequired ||
      profile.verification ===
        "strict"
        ? "strict"
        : "standard";

  const warnings:
    string[] =
      [];

  if (
    profile.name ===
      "experimental" &&
    !allowExperimentalCandidates
  ) {
    warnings.push(
      "Experimental profile is active, but task constraints disallow experimental candidates.",
    );
  }

  if (
    profile.name !==
      "private" &&
    taskForcesLocal
  ) {
    warnings.push(
      "Task privacy constraints override profile remote preferences; local execution is required.",
    );
  }

  if (
    task.maxPasses !==
      undefined &&
    profile.strategy
      .maxPasses >
      task.maxPasses
  ) {
    warnings.push(
      "Profile pass budget was clamped to the task/reasoning-contract ceiling.",
    );
  }

  if (
    reasoningEffort !==
    profile.reasoningEffort
  ) {
    warnings.push(
      "Profile reasoning effort was raised to satisfy the task's required reasoning depth.",
    );
  }

  return {
    profile:
      profile.name,

    priorities:
      effectivePriorities(
        profile,
        task,
      ),

    requireLocal,

    allowRemote,

    preferLocal:
      requireLocal ||
      profile.routing
        .preferLocal,

    preferLowCost:
      profile.routing
        .preferLowCost,

    allowExperimentalCandidates,

    candidateBreadth:
      profile.routing
        .candidateBreadth,

    preferredStrategies:
      structuredClone(
        profile.strategy
          .preferred,
      ),

    maxPasses,

    reasoningEffort,

    verification,

    validationRequired,

    warnings,

    invariants: {
      taskPrivacyCanBeWeakened:
        false,

      taskPassBudgetCanBeExceeded:
        false,

      requiredReasoningCanBeReduced:
        false,

      registryEligibilityBypassed:
        false,

      authorityModified:
        false,

      safetyModified:
        false,

      verificationDisabled:
        false,

      workflowRewritten:
        false,
    },
  };
}

FILE: packages\models\src\modelRegistry.ts

import {
  getModelCandidates,
  type ModelRole,
} from "./modelRouter";

export type ModelProviderId = string;

export type ModelCapability =
  | "chat"
  | "planning"
  | "coding"
  | "repair"
  | "json"
  | "tools"
  | "vision"
  | "embeddings"
  | "other";

export type ModelRegistrySource =
  | "builtin"
  | "configuration"
  | "discovery"
  | "user"
  | "runtime";

export type ModelAvailability =
  | "available"
  | "unavailable"
  | "unknown";

export interface ModelContextLimits {
  known: boolean;
  contextWindowTokens?: number;
  maxOutputTokens?: number;
}

export interface ModelCostCharacteristics {
  known: boolean;
  mode:
    | "local-compute"
    | "metered"
    | "subscription"
    | "unknown";
  currency?: string;
  inputPerMillionTokens?: number;
  outputPerMillionTokens?: number;
}

export interface ModelPrivacyProperties {
  execution:
    | "local"
    | "remote"
    | "hybrid"
    | "unknown";
  promptLeavesDevice:
    | boolean
    | null;
  retention:
    | "none"
    | "local-runtime"
    | "provider-policy"
    | "unknown";
}

export interface ModelRegistryEntry {
  id: string;
  provider: ModelProviderId;
  model: string;
  displayName: string;

  roles: ModelRole[];
  capabilities: ModelCapability[];

  enabled: boolean;
  local: boolean;

  availability: ModelAvailability;
  version?: string;

  contextLimits: ModelContextLimits;
  cost: ModelCostCharacteristics;
  privacy: ModelPrivacyProperties;

  strengths: string[];
  weaknesses: string[];

  source: ModelRegistrySource;
  tags: string[];

  metadata?: Record<
    string,
    string | number | boolean | null
  >;
}

export interface ModelRegistryQuery {
  provider?: ModelProviderId;
  role?: ModelRole;
  capability?: ModelCapability;
  enabled?: boolean;
  local?: boolean;
}

function normalizeProvider(
  provider: string,
): string {
  return provider
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function normalizeModel(
  model: string,
): string {
  return model.trim();
}

export function createModelRegistryId(
  provider: string,
  model: string,
): string {
  const normalizedProvider =
    normalizeProvider(provider);

  const normalizedModel =
    normalizeModel(model);

  if (!normalizedProvider) {
    throw new Error(
      "Model provider must not be empty.",
    );
  }

  if (!normalizedModel) {
    throw new Error(
      "Model name must not be empty.",
    );
  }

  return `${normalizedProvider}:${normalizedModel}`;
}

function cloneEntry(
  entry: ModelRegistryEntry,
): ModelRegistryEntry {
  return structuredClone(entry);
}

function uniqueSorted<T extends string>(
  values: readonly T[],
): T[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(values),
  ].sort() as T[];
}

function normalizeEntry(
  entry: ModelRegistryEntry,
): ModelRegistryEntry {
  const provider =
    normalizeProvider(entry.provider);

  const model =
    normalizeModel(entry.model);

  const id =
    createModelRegistryId(
      provider,
      model,
    );

  if (
    entry.id &&
    entry.id !== id
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `Model registry id mismatch: expected ${id}, received ${entry.id}`,
    );
  }

  return {
    ...structuredClone(entry),
    id,
    provider,
    model,
    displayName:
      entry.displayName.trim() ||
      model,
    roles:
      uniqueSorted(entry.roles),
    capabilities:
      uniqueSorted(
        entry.capabilities,
      ),
    version:
      entry.version?.trim() ||
      undefined,
    strengths:
      uniqueSorted(
        entry.strengths
          .map(
            (value) =>
              value.trim(),
          )
          .filter(Boolean),
      ),
    weaknesses:
      uniqueSorted(
        entry.weaknesses
          .map(
            (value) =>
              value.trim(),
          )
          .filter(Boolean),
      ),
    tags:
      uniqueSorted(
        entry.tags
          .map(
            (tag) =>
              tag.trim(),
          )
          .filter(Boolean),
      ),
  };
}

export class ModelRegistry {
  private readonly entries =
    new Map<
      string,
      ModelRegistryEntry
    >();

  register(
    entry: ModelRegistryEntry,
  ): ModelRegistryEntry {
    const normalized =
      normalizeEntry(entry);

    if (
      this.entries.has(
        normalized.id,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Model is already registered: ${normalized.id}`,
      );
    }

    this.entries.set(
      normalized.id,
      normalized,
    );

    return cloneEntry(
      normalized,
    );
  }

  upsert(
    entry: ModelRegistryEntry,
  ): ModelRegistryEntry {
    const normalized =
      normalizeEntry(entry);

    this.entries.set(
      normalized.id,
      normalized,
    );

    return cloneEntry(
      normalized,
    );
  }

  get(
    id: string,
  ): ModelRegistryEntry | undefined {
    const entry =
      this.entries.get(id);

    return entry
      ? cloneEntry(entry)
      : undefined;
  }

  getByProviderModel(
    provider: string,
    model: string,
  ): ModelRegistryEntry | undefined {
    return this.get(
      createModelRegistryId(
        provider,
        model,
      ),
    );
  }

  has(
    id: string,
  ): boolean {
    return this.entries.has(id);
  }

  remove(
    id: string,
  ): boolean {
    return this.entries.delete(id);
  }

  list(
    query: ModelRegistryQuery = {},
  ): ModelRegistryEntry[] {
    const provider =
      query.provider
        ? normalizeProvider(
            query.provider,
          )
        : undefined;

    return [
      ...this.entries.values(),
    ]
      .filter(
        (entry) =>
          (
            provider === undefined ||
            entry.provider === provider
          ) &&
          (
            query.role === undefined ||
            entry.roles.includes(
              query.role,
            )
          ) &&
          (
            query.capability === undefined ||
            entry.capabilities.includes(
              query.capability,
            )
          ) &&
          (
            query.enabled === undefined ||
            entry.enabled ===
              query.enabled
          ) &&
          (
            query.local === undefined ||
            entry.local ===
              query.local
          ),
      )
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          a.id.localeCompare(
            b.id,
          ),
      )
      .map(cloneEntry);
  }
}

function capabilitiesForRole(
  role: ModelRole,
): ModelCapability[] {
  switch (role) {
    case "planner":
      return [
        "planning",
      ];

    case "code":
      return [
        "coding",
      ];

    case "repair":
      return [
        "repair",
      ];

    case "default":
    default:
      return [
        "chat",
      ];
  }
}

export function createConfiguredModelRegistry():
  ModelRegistry {
  const registry =
    new ModelRegistry();

  const roles:
    ModelRole[] = [
      "default",
      "code",
      "planner",
      "repair",
    ];

  for (const role of roles) {
    const candidates =
      getModelCandidates(role);

    for (
      const candidate
      of candidates
    ) {
      const id =
        createModelRegistryId(
          "ollama",
          candidate.model,
        );

      const existing =
        registry.get(id);

      if (existing) {
        registry.upsert({
          ...existing,
          roles:
            uniqueSorted([
              ...existing.roles,
              role,
            ]),
          capabilities:
            uniqueSorted([
              ...existing.capabilities,
              ...capabilitiesForRole(
                role,
              ),
            ]),
          tags:
            uniqueSorted([
              ...existing.tags,
              candidate.source,
            ]),
        });

        continue;
      }

      registry.register({
        id,
        provider:
          "ollama",
        model:
          candidate.model,
        displayName:
          candidate.model,
        roles: [
          role,
        ],
        capabilities:
          capabilitiesForRole(
            role,
          ),
        enabled:
          true,
        local:
          true,
        availability:
          "unknown",
        contextLimits: {
          known:
            false,
        },
        cost: {
          known:
            true,
          mode:
            "local-compute",
        },
        privacy: {
          execution:
            "local",
          promptLeavesDevice:
            false,
          retention:
            "unknown",
        },
        strengths: [
          ...capabilitiesForRole(
            role,
          ),
        ],
        weaknesses: [
          "Empirical performance not yet calibrated",
        ],
        source:
          "configuration",
        tags: [
          candidate.source,
        ],
      });
    }
  }

  return registry;
}

const defaultModelRegistry =
  createConfiguredModelRegistry();

export function getDefaultModelRegistry():
  ModelRegistry {
  return defaultModelRegistry;
}

import {
  getDefaultModelRegistry,
} from "./modelRegistry";
import type {
  InputInterpretation,
} from "./inputInterpreter";

export const MODEL_ROUTER_VERSION =
  "oh4m.v1";

export interface ModelRoutingCandidate {
  id: string;
  provider: string;
  model: string;
  displayName: string;

  enabled: boolean;
  availability:
    | "available"
    | "unavailable"
    | "unknown";

  local: boolean;

  roles: string[];
  capabilities: string[];

  contextLimits: {
    known: boolean;
    contextWindowTokens?: number;
    maxOutputTokens?: number;
  };

  cost: {
    known: boolean;
    mode:
      | "local-compute"
      | "metered"
      | "subscription"
      | "unknown";
    currency?: string;
    inputPerMillionTokens?: number;
    outputPerMillionTokens?: number;
  };

  privacy: {
    execution:
      | "local"
      | "remote"
      | "hybrid"
      | "unknown";
    promptLeavesDevice:
      | boolean
      | null;
    retention:
      | "none"
      | "local-runtime"
      | "provider-policy"
      | "unknown";
  };

  strengths: string[];
  weaknesses: string[];
  tags: string[];
}

export interface ModelCalibrationEvidence {
  score: number;
  confidence: number;
  sampleCount?: number;
}

export interface ModelPreferenceEvidence {
  score: number;
  confidence: number;
  sampleCount?: number;
}

export interface ModelTelemetryEvidence {
  successRate?: number;
  failureRate?: number;
  averageLatencyMs?: number;
  confidence?: number;
  sampleCount?: number;
}

export interface ModelRoutingEvidence {
  objectiveCalibration?: Record<
    string,
    ModelCalibrationEvidence
  >;

  subjectivePreference?: Record<
    string,
    ModelPreferenceEvidence
  >;

  telemetry?: Record<
    string,
    ModelTelemetryEvidence
  >;
}

export interface ModelRoutingConstraints {
  requiredModelIds?: string[];
  excludedModelIds?: string[];

  requireLocal?: boolean;
  allowRemote?: boolean;

  maxInputCostPerMillion?: number;
  maxOutputCostPerMillion?: number;

  maxLatencyMs?: number;

  minimumRouteConfidence?: number;
}

export type ModelRouteComponent =
  | "objective-calibration"
  | "task-fit"
  | "privacy"
  | "latency"
  | "cost"
  | "availability"
  | "subjective-preference"
  | "reliability";

export interface ModelRouteScoreComponent {

  score: number;
  weight: number;
  weightedScore: number;

    | "measured"
    | "declared"
    | "derived"

}

function clamp01(
  value: number,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function asRecord(
  value: unknown,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    value &&
    typeof value ===
      "object" &&
    !Array.isArray(
      value,
    )
  )
    ? value as
        Record<
          string,
          unknown
        >
    : {};
}

function asString(
  value: unknown,
  fallback = "",
): string {
  return typeof value ===
    "string"
    ? value
    : fallback;
}

function asBoolean(
  value: unknown,
  fallback = false,
): boolean {
  return typeof value ===
    "boolean"
    ? value
    : fallback;
}

function asNumber(
  value: unknown,
): number | undefined {
  return (
    typeof value ===
      "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  )
    ? value
    : undefined;
}

function asStrings(
  value: unknown,
): string[] {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  return [
    ...new Set(
      value
        .filter(
          (
            entry,
          ): entry is string =>
            typeof entry ===
            "string",
        )
        .map(
          (entry) =>
            entry
              .trim()
              .toLowerCase(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function normalizeRegistryCandidate(
  value: unknown,
): ModelRoutingCandidate | null {
  const record =
    asRecord(
      value,
    );

  const provider =
    asString(
      record.provider,
    ).trim();

  const model =
    asString(
      record.model,
    ).trim();

  const id =
    asString(
      record.id,
      provider &&
      model
        ? `${provider}:${model}`
        : "",
    ).trim();

  if (
    !id ||
    !provider ||
    !model
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const contextLimits =
    asRecord(
      record.contextLimits,
    );

  const cost =
    asRecord(
      record.cost,
    );

  const privacy =
    asRecord(
      record.privacy,
    );

  const availabilityRaw =
    asString(
      record.availability,
      "unknown",
    );

  const availability =
    availabilityRaw ===
      "available" ||
    availabilityRaw ===
      "unavailable"
      ? availabilityRaw
      : "unknown";

  const costModeRaw =
    asString(
      cost.mode,
      "unknown",
    );

  const costMode:
    ModelRoutingCandidate[
      "cost"
    ]["mode"] =
      costModeRaw ===
        "local-compute" ||
      costModeRaw ===
        "metered" ||
      costModeRaw ===
        "subscription"
        ? costModeRaw
        : "unknown";

  const executionRaw =
    asString(
      privacy.execution,
      "unknown",
    );

  const execution:
    ModelRoutingCandidate[
      "privacy"
    ]["execution"] =
      executionRaw ===
        "local" ||
      executionRaw ===
        "remote" ||
      executionRaw ===
        "hybrid"
        ? executionRaw
        : "unknown";

  const retentionRaw =
    asString(
      privacy.retention,
      "unknown",
    );

  const retention:
    ModelRoutingCandidate[
      "privacy"
    ]["retention"] =
      retentionRaw ===
        "none" ||
      retentionRaw ===
        "local-runtime" ||
      retentionRaw ===
        "provider-policy"
        ? retentionRaw
        : "unknown";

  const promptLeavesDevice =
    typeof privacy
      .promptLeavesDevice ===
      "boolean"
      ? privacy
          .promptLeavesDevice
      : null;

  return {
    id,
    provider,
    model,
    displayName:
      asString(
        record.displayName,
        id,
      ),
    enabled:
      asBoolean(
        record.enabled,
        true,
      ),
    availability,
    local:
      asBoolean(
        record.local,
        execution ===
          "local",
      ),
    roles:
      asStrings(
        record.roles,
      ),
    capabilities:
      asStrings(
        record.capabilities,
      ),
    contextLimits: {
      known:
        asBoolean(
          contextLimits.known,
          false,
        ),
      contextWindowTokens:
        asNumber(
          contextLimits
            .contextWindowTokens,
        ),
      maxOutputTokens:
        asNumber(
          contextLimits
            .maxOutputTokens,
        ),
    },
    cost: {
      known:
        asBoolean(
          cost.known,
          false,
        ),
      mode:
        costMode,
      currency:
        asString(
          cost.currency,
        ) ||
        undefined,
      inputPerMillionTokens:
        asNumber(
          cost
            .inputPerMillionTokens,
        ),
      outputPerMillionTokens:
        asNumber(
          cost
            .outputPerMillionTokens,
        ),
    },
    privacy: {
      execution,
      promptLeavesDevice,
      retention,
    },
    strengths:
      asStrings(
        record.strengths,
      ),
    weaknesses:
      asStrings(
        record.weaknesses,
      ),
    tags:
      asStrings(
        record.tags,
      ),
  };
}

function defaultCandidateSource():
  ModelRoutingCandidate[] {
  const registry =
    getDefaultModelRegistry();

  return registry
    .list()
    .map(
      (entry) =>
        normalizeRegistryCandidate(
          entry,
        ),
    )
    .filter(
      (
        entry,
      ): entry is
        ModelRoutingCandidate =>
          entry !== null,
    );
}

function normalizedSet(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    (values ?? [])
      .map(
        (value) =>
          value
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase(),
      )
      .filter(Boolean),
  );
}

function taskTerms(
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): Set<string> {
  return normalizedSet([
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .primaryDomain.value,
    ...interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .secondaryDomains,
    ...(
      interpretation
        .taskSignals
        .requiredTools ??
      []
    ),
  ]);
}

function candidateTerms(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
): Set<string> {
  return normalizedSet([
    ...candidate.roles,
    ...candidate.capabilities,
    ...candidate.strengths,
    ...candidate.tags,
  ]);
}

function taskFitScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const wanted =
    taskTerms(
      interpretation,
    );

  const offered =
    candidateTerms(
      candidate,
    );

  if (
    wanted.size ===
    0
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0.5,
      reasons: [
        "No task-fit terms were available; neutral task-fit score used.",
      ],
    };
  }

  let matches =
    0;

  const matched:
    string[] =
      [];

  for (
    const term
    of wanted
  ) {
    if (
      offered.has(
        term,
      )
    ) {
      matches += 1;
      matched.push(
        term,
      );
    }
  }

  const domain =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .primaryDomain.value
      .toLowerCase();

  let domainBoost =
    0;

  if (
    offered.has(
      domain,
    )
  ) {
    domainBoost =
      0.25;
  }

  const score =
    clamp01(
      0.35 +
      Math.min(
        0.4,
        matches *
          0.12,
      ) +
      domainBoost,
    );

  return {
    score,
    reasons:
      matched.length >
      0
        ? [
            `Matched task-fit terms: ${matched.join(", ")}.`,
          ]
        : [
            "No declared capability/tag directly matched the task; conservative baseline used.",
          ],
  };
}

function privacyScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const privacy =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .privacySensitivity.value;

  const local =
    candidate.local ||
    candidate.privacy
      .execution ===
      "local";

  const leaves =
    candidate.privacy
      .promptLeavesDevice ===
      true;

  if (
    privacy ===
      "highly-sensitive"
  ) {
    if (
      local &&
      !leaves
    ) {
      return {
        score:
          1,
        reasons: [
          "Highly-sensitive task remains local.",
        ],
      };
    }

    return {
      score:
        0,
      reasons: [
        "Highly-sensitive task is not guaranteed to remain local.",
      ],
    };
  }

  if (
    privacy ===
      "sensitive"
  ) {
    return local &&
      !leaves
      ? {
          score:
            1,
          reasons: [
            "Sensitive task benefits from local execution.",
          ],
        }
      : {
          score:
            0.35,
          reasons: [
            "Sensitive task would use non-local/uncertain privacy handling.",
          ],
        };
  }

  if (
    privacy ===
      "personal" ||
    privacy ===
      "internal"
  ) {
    return local
      ? {
          score:
            0.9,
          reasons: [
            "Local execution reduces exposure for non-public context.",
          ],
        }
      : {
          score:
            0.65,
          reasons: [
            "Remote execution is acceptable but less privacy-preserving than local.",
          ],
        };
  }

  return {
    score:
      local
        ? 0.85
        : 0.75,
    reasons: [
      "Public/low-sensitivity task has no strong privacy restriction.",
    ],
  };
}

function costScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
  constraints:
    ModelRoutingConstraints,
): {
  score: number;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  if (
    candidate.cost.mode ===
      "local-compute"
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        1,
      reasons: [
        "Local-compute model has no metered token charge in the registry.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const inputCost =
    candidate.cost
      .inputPerMillionTokens;

  const outputCost =
    candidate.cost
      .outputPerMillionTokens;

  if (
    constraints
      .maxInputCostPerMillion !==
      undefined &&
    inputCost !==
      undefined &&
    inputCost >
      constraints
        .maxInputCostPerMillion
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0,
      reasons: [
        "Input token cost exceeds explicit routing ceiling.",
      ],
    };
  }

  if (
    constraints
      .maxOutputCostPerMillion !==
      undefined &&
    outputCost !==
      undefined &&
    outputCost >
      constraints
        .maxOutputCostPerMillion
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0,
      reasons: [
        "Output token cost exceeds explicit routing ceiling.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const tolerance =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .costTolerance.value;

  if (
    !candidate.cost.known
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        tolerance ===
          "minimal"
          ? 0.35
          : 0.5,
      reasons: [
        "Cost is unknown; router does not assume it is cheap.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const combined =
    (
      inputCost ??
      0
    ) +
    (
      outputCost ??
      0
    );

  const divisor =
    tolerance ===
      "minimal"
      ? 10
      : tolerance ===
          "low"
        ? 25
        : tolerance ===
            "standard"
          ? 75
          : 150;

  return {
    score:
      clamp01(
        1 -
        combined /
          divisor,
      ),
    reasons: [
      `Known metered cost considered against ${tolerance} task tolerance.`,
    ],
  };
}

function latencyScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
  evidence:
    ModelRoutingEvidence,
  constraints:
    ModelRoutingConstraints,
): {
  score: number;
  confidence: number;
  measured: boolean;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const telemetry =
    evidence.telemetry
      ?.[candidate.id];

  const latency =
    telemetry
      ?.averageLatencyMs;

  if (
    latency ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        interpretation
          .taskIntelligence
          .latencyTolerance.value ===
          "immediate"
          ? 0.4
          : 0.55,
      confidence:
        0.25,
      measured:
        false,
      reasons: [
        "No measured latency evidence is available.",
      ],
    };
  }

  if (
    constraints
      .maxLatencyMs !==
      undefined &&
    latency >
      constraints
        .maxLatencyMs
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0,
      confidence:
        clamp01(
          telemetry
            ?.confidence ??
          0.7,
        ),
      measured:
        true,
      reasons: [
        `Measured latency ${latency}ms exceeds explicit ceiling.`,
      ],
    };
  }

  const tolerance =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .latencyTolerance.value;

  const target =
    tolerance ===
      "immediate"
      ? 1_500
      : tolerance ===
          "interactive"
        ? 5_000
        : tolerance ===
            "flexible"
          ? 20_000
          : 60_000;

  return {
    score:
      clamp01(
        target /
        Math.max(
          target,
          latency,
        ),
      ),
    confidence:
      clamp01(
        telemetry
          ?.confidence ??
        0.7,
      ),
    measured:
      true,
    reasons: [
      `Measured average latency ${latency}ms evaluated against ${tolerance} target ${target}ms.`,
    ],
  };
}

function reliabilityScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  evidence:
    ModelRoutingEvidence,
): {
  score: number;
  confidence: number;
  measured: boolean;
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const telemetry =
    evidence.telemetry
      ?.[candidate.id];

  if (
    !telemetry
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0.5,
      confidence:
        0.2,
      measured:
        false,
      reasons: [
        "No measured reliability evidence is available.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const success =
    telemetry.successRate;

  const failure =
    telemetry.failureRate;

  if (
    success ===
      undefined &&
    failure ===
      undefined
  ) {
    return {
      score:
        0.5,
      confidence:
        clamp01(
          telemetry.confidence ??
          0.3,
        ),
      measured:
        false,
      reasons: [
        "Telemetry exists but contains no success/failure rate.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const score =
    success !==
      undefined
      ? clamp01(
          success,
        )
      : clamp01(
          1 -
          (
            failure ??
            0
          ),
        );

  return {
    score,
    confidence:
      clamp01(
        telemetry.confidence ??
        0.7,
      ),
    measured:
      true,
    reasons: [
      "Reliability score derives from measured invocation telemetry.",
    ],
  };
}

function availabilityScore(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
): number {
  switch (
    candidate.availability
  ) {
    case "available":
      return 1;
    case "unknown":
      return 0.5;
    case "unavailable":
    default:
      return 0;
  }
}

function dynamicWeights(
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
): Record<
  ModelRouteComponent,
  number
> {
  const weights:
    Record<
      ModelRouteComponent,
      number
    > = {
      "objective-calibration":
        0.24,
      "task-fit":
        0.18,
      privacy:
        0.14,
      latency:
        0.1,
      cost:
        0.09,
      availability:
        0.08,
      "subjective-preference":
        0.07,
      reliability:
        0.1,
    };

  const factuality =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .factuality.value;

  const privacy =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .privacySensitivity.value;

  const latency =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .latencyTolerance.value;

  const cost =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .costTolerance.value;

  if (
    factuality ===
      "critical" ||
    factuality ===
      "high"
  ) {
    weights[
      "objective-calibration"
    ] += 0.08;
    weights[
      "subjective-preference"
    ] -= 0.03;
    weights.cost -=
      0.02;
    weights.latency -=
      0.03;
  }

  if (
    privacy ===
      "highly-sensitive" ||
    privacy ===
      "sensitive"
  ) {
    weights.privacy +=
      0.1;
    weights[
      "subjective-preference"
    ] -= 0.02;
    weights.cost -=
      0.03;
    weights.latency -=
      0.05;
  }

  if (
    latency ===
      "immediate"
  ) {
    weights.latency +=
      0.1;
    weights[
      "objective-calibration"
    ] -= 0.04;
    weights[
      "subjective-preference"
    ] -= 0.02;
    weights.cost -=
      0.04;
  }

  if (
    cost ===
      "minimal" ||
    cost ===
      "low"
  ) {
    weights.cost +=
      0.1;
    weights[
      "subjective-preference"
    ] -= 0.02;
    weights[
      "objective-calibration"
    ] -= 0.04;
    weights.latency -=
      0.04;
  }

  const total =
    Object.values(
      weights,
    ).reduce(
      (
        sum,
        value,
      ) =>
        sum +
        Math.max(
          0,
          value,
        ),
      0,
    );

  for (
    const key
    of Object.keys(
      weights,
    ) as
      ModelRouteComponent[]
  ) {
    weights[key] =
      total > 0
        ? Math.max(
            0,
            weights[key],
          ) /
          total
        : 0;
  }

  return weights;
}

function eligibility(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
  constraints:
    ModelRoutingConstraints,
): string[] {
  const reasons:
    string[] =
      [];

  const required =
    normalizedSet(
      constraints
        .requiredModelIds,
    );

  const excluded =
    normalizedSet(
      constraints
        .excludedModelIds,
    );

  if (
    !candidate.enabled
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "model is disabled",
    );
  }

  if (
    candidate.availability ===
      "unavailable"
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "model is unavailable",
    );
  }

  if (
    required.size >
      0 &&
    !required.has(
      candidate.id
        .toLowerCase(),
    )
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "model is not in requiredModelIds",
    );
  }

  if (
    excluded.has(
      candidate.id
        .toLowerCase(),
    )
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "model is explicitly excluded",
    );
  }

  const isLocal =
    candidate.local ||
    candidate.privacy
      .execution ===
      "local";

  if (
    constraints.requireLocal &&
    !isLocal
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "explicit constraint requires local execution",
    );
  }

  if (
    constraints.allowRemote ===
      false &&
    !isLocal
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "remote execution is explicitly disallowed",
    );
  }

  const privacySensitivity =
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .privacySensitivity
      .value;

  const privacyRequiresLocal =
    privacySensitivity ===
      "highly-sensitive" ||
    interpretation
      .taskIntelligence
      .derived
      .prefersLocalExecution ===
      true;

  if (
    privacyRequiresLocal &&
    (
      !isLocal ||
      candidate.privacy
        .promptLeavesDevice ===
        true
    )
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      privacySensitivity ===
        "highly-sensitive"
        ? "highly-sensitive task requires a model that keeps the prompt local"
        : "task intelligence requires local execution for this privacy-sensitive task",
    );
  }

  if (
    constraints
      .maxInputCostPerMillion !==
      undefined &&
    candidate.cost
      .inputPerMillionTokens !==
      undefined &&
    candidate.cost
      .inputPerMillionTokens >
      constraints
        .maxInputCostPerMillion
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "input cost exceeds explicit ceiling",
    );
  }

  if (
    constraints
      .maxOutputCostPerMillion !==
      undefined &&
    candidate.cost
      .outputPerMillionTokens !==
      undefined &&
    candidate.cost
      .outputPerMillionTokens >
      constraints
        .maxOutputCostPerMillion
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "output cost exceeds explicit ceiling",
    );
  }

  return reasons;
}

function component(
  name:
    ModelRouteComponent,
  score:
    number,
  weight:
    number,
  confidence:
    number,
  evidence:
    ModelRouteScoreComponent[
      "evidence"
    ],
  reasons:
    string[],
): ModelRouteScoreComponent {
  const normalizedScore =
    clamp01(
      score,
    );

  const normalizedWeight =
    Math.max(
      0,
      weight,
    );

  return {
    component:
      name,
    score:
      normalizedScore,
    weight:
      normalizedWeight,
    weightedScore:
      normalizedScore *
      normalizedWeight,
    confidence:
      clamp01(
        confidence,
      ),
    evidence,
    reasons,
  };
}

function scoreCandidate(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
  interpretation:
    InputInterpretation,
  evidence:
    ModelRoutingEvidence,
  constraints:
    ModelRoutingConstraints,
): RankedModelCandidate {
  const exclusions =
    eligibility(
      candidate,
      interpretation,
      constraints,
    );

  const weights =
    dynamicWeights(
      interpretation,
    );

  const calibration =
    evidence
      .objectiveCalibration
      ?.[candidate.id];

  const preference =
    evidence
      .subjectivePreference
      ?.[candidate.id];

  const taskFit =
    taskFitScore(
      candidate,
      interpretation,
    );

  const privacy =
    privacyScore(
      candidate,
      interpretation,
    );

  const latency =
    latencyScore(
      candidate,
      interpretation,
      evidence,
      constraints,
    );

  const cost =
    costScore(
      candidate,
      interpretation,
      constraints,
    );

  const reliability =
    reliabilityScore(
      candidate,
      evidence,
    );

  const components: ModelRouteScoreComponent[] =
    [
      component(
        "objective-calibration",
        calibration
          ?.score ??
        0.5,
        weights[
          "objective-calibration"
        ],
        calibration
          ?.confidence ??
        0.2,
        calibration
          ? "measured"
          : "unknown",
        calibration
          ? [
              `Objective calibration score=${clamp01(calibration.score).toFixed(2)} sampleCount=${calibration.sampleCount ?? "unknown"}.`,
            ]
          : [
              "No objective calibration evidence; neutral score used rather than inventing capability.",
            ],
      ),
      component(
        "task-fit",
        taskFit.score,
        weights[
          "task-fit"
        ],
        0.6,
        "derived",
        taskFit.reasons,
      ),
      component(
        "privacy",
        privacy.score,
        weights.privacy,
        candidate.privacy
          .execution !==
          "unknown"
          ? 0.9
          : 0.4,
        "declared",
        privacy.reasons,
      ),
      component(
        "latency",
        latency.score,
        weights.latency,
        latency.confidence,
        latency.measured
          ? "measured"
          : "unknown",
        latency.reasons,
      ),
      component(
        "cost",
        cost.score,
        weights.cost,
        candidate.cost
          .known ||
        candidate.cost.mode ===
          "local-compute"
          ? 0.9
          : 0.3,
        candidate.cost
          .known ||
        candidate.cost.mode ===
          "local-compute"
          ? "declared"
          : "unknown",
        cost.reasons,
      ),
      component(
        "availability",
        availabilityScore(
          candidate,
        ),
        weights.availability,
        candidate.availability ===
          "unknown"
          ? 0.4
          : 0.95,
        "declared",
        [
          `Registry availability=${candidate.availability}.`,
        ],
      ),
      component(
        "subjective-preference",
        preference
          ?.score ??
        0.5,
        weights[
          "subjective-preference"
        ],
        preference
          ?.confidence ??
        0.2,
        preference
          ? "measured"
          : "unknown",
        preference
          ? [
              `Subjective preference score=${clamp01(preference.score).toFixed(2)} sampleCount=${preference.sampleCount ?? "unknown"}.`,
              "Preference is intentionally separate from objective capability.",
            ]
          : [
              "No subjective preference evidence; neutral score used.",
            ],
      ),
      component(
        "reliability",
        reliability.score,
        weights.reliability,
        reliability.confidence,
        reliability.measured
          ? "measured"
          : "unknown",
        reliability.reasons,
      ),
    ];

  const score =
    exclusions.length >
      0
      ? 0
      : clamp01(
          components.reduce(
            (
              total,
              entry,
            ) =>
              total +
              entry.weightedScore,
            0,
          ),
        );

  const evidenceWeight =
    components.reduce(
      (
        total,
        entry,
      ) =>
        total +
        entry.weight,
      0,
    );

  const confidence =
    exclusions.length >
      0
      ? 1
      : clamp01(
          evidenceWeight >
            0
            ? components.reduce(
                (
                  total,
                  entry,
                ) =>
                  total +
                  entry.confidence *
                    entry.weight,
                0,
              ) /
              evidenceWeight
            : 0,
        );

  const reasons = [
    ...components.flatMap(
      (entry) =>
        entry.reasons.map(
          (reason) =>
            `${entry.component}: ${reason}`,
        ),
    ),
  ];

  return {
    candidate:
      structuredClone(
        candidate,
      ),
    eligible:
      exclusions.length ===
      0,
    exclusionReasons:
      exclusions,
    score,
    confidence,
    components,
    reasons,
  };
}

export class ModelRouter {
  constructor(
    private readonly candidateSource:
      CognitiveModelCandidateSource =
        defaultCandidateSource,
  ) {}

  async route(
    input: {
      interpretation:
        InputInterpretation;
      evidence?:
        ModelRoutingEvidence;
      constraints?:
        ModelRoutingConstraints;
    },
  ): Promise<ModelRouteDecision> {
    const evidence =
      input.evidence ??
      {};

    const constraints =
      structuredClone(
        input.constraints ??
        {},
      );

    const candidates =
      await this.candidateSource();

    const ranking =
      candidates
        .map(
          (candidate) =>
            scoreCandidate(
              candidate,
              input.interpretation,
              evidence,
              constraints,
            ),
        )
        .sort(
          (
            left,
            right,
          ) => {
            if (
              left.eligible !==
              right.eligible
            ) {
              return left.eligible
                ? -1
                : 1;
            }

            if (
              left.score !==
              right.score
            ) {
              return (
                right.score -
                left.score
              );
            }

            if (
              left.confidence !==
              right.confidence
            ) {
              return (
                right.confidence -
                left.confidence
              );
            }

            return left
              .candidate.id
              .localeCompare(
                right
                  .candidate.id,
              );
          },
        );

    const minimum =
      clamp01(
        constraints
          .minimumRouteConfidence ??
        0,
      );

    const selected =
      ranking.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.eligible &&
          entry.confidence >=
            minimum,
      );

    const warnings:
      string[] =
        [];

    if (
      candidates.length ===
      0
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Model registry returned no candidates.",
      );
    }

    if (
      ranking.some(
        (entry) =>
          entry.components.some(
            (componentScore) =>
              componentScore
                .evidence ===
              "unknown",
          ),
      )
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "One or more routing dimensions lacked measured/declared evidence; neutral conservative scores were used.",
      );
    }

    if (!selected) {
      warnings.push(
        "No eligible model satisfied the routing constraints/confidence threshold.",
      );
    }

    return {
      version:
        MODEL_ROUTER_VERSION,
      routedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      status:
        selected
          ? "selected"
          : "no-eligible-candidate",
      selected:
        selected
          ? structuredClone(
              selected,
            )
          : undefined,
      ranking,
      taskSummary: {
        primaryDomain:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .primaryDomain
            .value,
        complexity:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .complexity.value,
        creativity:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .creativity.value,
        factuality:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .factuality.value,
        reasoningDepth:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .reasoningDepth.value,
        privacySensitivity:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .privacySensitivity
            .value,
        latencyTolerance:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .latencyTolerance
            .value,
        costTolerance:
          input
            .interpretation
            .taskIntelligence
            .costTolerance.value,
        requiredTools: [
          ...(
            input
              .interpretation
              .taskSignals
              .requiredTools ??
            []
          ),
        ],
      },
      constraints,
      warnings,
    };
  }
}

const defaultModelRouter =
  new ModelRouter();

export function getModelRouter():
  ModelRouter {
  return defaultModelRouter;
}

FILE: packages\models\src\strategyRouter.ts
