import type {
  InputInterpretation,
} from "./inputInterpreter";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const GOAL_DECOMPOSER_VERSION =
  "oh4p.v1";

export type GoalNodeKind =
  | "root"
  | "subgoal"
  | "verification"
  | "evidence"
  | "integration";

export type GoalNodeStatus =
  | "planned"
  | "blocked"
  | "ready";

export interface GoalAcceptanceCriterion {
  id: string;
  description: string;
  required: boolean;
}

export interface GoalEvidenceNeed {
  kind:
    | "context"
    | "file"
    | "tool"
    | "external"
    | "validation";

  description: string;
  required: boolean;
}

export interface GoalNode {
  id: string;

  parentId?: string;

  kind: GoalNodeKind;
  title: string;
  objective: string;

  status: GoalNodeStatus;

  depth: number;

  dependsOn: string[];

  acceptanceCriteria:
    GoalAcceptanceCriterion[];

  evidenceNeeds:
    GoalEvidenceNeed[];

  requiredTools: string[];

  assumptions: string[];
  blockers: string[];

  provenance: {
    source:
      | "interpreted-goal"
      | "reasoning-contract"
      | "decomposer";
    sourceRef: string;
  };
}

export interface GoalDependencyEdge {
  fromGoalId: string;
  toGoalId: string;

  kind:
    | "requires"
    | "verifies"
    | "integrates";

  reason: string;
}

export interface GoalDecompositionDraftNode {
  id?: string;

  parentId?: string;

  kind?: GoalNodeKind;

  title: string;
  objective: string;

  dependsOn?: string[];

  acceptanceCriteria?: Array<
    | string
    | {
        description: string;
        required?: boolean;
      }
  >;

  evidenceNeeds?: Array<
    | string
    | {
        kind?:
          GoalEvidenceNeed["kind"];
        description: string;
        required?: boolean;
      }
  >;

  requiredTools?: string[];

  assumptions?: string[];
  blockers?: string[];
}

export interface GoalDecompositionDraft {
  rootTitle?: string;
  rootObjective?: string;

  nodes:
    GoalDecompositionDraftNode[];

  notes?: string[];
}

export interface GoalDecomposition {
  version: string;
  generatedAt: string;

  rootGoalId: string;

  nodes: GoalNode[];
  edges: GoalDependencyEdge[];

  maxDepth: number;

  complete: boolean;
  planningReady: boolean;

  unresolvedQuestions: Array<{
    question: string;
    blocking: boolean;
    reason: string;
  }>;

  warnings: string[];
}

export type GoalDecompositionAnalyzer =
  (
    input: {
      interpretation:
        InputInterpretation;
      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;
    },
  ) =>
    GoalDecompositionDraft
    | Promise<GoalDecompositionDraft>;

function normalizeText(
  value: string,
): string {
  return value
    .replace(
      /\s+/g,
      " ",
    )
    .trim();
}

function requireText(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    normalizeText(
      value,
    );

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function uniqueText(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (values ?? [])
        .map(
          normalizeText,
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function slug(
  value: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /[^a-z0-9]+/g,
        "-",
      )
      .replace(
        /^-+|-+$/g,
        "",
      )
      .slice(
        0,
        48,
      );

  return normalized ||
    "goal";
}

function defaultDraft(
  input: {
    interpretation:
      InputInterpretation;
    reasoningContract:
      ReasoningContract;
  },
): GoalDecompositionDraft {
  const interpretation =
    input.interpretation;

  const contract =
    input.reasoningContract;

  const tools =
    interpretation
      .taskSignals
      .requiredTools ??
    [];

  const nodes:
    GoalDecompositionDraftNode[] =
      [];

  nodes.push({
    id:
      "understand-requirements",
    title:
      "Establish requirements",
    objective:
      "Convert the interpreted goal, desired outcome, and explicit constraints into concrete success requirements.",
    acceptanceCriteria: [
      "Goal and desired outcome are represented.",
      "Explicit constraints are preserved.",
      "Blocking ambiguity is not silently resolved.",
    ],
    evidenceNeeds: [
      {
        kind:
          "context",
        description:
          "Use the interpreted task contract as the source of requirements.",
        required:
          true,
      },
    ],
  });

  if (
    contract.evidence
      .requirement !==
    "none"
  ) {
    nodes.push({
      id:
        "secure-evidence",
      title:
        "Secure required evidence",
      objective:
        "Identify and obtain the evidence required by the active reasoning contract before relying on material factual conclusions.",
      dependsOn: [
        "understand-requirements",
      ],
      acceptanceCriteria: [
        `Evidence requirement ${contract.evidence.requirement} is satisfied or explicitly reported missing.`,
      ],
      evidenceNeeds: [
        {
          kind:
            contract.execution
              .allowTools
              ? "tool"
              : "context",
          description:
            "Evidence must preserve provenance, freshness, and confidence where available.",
          required:
            true,
        },
      ],
      requiredTools:
        tools,
    });
  }

  nodes.push({
    id:
      "produce-result",
    title:
      "Produce candidate result",
    objective:
      "Create a complete candidate that satisfies the interpreted goal under the active reasoning contract.",
    dependsOn: [
      contract.evidence
        .requirement !==
        "none"
        ? "secure-evidence"
        : "understand-requirements",
    ],
    acceptanceCriteria: [
      interpretation
        .desiredOutcome
        .summary,
      "Explicit constraints are satisfied.",
      "Assumptions and uncertainties remain visible.",
    ],
    requiredTools:
      contract.execution
        .allowTools
        ? tools
        : [],
  });

  if (
    contract.validation
      .required
  ) {
    nodes.push({
      id:
        "validate-result",
      kind:
        "verification",
      title:
        "Validate candidate result",
      objective:
        "Validate the candidate against constraints, evidence requirements, factuality requirements, and the expected output contract.",
      dependsOn: [
        "produce-result",
      ],
      acceptanceCriteria: [
        `Validation mode ${contract.validation.mode} completes.`,
        "Validation failures remain visible.",
      ],
      evidenceNeeds: [
        {
          kind:
            "validation",
          description:
            "Validation result must be retained as an auditable artifact.",
          required:
            true,
        },
      ],
    });
  }

  nodes.push({
    id:
      "finalize-outcome",
    kind:
      "integration",
    title:
      "Finalize outcome",
    objective:
      "Assemble the accepted result, evidence references, confidence, assumptions, uncertainties, validation results, and any non-executable proposed actions.",
    dependsOn: [
      contract.validation
        .required
        ? "validate-result"
        : "produce-result",
    ],
    acceptanceCriteria: [
      "Result satisfies the desired outcome.",
      "Confidence is recorded.",
      "Proposed actions remain non-executable.",
    ],
  });

  return {
    rootTitle:
      interpretation
        .goal.summary,
    rootObjective:
      interpretation
        .desiredOutcome
        .summary,
    nodes,
    notes: [
      "Default decomposition is deterministic and contract-derived.",
    ],
  };
}

function normalizedAcceptanceCriteria(
  nodeId: string,
  values:
    GoalDecompositionDraftNode[
      "acceptanceCriteria"
    ],
): GoalAcceptanceCriterion[] {
  return (
    values ??
    []
  )
    .map(
      (
        value,
        index,
      ) => {
        if (
          typeof value ===
          "string"
        ) {
          return {
            id:
              `${nodeId}:criterion:${index + 1}`,
            description:
              requireText(
                value,
                "acceptance criterion",
              ),
            required:
              true,
          };
        }

        return {
          id:
            `${nodeId}:criterion:${index + 1}`,
          description:
            requireText(
              value.description,
              "acceptance criterion",
            ),
          required:
            value.required ??
            true,
        };
      },
    );
}

function normalizedEvidenceNeeds(
  values:
    GoalDecompositionDraftNode[
      "evidenceNeeds"
    ],
): GoalEvidenceNeed[] {
  return (
    values ??
    []
  )
    .map(
      (value) => {
        if (
          typeof value ===
          "string"
        ) {
          return {
            kind:
              "context" as const,
            description:
              requireText(
                value,
                "evidence need",
              ),
            required:
              true,
          };
        }

        return {
          kind:
            value.kind ??
            "context",
          description:
            requireText(
              value.description,
              "evidence need",
            ),
          required:
            value.required ??
            true,
        };
      },
    );
}

function validateAcyclic(
  nodes:
    readonly GoalNode[],
): void {
  const dependencies =
    new Map<
      string,
      string[]
    >(
      nodes.map(
        (node) => [
          node.id,
          node.dependsOn,
        ],
      ),
    );

  const visiting =
    new Set<string>();

  const visited =
    new Set<string>();

  const visit =
    (
      id: string,
    ): void => {
      if (
        visited.has(
          id,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (
        visiting.has(
          id,
        )
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Goal decomposition contains a dependency cycle involving ${id}.`,
        );
      }

      visiting.add(
        id,
      );

      for (
        const dependency
        of dependencies.get(
          id,
        ) ??
        []
      ) {
        visit(
          dependency,
        );
      }

      visiting.delete(
        id,
      );

      visited.add(
        id,
      );
    };

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    visit(
      node.id,
    );
  }
}

function calculateDepths(
  rootId: string,
  nodes:
    GoalNode[],
): void {
  const byId =
    new Map(
      nodes.map(
        (node) => [
          node.id,
          node,
        ],
      ),
    );

  const depthOf =
    (
      node:
        GoalNode,
      stack =
        new Set<string>(),
    ): number => {
      if (
        node.id ===
        rootId
      ) {
        return 0;
      }

      if (
        stack.has(
          node.id,
        )
      ) {
        return node.depth;
      }

      stack.add(
        node.id,
      );

      const parent =
        node.parentId
          ? byId.get(
              node.parentId,
            )
          : undefined;

      const depth =
        parent
          ? depthOf(
              parent,
              stack,
            ) +
            1
          : 1;

      stack.delete(
        node.id,
      );

      return depth;
    };

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    node.depth =
      depthOf(
        node,
      );
  }
}

function buildEdges(
  nodes:
    readonly GoalNode[],
): GoalDependencyEdge[] {
  const edges:
    GoalDependencyEdge[] =
      [];

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    for (
      const dependency
      of node.dependsOn
    ) {
      edges.push({
        fromGoalId:
          dependency,
        toGoalId:
          node.id,
        kind:
          node.kind ===
            "verification"
            ? "verifies"
            : node.kind ===
                "integration"
              ? "integrates"
              : "requires",
        reason:
          `${node.title} depends on ${dependency}.`,
      });
    }
  }

  return edges;
}

function normalizeDraft(
  input: {
    interpretation:
      InputInterpretation;
    reasoningContract:
      ReasoningContract;
    draft:
      GoalDecompositionDraft;
  },
): GoalDecomposition {
  const rootGoalId =
    `root:${slug(
      input.interpretation
        .goal.summary,
    )}`;

  const rootTitle =
    requireText(
      input.draft
        .rootTitle ??
      input.interpretation
        .goal.summary,
      "root goal title",
    );

  const rootObjective =
    requireText(
      input.draft
        .rootObjective ??
      input.interpretation
        .desiredOutcome
        .summary,
      "root goal objective",
    );

  const nodes:
    GoalNode[] =
      [
        {
          id:
            rootGoalId,
          kind:
            "root",
          title:
            rootTitle,
          objective:
            rootObjective,
          status:
            input.interpretation
              .clarificationRequired
              ? "blocked"
              : "ready",
          depth:
            0,
          dependsOn:
            [],
          acceptanceCriteria: [
            {
              id:
                `${rootGoalId}:criterion:1`,
              description:
                input.interpretation
                  .desiredOutcome
                  .summary,
              required:
                true,
            },
          ],
          evidenceNeeds:
            [],
          requiredTools:
            [],
          assumptions:
            [],
          blockers:
            input.interpretation
              .unresolvedQuestions
              .filter(
                (question) =>
                  question.blocking,
              )
              .map(
                (question) =>
                  question.question,
              ),
          provenance: {
            source:
              "interpreted-goal",
            sourceRef:
              "oh4j.goal",
          },
        },
      ];

  const assignedIds =
    new Set<string>([
      rootGoalId,
    ]);

  const idByDraftId =
    new Map<
      string,
      string
    >();

  input.draft.nodes.forEach(
    (
      draftNode,
      index,
    ) => {
      const requested =
        normalizeText(
          draftNode.id ??
          "",
        );

      let id =
        requested ||
        `goal:${index + 1}:${slug(
          draftNode.title,
        )}`;

      if (
        assignedIds.has(
          id,
        )
      ) {
        let suffix =
          2;

        while (
          assignedIds.has(
            `${id}:${suffix}`,
          )
        ) {
          suffix +=
            1;
        }

        id =
          `${id}:${suffix}`;
      }

      assignedIds.add(
        id,
      );

      if (
        requested
      ) {
        idByDraftId.set(
          requested,
          id,
        );
      }

      idByDraftId.set(
        `#${index}`,
        id,
      );
    },
  );

  const resolveRef =
    (
      value:
        string
        | undefined,
    ): string | undefined => {
      if (!value) {
        return undefined;
      }

      if (
        value ===
        "root"
      ) {
        return rootGoalId;
      }

      return idByDraftId.get(
        value,
      ) ??
      value;
    };

  input.draft.nodes.forEach(
    (
      draftNode,
      index,
    ) => {
      const id =
        idByDraftId.get(
          `#${index}`,
        );

      if (!id) {
        throw new Error(
          "Internal goal-id assignment failed.",
        );
      }

      const parentId =
        resolveRef(
          draftNode.parentId,
        ) ??
        rootGoalId;

      const dependsOn =
        uniqueText(
          (
            draftNode.dependsOn ??
            []
          )
            .map(
              (dependency) =>
                resolveRef(
                  dependency,
                ) ??
                dependency,
            ),
        );

      const blockers =
        uniqueText(
          draftNode.blockers,
        );

      nodes.push({
        id,
        parentId,
        kind:
          draftNode.kind ??
          "subgoal",
        title:
          requireText(
            draftNode.title,
            "goal title",
          ),
        objective:
          requireText(
            draftNode.objective,
            "goal objective",
          ),
        status:
          blockers.length >
          0
            ? "blocked"
            : dependsOn.length >
                0
              ? "planned"
              : "ready",
        depth:
          1,
        dependsOn,
        acceptanceCriteria:
          normalizedAcceptanceCriteria(
            id,
            draftNode
              .acceptanceCriteria,
          ),
        evidenceNeeds:
          normalizedEvidenceNeeds(
            draftNode
              .evidenceNeeds,
          ),
        requiredTools:
          uniqueText(
            draftNode
              .requiredTools,
          ),
        assumptions:
          uniqueText(
            draftNode
              .assumptions,
          ),
        blockers,
        provenance: {
          source:
            "decomposer",
          sourceRef:
            `oh4p.node.${index + 1}`,
        },
      });
    },
  );

  const nodeIds =
    new Set(
      nodes.map(
        (node) =>
          node.id,
      ),
    );

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    if (
      node.parentId &&
      !nodeIds.has(
        node.parentId,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Goal ${node.id} references unknown parent ${node.parentId}.`,
      );
    }

    for (
      const dependency
      of node.dependsOn
    ) {
      if (
        !nodeIds.has(
          dependency,
        )
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Goal ${node.id} depends on unknown goal ${dependency}.`,
        );
      }

      if (
        dependency ===
        node.id
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Goal ${node.id} cannot depend on itself.`,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  validateAcyclic(
    nodes,
  );

  calculateDepths(
    rootGoalId,
    nodes,
  );

  const maxDepth =
    nodes.reduce(
      (
        maximum,
        node,
      ) =>
        Math.max(
          maximum,
          node.depth,
        ),
      0,
    );

  const warnings =
    uniqueText([
      ...(input.draft
        .notes ??
        []),
      ...(
        input.interpretation
          .clarificationRequired
          ? [
              "Blocking unresolved questions prevent planning-ready decomposition.",
            ]
          : []
      ),
      ...(
        nodes.some(
          (node) =>
            node.acceptanceCriteria
              .length ===
            0,
        )
          ? [
              "One or more subgoals lack explicit acceptance criteria.",
            ]
          : []
      ),
    ]);

  const blockersPresent =
    nodes.some(
      (node) =>
        node.blockers
          .length >
        0,
    );

  const missingAcceptance =
    nodes
      .filter(
        (node) =>
          node.kind !==
          "root",
      )
      .some(
        (node) =>
          node.acceptanceCriteria
            .length ===
          0,
      );

  const complete =
    !input.interpretation
      .clarificationRequired &&
    !blockersPresent;

  return {
    version:
      GOAL_DECOMPOSER_VERSION,
    generatedAt:
      new Date()
        .toISOString(),
    rootGoalId,
    nodes,
    edges:
      buildEdges(
        nodes,
      ),
    maxDepth,
    complete,
    planningReady:
      complete &&
      !missingAcceptance,
    unresolvedQuestions:
      input.interpretation
        .unresolvedQuestions
        .map(
          (question) => ({
            question:
              question.question,
            blocking:
              question.blocking,
            reason:
              question.reason,
          }),
        ),
    warnings,
  };
}

export class GoalDecomposer {
  constructor(
    private readonly analyzer:
      GoalDecompositionAnalyzer =
        defaultDraft,
  ) {}

  async decompose(
    input: {
      interpretation:
        InputInterpretation;
      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;
    },
  ): Promise<GoalDecomposition> {
    if (
      input.reasoningContract
        .inputs
        .blockingQuestionsMustStop &&
      input.interpretation
        .clarificationRequired
    ) {
      const blockedDraft:
        GoalDecompositionDraft = {
          rootTitle:
            input.interpretation
              .goal.summary,
          rootObjective:
            input.interpretation
              .desiredOutcome
              .summary,
          nodes:
            [],
          notes: [
            "Decomposition stopped because the reasoning contract forbids guessing through blocking ambiguity.",
          ],
        };

      return normalizeDraft({
        ...input,
        draft:
          blockedDraft,
      });
    }

    const draft =
      await this.analyzer(
        input,
      );

    if (
      !draft ||
      !Array.isArray(
        draft.nodes,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "Goal decomposition analyzer must return a nodes array.",
      );
    }

    return normalizeDraft({
      ...input,
      draft,
    });
  }
}

const defaultGoalDecomposer =
  new GoalDecomposer();

export function getGoalDecomposer():
  GoalDecomposer {
  return defaultGoalDecomposer;
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\cognitivePlanningEngine.ts

import type {
  GoalDecomposition,
  GoalEvidenceNeed,
  GoalNode,
} from "./goalDecomposer";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const COGNITIVE_PLANNING_ENGINE_VERSION =
  "oh4q.v1";

export type CognitivePlanStatus =
  | "ready"
  | "blocked";

export type CognitivePlanStepReadiness =
  | "ready"
  | "waiting"
  | "blocked";

export type CognitivePlanStepKind =
  | "prepare"
  | "work"
  | "evidence"
  | "verification"
  | "integration";

export interface CognitivePlanVerificationCheckpoint {
  required: boolean;

  mode:
    ReasoningContract[
      "validation"
    ]["mode"];

  checks: string[];

  failureBehavior:
    ReasoningContract[
      "validation"
    ]["failureBehavior"];
}

export interface CognitivePlanStep {
  id: string;

  sequence: number;
  stage: number;

  goalId: string;

  kind:
    CognitivePlanStepKind;

  title: string;
  objective: string;

  readiness:
    CognitivePlanStepReadiness;

  dependsOnStepIds:
    string[];

  acceptanceCriteria:
    string[];

  evidenceNeeds:
    GoalEvidenceNeed[];

  requiredTools:
    string[];

  assumptions:
    string[];

  blockers:
    string[];

  verification:
    CognitivePlanVerificationCheckpoint;

  mayExecute: false;
  requiresAuthorityBeforeExecution: true;

  provenance: {
    goalNodeId: string;
    goalSourceRef: string;
    planningEngineVersion: string;
  };
}

export interface CognitivePlanStage {
  stage: number;
  stepIds: string[];
  parallelizable: boolean;
}

export interface CognitivePlan {
  version: string;
  generatedAt: string;

  status:
    CognitivePlanStatus;

  rootGoalId: string;
  goal: string;
  desiredOutcome: string;

  strategy:
    ReasoningContract[
      "strategy"
    ];

  steps:
    CognitivePlanStep[];

  stages:
    CognitivePlanStage[];

  planningReady: boolean;

  unresolvedQuestions:
    GoalDecomposition[
      "unresolvedQuestions"
    ];

  warnings: string[];

  invariants: {
    dependencyOrderValidated: true;
    schedulingPerformed: false;
    priorityAssigned: false;
    actionsExecutable: false;
    authorityBypassed: false;
  };
}

function uniqueText(
  values:
    readonly string[],
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      values
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function stepIdForGoal(
  goalId: string,
): string {
  return `plan:${goalId}`;
}

function kindForNode(
  node: GoalNode,
): CognitivePlanStepKind {
  if (
    node.kind ===
      "verification"
  ) {
    return "verification";
  }

  if (
    node.kind ===
      "integration"
  ) {
    return "integration";
  }

  if (
    node.evidenceNeeds
      .some(
        (need) =>
          need.required,
      )
  ) {
    return "evidence";
  }

  if (
    node.title
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(
        "requirement",
      )
  ) {
    return "prepare";
  }

  return "work";
}

function validateDependencyGraph(
  decomposition:
    GoalDecomposition,
): void {
  const nodes =
    decomposition.nodes
      .filter(
        (node) =>
          node.kind !==
          "root",
      );

  const ids =
    new Set(
      nodes.map(
        (node) =>
          node.id,
      ),
    );

  const rootId =
    decomposition.rootGoalId;

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    for (
      const dependency
      of node.dependsOn
    ) {
      if (
        dependency ===
        rootId
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      if (
        !ids.has(
          dependency,
        )
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Planning graph references unknown dependency ${dependency} from ${node.id}.`,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  const visiting =
    new Set<string>();

  const visited =
    new Set<string>();

  const byId =
    new Map(
      nodes.map(
        (node) => [
          node.id,
          node,
        ],
      ),
    );

  const visit =
    (
      id: string,
    ): void => {
      if (
        visited.has(
          id,
        )
      ) {
        return;
      }

      if (
        visiting.has(
          id,
        )
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Planning graph contains a dependency cycle involving ${id}.`,
        );
      }

      visiting.add(
        id,
      );

      const node =
        byId.get(
          id,
        );

      for (
        const dependency
        of node?.dependsOn ??
        []
      ) {
        if (
          dependency !==
          rootId
        ) {
          visit(
            dependency,
          );
        }
      }

      visiting.delete(
        id,
      );

      visited.add(
        id,
      );
    };

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    visit(
      node.id,
    );
  }
}

function topologicalOrder(
  decomposition:
    GoalDecomposition,
): GoalNode[] {
  validateDependencyGraph(
    decomposition,
  );

  const nodes =
    decomposition.nodes
      .filter(
        (node) =>
          node.kind !==
          "root",
      );

  const rootId =
    decomposition.rootGoalId;

  const byId =
    new Map(
      nodes.map(
        (node) => [
          node.id,
          node,
        ],
      ),
    );

  const indegree =
    new Map<
      string,
      number
    >();

  const outgoing =
    new Map<
      string,
      string[]
    >();

  for (
    const node
    of nodes
  ) {
    const dependencies =
      node.dependsOn
        .filter(
          (dependency) =>
            dependency !==
            rootId,
        );

    indegree.set(
      node.id,
      dependencies.length,
    );

    for (
      const dependency
      of dependencies
    ) {
      const list =
        outgoing.get(
          dependency,
        ) ??
        [];

      list.push(
        node.id,
      );

      outgoing.set(
        dependency,
        list,
      );
    }
  }

  const ready =
    nodes
      .filter(
        (node) =>
          (
            indegree.get(
              node.id,
            ) ??
            0
          ) ===
          0,
      )
      .sort(
        (
          left,
          right,
        ) => {
          if (
            left.depth !==
            right.depth
          ) {
            return (
              left.depth -
              right.depth
            );
          }

          return left.id
            .localeCompare(
              right.id,
            );
        },
      );

  const ordered:
    GoalNode[] =
      [];

  while (
    ready.length >
    0
  ) {
    const current =
      ready.shift();

    if (!current) {
      break;
    }

    ordered.push(
      current,
    );

    for (
      const childId
      of (
        outgoing.get(
          current.id,
        ) ??
        []
      ).sort()
    ) {
      const next =
        (
          indegree.get(
            childId,
          ) ??
          0
        ) -
        1;

      indegree.set(
        childId,
        next,
      );

      if (
        next ===
        0
      ) {
        const child =
          byId.get(
            childId,
          );

        if (child) {
          ready.push(
            child,
          );

          ready.sort(
            (
              left,
              right,
            ) => {
              if (
                left.depth !==
                right.depth
              ) {
                return (
                  left.depth -
                  right.depth
                );
              }

              return left.id
                .localeCompare(
                  right.id,
                );
            },
          );
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (
    ordered.length !==
    nodes.length
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Planning graph could not be topologically ordered.",
    );
  }

  return ordered;
}

function stageMap(
  ordered:
    readonly GoalNode[],
  rootGoalId:
    string,
): Map<string, number> {
  const stages =
    new Map<
      string,
      number
    >();

  for (
    const node
    of ordered
  ) {
    const dependencyStages =
      node.dependsOn
        .filter(
          (dependency) =>
            dependency !==
            rootGoalId,
        )
        .map(
          (dependency) =>
            stages.get(
              dependency,
            ) ??
            0,
        );

    stages.set(
      node.id,
      dependencyStages.length >
        0
        ? Math.max(
            ...dependencyStages,
          ) +
          1
        : 1,
    );
  }

  return stages;
}

function verificationFor(
  node:
    GoalNode,
  contract:
    ReasoningContract,
): CognitivePlanVerificationCheckpoint {
  const nodeIsVerification =
    node.kind ===
    "verification";

  const required =
    nodeIsVerification ||
    contract.validation
      .required;

  const checks =
    uniqueText([
      ...node
        .acceptanceCriteria
        .filter(
          (criterion) =>
            criterion.required,
        )
        .map(
          (criterion) =>
            criterion.description,
        ),
      ...(
        contract.validation
          .validateConstraints
          ? [
              "Verify explicit constraints remain satisfied.",
            ]
          : []
      ),
      ...(
        contract.validation
          .validateEvidence
          ? [
              "Verify required evidence is present and provenance-bearing.",
            ]
          : []
      ),
      ...(
        contract.validation
          .validateFactuality
          ? [
              "Verify material factual claims meet the active factuality contract.",
            ]
          : []
      ),
    ]);

  return {
    required,
    mode:
      contract.validation
        .mode,
    checks,
    failureBehavior:
      contract.validation
        .failureBehavior,
  };
}

function readinessFor(
  node:
    GoalNode,
  dependencyStepIds:
    readonly string[],
  planBlocked:
    boolean,
): CognitivePlanStepReadiness {
  if (
    planBlocked ||
    node.status ===
      "blocked" ||
    node.blockers.length >
      0
  ) {
    return "blocked";
  }

  if (
    dependencyStepIds.length >
    0
  ) {
    return "waiting";
  }

  return "ready";
}

function buildStages(
  steps:
    readonly CognitivePlanStep[],
): CognitivePlanStage[] {
  const stageNumbers =
    [
      ...new Set(
        steps.map(
          (step) =>
            step.stage,
        ),
      ),
    ].sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) =>
        left -
        right,
    );

  return stageNumbers.map(
    (stage) => {
      const stepIds =
        steps
          .filter(
            (step) =>
              step.stage ===
              stage,
          )
          .map(
            (step) =>
              step.id,
          );

      return {
        stage,
        stepIds,
        parallelizable:
          stepIds.length >
          1,
      };
    },
  );
}

export class CognitivePlanningEngine {
  plan(
    input: {
      decomposition:
        GoalDecomposition;
      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;
    },
  ): CognitivePlan {
    const decomposition =
      input.decomposition;

    const contract =
      input.reasoningContract;

    if (
      decomposition.version
        .trim()
        .length ===
      0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "OH-4Q requires a versioned OH-4P decomposition.",
      );
    }

    if (
      contract.version
        .trim()
        .length ===
      0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "OH-4Q requires a versioned OH-4O reasoning contract.",
      );
    }

    const root =
      decomposition.nodes
        .find(
          (node) =>
            node.id ===
            decomposition
              .rootGoalId,
        );

    if (
      !root ||
      root.kind !==
        "root"
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "OH-4Q requires a valid OH-4P root goal.",
      );
    }

    const ordered =
      topologicalOrder(
        decomposition,
      );

    const stages =
      stageMap(
        ordered,
        decomposition
          .rootGoalId,
      );

    const planBlocked =
      !decomposition
        .planningReady ||
      decomposition
        .unresolvedQuestions
        .some(
          (question) =>
            question.blocking,
        );

    const steps =
      ordered.map(
        (
          node,
          index,
        ): CognitivePlanStep => {
          const dependsOnStepIds =
            node.dependsOn
              .filter(
                (dependency) =>
                  dependency !==
                  decomposition
                    .rootGoalId,
              )
              .map(
                stepIdForGoal,
              );

          return {
            id:
              stepIdForGoal(
                node.id,
              ),
            sequence:
              index +
              1,
            stage:
              stages.get(
                node.id,
              ) ??
              1,
            goalId:
              node.id,
            kind:
              kindForNode(
                node,
              ),
            title:
              node.title,
            objective:
              node.objective,
            readiness:
              readinessFor(
                node,
                dependsOnStepIds,
                planBlocked,
              ),
            dependsOnStepIds,
            acceptanceCriteria:
              node
                .acceptanceCriteria
                .map(
                  (criterion) =>
                    criterion.description,
                ),
            evidenceNeeds:
              structuredClone(
                node.evidenceNeeds,
              ),
            requiredTools: [
              ...node
                .requiredTools,
            ],
            assumptions: [
              ...node
                .assumptions,
            ],
            blockers: [
              ...node
                .blockers,
            ],
            verification:
              verificationFor(
                node,
                contract,
              ),
            mayExecute:
              false,
            requiresAuthorityBeforeExecution:
              true,
            provenance: {
              goalNodeId:
                node.id,
              goalSourceRef:
                node.provenance
                  .sourceRef,
              planningEngineVersion:
                COGNITIVE_PLANNING_ENGINE_VERSION,
            },
          };
        },
      );

    const warnings =
      uniqueText([
        ...decomposition
          .warnings,
        ...contract
          .warnings,
        ...(
          planBlocked
            ? [
                "Plan is blocked because the goal decomposition is not planning-ready or contains blocking unresolved questions.",
              ]
            : []
        ),
        ...(
          contract.execution
            .allowTools
            ? [
                "Plan may reference tools, but OH-4Q does not execute tools.",
              ]
            : []
        ),
        "Plan order is dependency-derived only; OH-4Q does not assign runtime priority or schedule time.",
      ]);

    return {
      version:
        COGNITIVE_PLANNING_ENGINE_VERSION,
      generatedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      status:
        planBlocked
          ? "blocked"
          : "ready",
      rootGoalId:
        decomposition
          .rootGoalId,
      goal:
        root.title,
      desiredOutcome:
        root.objective,
      strategy:
        contract.strategy,
      steps,
      stages:
        buildStages(
          steps,
        ),
      planningReady:
        !planBlocked,
      unresolvedQuestions:
        structuredClone(
          decomposition
            .unresolvedQuestions,
        ),
      warnings,
      invariants: {
        dependencyOrderValidated:
          true,
        schedulingPerformed:
          false,
        priorityAssigned:
          false,
        actionsExecutable:
          false,
        authorityBypassed:
          false,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultCognitivePlanningEngine =
  new CognitivePlanningEngine();

export function getCognitivePlanningEngine():
  CognitivePlanningEngine {
  return defaultCognitivePlanningEngine;
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\multiModelOrchestrator.ts
============================================================
import type {
  CompiledPrompt,
} from "./promptCompiler";

import type {
  ModelRouteDecision,
  ModelRoutingCandidate,
  RankedModelCandidate,
} from "./modelDecisionRouter";

import type {
  CognitiveStrategyKind,
  StrategyRouteDecision,
} from "./strategyRouter";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const MULTI_MODEL_ORCHESTRATOR_VERSION =
  "oh4s.v1";

export type CognitiveModelPassKind =
  | "primary"
  | "secondary"
  | "critic"
  | "refinement"
  | "fallback";

export interface CognitiveModelExecutionRequest {
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate;

  pass:
    CognitiveModelPassKind;

  purpose: string;

  prompt: string;

  timeoutMs?: number;
}

export interface CognitiveModelExecutionResult {
  ok: boolean;

  text?: string;
  error?: string;

  provider?: string;
  model?: string;

  latencyMs?: number;
  confidence?: number;

  metadata?: Record<
    string,
    unknown
  >;
}

export type CognitiveModelExecutor =
  (
    request:
      CognitiveModelExecutionRequest,
  ) =>
    Promise<
      CognitiveModelExecutionResult
    >;

export interface MultiModelOrchestrationRun {
  id: string;

  sequence: number;

  candidateId: string;
  pass:
    CognitiveModelPassKind;

  purpose: string;

  ok: boolean;

  text?: string;
  error?: string;

  provider?: string;
  model?: string;

  latencyMs?: number;
  confidence?: number;

  routeScore?: number;
  routeConfidence?: number;

  retainedAsAuditArtifact: boolean;
}

export type MultiModelOrchestrationStatus =
  | "completed"
  | "partial"
  | "blocked"
  | "failed";

export interface MultiModelOrchestrationResult {
  version: string;
  startedAt: string;
  completedAt: string;

  status:
    MultiModelOrchestrationStatus;

  strategy:
    CognitiveStrategyKind;

  selectedModelId?:
    string;

  finalText?: string;

  finalSourceRunId?: string;

  runs:
    MultiModelOrchestrationRun[];

  blockedRequirements: string[];

  warnings: string[];

  provenance: {
    promptCompilerVersion:
      string;
    modelRouterVersion:
      string;
    strategyRouterVersion:
      string;
    reasoningContractVersion:
      string;
  };

  invariants: {
    toolsExecuted: false;
    actionsExecuted: false;
    authorityBypassed: false;
    privateScratchReasoningRequired: false;
    privateScratchReasoningPersisted: false;
  };
}

function clamp01(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined ||
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function eligibleRanking(
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): RankedModelCandidate[] {
  return route.ranking
    .filter(
      (entry) =>
        entry.eligible,
    );
}

function selectedCandidate(
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): RankedModelCandidate | undefined {
  if (
    route.status !==
      "selected"
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return route.selected;
}

function routeEntryFor(
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
  id: string,
): RankedModelCandidate | undefined {
  return route.ranking
    .find(
      (entry) =>
        entry.candidate.id ===
        id,
    );
}

function promptForPass(
  base:
    CompiledPrompt,
  instruction:
    string,
  artifacts:
    Array<{
      label: string;
      text: string;
    }> = [],
): string {
  const artifactText =
    artifacts.length >
      0
      ? [
          "",
          "<<<PRIOR ORCHESTRATION ARTIFACTS>>>",
          ...artifacts.flatMap(
            (artifact) => [
              `[${artifact.label}]`,
              artifact.text,
            ],
          ),
        ].join(
          "\n",
        )
      : "";

  return [
    base.renderedPrompt,
    "",
    "<<<OH-4S ORCHESTRATION INSTRUCTION>>>",
    instruction,
    "Return only the task-relevant artifact for this pass.",
    "Do not claim tool execution, approval, authorization, or external actions unless supplied as authoritative input.",
    "Do not expose or reconstruct hidden chain-of-thought; provide conclusions, critique findings, assumptions, uncertainties, or revisions as appropriate.",
    artifactText,
  ].join(
    "\n",
  );
}

function numericCost(
  candidate:
    ModelRoutingCandidate,
): number {
  if (
    candidate.cost.mode ===
      "local-compute"
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  if (
    !candidate.cost.known
  ) {
    return Number
      .POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  }

  return (
    candidate.cost
      .inputPerMillionTokens ??
    0
  ) +
  (
    candidate.cost
      .outputPerMillionTokens ??
    0
  );
}

function cheapCandidate(
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): RankedModelCandidate | undefined {
  return eligibleRanking(
    route,
  )
    .slice()
    .sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) => {
        const costDiff =
          numericCost(
            left.candidate,
          ) -
          numericCost(
            right.candidate,
          );

        if (
          Number.isFinite(
            costDiff,
          ) &&
          costDiff !==
          0
        ) {
          return costDiff;
        }

        if (
          left.score !==
          right.score
        ) {
          return (
            right.score -
            left.score
          );
        }

        return left
          .candidate.id
          .localeCompare(
            right
              .candidate.id,
          );
      },
    )[0];
}

function competitionCandidates(
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): RankedModelCandidate[] {
  return eligibleRanking(
    route,
  )
    .slice(
      0,
      2,
    );
}

function provisionalCompetitionWinner(
  runs:
    readonly MultiModelOrchestrationRun[],
  route:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): MultiModelOrchestrationRun | undefined {
  const successful =
    runs.filter(
      (run) =>
        run.ok &&
        Boolean(
          run.text,
        ),
    );

  return successful
    .slice()
    .sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) => {
        const leftConfidence =
          left.confidence ??
          routeEntryFor(
            route,
            left.candidateId,
          )?.confidence ??
          0;

        const rightConfidence =
          right.confidence ??
          routeEntryFor(
            route,
            right.candidateId,
          )?.confidence ??
          0;

        if (
          leftConfidence !==
          rightConfidence
        ) {
          return (
            rightConfidence -
            leftConfidence
          );
        }

        const leftScore =
          left.routeScore ??
          0;

        const rightScore =
          right.routeScore ??
          0;

        if (
          leftScore !==
          rightScore
        ) {
          return (
            rightScore -
            leftScore
          );
        }

        return left.id
          .localeCompare(
            right.id,
          );
      },
    )[0];
}

function runId(
  sequence:
    number,
  pass:
    CognitiveModelPassKind,
  candidateId:
    string,
): string {
  return [
    "oh4s",
    sequence,
    pass,
    candidateId,
  ].join(
    ":",
  );
}

export class MultiModelOrchestrator {
  constructor(
    private readonly executor:
      CognitiveModelExecutor,
  ) {}

  async orchestrate(
    input: {
      compiledPrompt:
        CompiledPrompt;
      modelRoute:
        ModelRouteDecision;
      strategyRoute:
        StrategyRouteDecision;
      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;

      escalationConfidenceThreshold?:
        number;
      timeoutMs?:
        number;
    },
  ): Promise<MultiModelOrchestrationResult> {
    const startedAt =
      new Date()
        .toISOString();

    const warnings:
      string[] =
        [];

    const blockedRequirements:
      string[] =
        [];

    const runs:
      MultiModelOrchestrationRun[] =
        [];

    const strategySelection =
      input.strategyRoute
        .selected;

    const primary =
      selectedCandidate(
        input.modelRoute,
      );

    const finish =
      (
        status:
          MultiModelOrchestrationStatus,
        finalText?:
          string,
        finalSourceRunId?:
          string,
      ): MultiModelOrchestrationResult => ({
        version:
          MULTI_MODEL_ORCHESTRATOR_VERSION,
        startedAt,
        completedAt:
          new Date()
            .toISOString(),
        status,
        strategy:
          strategySelection
            ?.strategy ??
          input.reasoningContract
            .strategy,
        selectedModelId:
          primary
            ?.candidate.id,
        finalText,
        finalSourceRunId,
        runs:
          structuredClone(
            runs,
          ),
        blockedRequirements: [
          ...blockedRequirements,
        ],
        warnings: [
          ...warnings,
        ],
        provenance: {
          promptCompilerVersion:
            input
              .compiledPrompt
              .version,
          modelRouterVersion:
            input
              .modelRoute
              .version,
          strategyRouterVersion:
            input
              .strategyRoute
              .version,
          reasoningContractVersion:
            input
              .reasoningContract
              .version,
        },
        invariants: {
          toolsExecuted:
            false,
          actionsExecuted:
            false,
          authorityBypassed:
            false,
          privateScratchReasoningRequired:
            false,
          privateScratchReasoningPersisted:
            false,
        },
      });

    if (
      input.modelRoute
        .status !==
        "selected" ||
      !primary
    ) {
      blockedRequirements.push(
        "OH-4M must select an eligible primary model before orchestration.",
      );

      return finish(
        "blocked",
      );
    }

    if (
      input.strategyRoute
        .status !==
        "selected" ||
      !strategySelection
    ) {
      blockedRequirements.push(
        "OH-4N must select an eligible strategy before orchestration.",
      );

      return finish(
        "blocked",
      );
    }

    if (
      input.reasoningContract
        .strategy !==
      strategySelection
        .strategy
    ) {
      blockedRequirements.push(
        "OH-4O reasoning contract strategy does not match the OH-4N selected strategy.",
      );

      return finish(
        "blocked",
      );
    }

    const maxPasses =
      Math.max(
        1,
        input
          .reasoningContract
          .execution
          .maxPasses,
      );

    const execute =
      async (
        ranked:
          RankedModelCandidate,
        pass:
          CognitiveModelPassKind,
        purpose:
          string,
        prompt:
          string,
      ): Promise<MultiModelOrchestrationRun> => {
        if (
          runs.length >=
          maxPasses
        ) {
          return {
            id:
              runId(
                runs.length +
                1,
                pass,
                ranked
                  .candidate.id,
              ),
            sequence:
              runs.length +
              1,
            candidateId:
              ranked.candidate.id,
            pass,
            purpose,
            ok:
              false,
            error:
              "Reasoning-contract pass budget exhausted.",
            routeScore:
              ranked.score,
            routeConfidence:
              ranked.confidence,
            retainedAsAuditArtifact:
              true,
          };
        }

        const sequence =
          runs.length +
          1;

        let result:
          CognitiveModelExecutionResult;

        try {
          result =
            await this.executor({
              candidate:
                ranked.candidate,
              pass,
              purpose,
              prompt,
              timeoutMs:
                input.timeoutMs,
            });
        } catch (
          error
        ) {
          result = {
            ok:
              false,
            error:
              error instanceof Error
                ? error.message
                : String(
                    error,
                  ),
          };
        }

        const run:
          MultiModelOrchestrationRun = {
            id:
              runId(
                sequence,
                pass,
                ranked
                  .candidate.id,
              ),
            sequence,
            candidateId:
              ranked.candidate.id,
            pass,
            purpose,
            ok:
              result.ok,
            text:
              result.text,
            error:
              result.error,
            provider:
              result.provider,
            model:
              result.model,
            latencyMs:
              result.latencyMs,
            confidence:
              clamp01(
                result.confidence,
              ),
            routeScore:
              ranked.score,
            routeConfidence:
              ranked.confidence,
            retainedAsAuditArtifact:
              true,
          };

        runs.push(
          run,
        );

        return run;
      };

    const strategy =
      strategySelection
        .strategy;

    if (
      strategy ===
        "tool-assisted"
    ) {
      blockedRequirements.push(
        "Tool-assisted strategy requires authoritative tool results before model orchestration can continue.",
      );

      warnings.push(
        "OH-4S does not execute tools. Tool acquisition remains downstream of authority/action governance.",
      );

      return finish(
        "blocked",
      );
    }

    if (
      strategy ===
        "single-model" ||
      strategy ===
        "specialist"
    ) {
      const run =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "primary",
          strategy ===
            "specialist"
            ? "Produce the task artifact using the selected specialist model."
            : "Produce the task artifact using the selected primary model.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            strategy ===
              "specialist"
              ? "Act as the selected domain specialist. Produce the requested artifact while preserving all constraints and uncertainty."
              : "Produce the requested artifact in one bounded pass while preserving all constraints and uncertainty.",
          ),
        );

      if (
        run.ok &&
        run.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          "completed",
          run.text,
          run.id,
        );
      }

      warnings.push(
        "Primary model execution failed.",
      );

      return finish(
        "failed",
      );
    }

    if (
      strategy ===
        "cheap-first-escalation"
    ) {
      const cheap =
        cheapCandidate(
          input.modelRoute,
        ) ??
        primary;

      const first =
        await execute(
          cheap,
          "primary",
          "Attempt the task with the lowest-cost eligible model.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Produce a complete candidate. Be explicit about uncertainty. This is the cheap-first pass; do not claim success beyond the evidence available.",
          ),
        );

      const threshold =
        clamp01(
          input
            .escalationConfidenceThreshold ??
          0.72,
        ) ??
        0.72;

      if (
        first.ok &&
        first.text &&
        first.confidence !==
          undefined &&
        first.confidence >=
          threshold
      ) {
        warnings.push(
          "Cheap-first result met the configured execution-confidence threshold; escalation was not required.",
        );

        return finish(
          "completed",
          first.text,
          first.id,
        );
      }

      if (
        primary.candidate.id ===
        cheap.candidate.id
      ) {
        warnings.push(
          "Cheap-first pass could not escalate because the selected primary model is already the cheapest eligible candidate.",
        );

        return first.ok &&
          first.text
          ? finish(
              "partial",
              first.text,
              first.id,
            )
          : finish(
              "failed",
            );
      }

      const fallback =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "fallback",
          "Escalate to the OH-4M selected primary model after insufficient cheap-first confidence or failure.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Produce a fresh, higher-confidence candidate. A cheaper model was attempted first but did not satisfy the escalation threshold. Do not assume its answer is correct.",
            first.text
              ? [
                  {
                    label:
                      "CHEAP-FIRST CANDIDATE",
                    text:
                      first.text,
                  },
                ]
              : [],
          ),
        );

      if (
        fallback.ok &&
        fallback.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          "completed",
          fallback.text,
          fallback.id,
        );
      }

      return first.ok &&
        first.text
        ? finish(
            "partial",
            first.text,
            first.id,
          )
        : finish(
            "failed",
          );
    }

    if (
      strategy ===
        "competition"
    ) {
      const competitors =
        competitionCandidates(
          input.modelRoute,
        );

      if (
        competitors.length <
        2
      ) {
        blockedRequirements.push(
          "Competition requires at least two eligible OH-4M model candidates.",
        );

        return finish(
          "blocked",
        );
      }

      const remainingBudget =
        Math.max(
          0,
          maxPasses -
          runs.length,
        );

      const chosenCompetitors =
        competitors.slice(
          0,
          Math.min(
            2,
            remainingBudget,
          ),
        );

      const produced =
        await Promise.all(
          chosenCompetitors.map(
            (
              competitor,
              index,
            ) =>
              execute(
                competitor,
                index ===
                  0
                  ? "primary"
                  : "secondary",
                "Generate an independent candidate for competition.",
                promptForPass(
                  input
                    .compiledPrompt,
                  "Generate an independent candidate. Do not use or assume another model's answer. Preserve constraints and surface uncertainty.",
                ),
              ),
          ),
        );

      const winner =
        provisionalCompetitionWinner(
          produced,
          input.modelRoute,
        );

      warnings.push(
        "Competition winner is provisional and based only on execution/route confidence. Formal critique and verification are deferred to OH-4T.",
      );

      if (
        winner?.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          produced.every(
            (run) =>
              run.ok,
          )
            ? "completed"
            : "partial",
          winner.text,
          winner.id,
        );
      }

      return finish(
        "failed",
      );
    }

    if (
      strategy ===
        "refinement"
    ) {
      const draft =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "primary",
          "Produce the initial candidate for refinement.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Produce the first complete candidate. Preserve all explicit constraints and surface uncertainty.",
          ),
        );

      if (
        !draft.ok ||
        !draft.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          "failed",
        );
      }

      const refined =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "refinement",
          "Refine the initial candidate for quality, completeness, and task fit.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Refine the prior candidate. Correct weaknesses, improve completeness and clarity, preserve valid content, and do not invent missing evidence.",
            [
              {
                label:
                  "INITIAL CANDIDATE",
                text:
                  draft.text,
              },
            ],
          ),
        );

      if (
        refined.ok &&
        refined.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          "completed",
          refined.text,
          refined.id,
        );
      }

      warnings.push(
        "Refinement pass failed; returning the initial candidate as partial output.",
      );

      return finish(
        "partial",
        draft.text,
        draft.id,
      );
    }

    if (
      strategy ===
        "critic-loop"
    ) {
      const draft =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "primary",
          "Produce the initial candidate for critique.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Produce the first complete candidate. Preserve constraints, evidence boundaries, assumptions, and uncertainty.",
          ),
        );

      if (
        !draft.ok ||
        !draft.text
      ) {
        return finish(
          "failed",
        );
      }

      const critic =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "critic",
          "Critique the initial candidate against the active task and reasoning contract.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Critique the prior candidate. Identify concrete factual, logical, evidence, constraint, completeness, and uncertainty defects. Do not rewrite the answer yet.",
            [
              {
                label:
                  "CANDIDATE TO CRITIQUE",
                text:
                  draft.text,
              },
            ],
          ),
        );

      if (
        !critic.ok ||
        !critic.text
      ) {
        warnings.push(
          "Critic pass failed; returning the initial candidate as partial output.",
        );

        return finish(
          "partial",
          draft.text,
          draft.id,
        );
      }

      const refined =
        await execute(
          primary,
          "refinement",
          "Revise the candidate using the explicit critique findings.",
          promptForPass(
            input
              .compiledPrompt,
            "Revise the initial candidate using the critique. Repair supported defects, preserve valid content, and do not invent evidence. Return the revised final artifact.",
            [
              {
                label:
                  "INITIAL CANDIDATE",
                text:
                  draft.text,
              },
              {
                label:
                  "CRITIQUE FINDINGS",
                text:
                  critic.text,
              },
            ],
          ),
        );

      if (
        refined.ok &&
        refined.text
      ) {
        warnings.push(
          "OH-4S completed the strategy-level critic loop. Formal independent verification remains the responsibility of OH-4T.",
        );

        return finish(
          "completed",
          refined.text,
          refined.id,
        );
      }

      warnings.push(
        "Final refinement failed after a successful critic pass; returning the initial candidate as partial output.",
      );

      return finish(
        "partial",
        draft.text,
        draft.id,
      );
    }

    blockedRequirements.push(
      `Unsupported strategy: ${strategy}.`,
    );

    return finish(
      "blocked",
    );
  }
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\multiModelGatewayExecutor.ts
============================================================
import {
  generateWithModel,
} from "./gateway";

import type {
  CognitiveModelExecutor,
} from "./multiModelOrchestrator";

export const GATEWAY_EXPLICIT_MODEL_TARGETING =
  true;

export const GATEWAY_MODEL_TARGET_MODE =
  "provider-model" as const;

function numericMetadata(
  value: unknown,
): number | undefined {
  return (
    typeof value ===
      "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  )
    ? value
    : undefined;
}

export const gatewayCognitiveModelExecutor:
  CognitiveModelExecutor =
    async (
      request,
    ) => {
      if (
        !GATEWAY_EXPLICIT_MODEL_TARGETING
      ) {
        return {
          ok:
            false,
          error:
            "The live Unified LLM Gateway does not expose an explicit model-target field. OH-4S fails closed rather than pretending a specific OH-4M candidate was executed.",
          provider:
            request.candidate
              .provider,
          model:
            request.candidate
              .model,
        };
      }

      const started =
        Date.now();

      try {
        const result =
          await generateWithModel(
            {
        provider:
          request.candidate.provider,
        model:
          request.candidate.model,
              prompt:
                request.prompt,
              timeoutMs:
                request.timeoutMs,
            } as any,
          );

        const resultRecord =
          result as unknown as
            Record<
              string,
              unknown
            >;

        const text =
          typeof resultRecord.text ===
            "string"
            ? resultRecord.text
            : typeof resultRecord.output ===
                "string"
              ? resultRecord.output
              : undefined;

        const error =
          typeof resultRecord.error ===
            "string"
            ? resultRecord.error
            : undefined;

        const ok =
          typeof resultRecord.ok ===
            "boolean"
            ? resultRecord.ok
            : Boolean(
                text,
              );

        return {
          ok,
          text,
          error,
          provider:
            typeof resultRecord.provider ===
              "string"
              ? resultRecord.provider
              : request.candidate
                  .provider,
          model:
            typeof resultRecord.model ===
              "string"
              ? resultRecord.model
              : request.candidate
                  .model,
          latencyMs:
            numericMetadata(
              resultRecord.latencyMs,
            ) ??
            (
              Date.now() -
              started
            ),
          confidence:
            numericMetadata(
              resultRecord.confidence,
            ),
          metadata: {
            targetMode:
              GATEWAY_MODEL_TARGET_MODE,
            requestedCandidateId:
              request.candidate.id,
          },
        };
      } catch (
        error
      ) {
        return {
          ok:
            false,
          error:
            error instanceof Error
              ? error.message
              : String(
                  error,
                ),
          provider:
            request.candidate
              .provider,
          model:
            request.candidate
              .model,
          latencyMs:
            Date.now() -
            started,
        };
      }
    };

export function getGatewayCognitiveModelExecutor():
  CognitiveModelExecutor {
  return gatewayCognitiveModelExecutor;
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\criticVerificationLayer.ts
