import type {
  CriticVerificationResult,
  VerificationVerdict,
} from "./criticVerificationLayer";

import type {
  ModelRouteDecision,
} from "./modelDecisionRouter";

import type {
  MultiModelOrchestrationResult,
} from "./multiModelOrchestrator";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const CONFIDENCE_ENGINE_VERSION =
  "oh4u.v1";

export type ConfidenceBand =
  | "very-low"
  | "low"
  | "moderate"
  | "high"
  | "very-high";

export type ConfidenceEvidenceFreshness =
  | "fresh"
  | "aging"
  | "stale"
  | "unknown";

export interface ConfidenceModelHistory {
  sampleCount: number;

  successRate?: number;
  verificationPassRate?: number;
  recentFailureRate?: number;

  calibratedScore?: number;
}

export interface ConfidenceContextEvidence {
  taskNovelty?: number;
  disagreement?: number;

  evidenceFreshness?:
    ConfidenceEvidenceFreshness;

  evidenceConfidence?: number;

  uncertaintySignals?: {
    assumptions?: number;
    unresolvedQuestions?: number;
    contradictions?: number;
    explicitUncertainties?: number;
  };

  modelHistory?:
    ConfidenceModelHistory;
}

export type ConfidenceComponentKind =
  | "verification"
  | "deterministic-coverage"
  | "independent-critic"
  | "model-routing"
  | "orchestration"
  | "model-history"
  | "evidence-quality"
  | "disagreement"
  | "task-novelty"
  | "uncertainty";

export interface ConfidenceComponent {
  kind:
    ConfidenceComponentKind;

  weight: number;
  value: number;
  weightedValue: number;

  evidenceQuality:
    | "strong"
    | "moderate"
    | "weak"
    | "missing";

  reasons: string[];
}

export interface ConfidenceCap {
  id: string;

  maximum: number;

  reason: string;
}

export interface ConfidenceAssessment {
  version: string;
  assessedAt: string;

  score: number;
  percentage: number;

  band:
    ConfidenceBand;

  verificationVerdict:
    VerificationVerdict;

  trustedByVerification:
    boolean;

  components:
    ConfidenceComponent[];

  caps:
    ConfidenceCap[];

  strongestPositiveSignals:
    string[];

  strongestNegativeSignals:
    string[];

  dataQuality: {
    score: number;
    missingSignals:
      string[];
  };

  modelReportedConfidence?: {
    value: number;
    advisoryOnly: true;
    reason: string;
  };

  warnings: string[];

  provenance: {
    criticVerificationVersion:
      string;
    orchestratorVersion:
      string;
    modelRouterVersion:
      string;
    reasoningContractVersion:
      string;
  };

  invariants: {
    modelSelfConfidenceAuthoritative: false;
    escalationDecided: false;
    toolsExecuted: false;
    actionsExecuted: false;
    authorityBypassed: false;
  };
}

const WEIGHTS:
  Record<
    ConfidenceComponentKind,
    number
  > = {
    verification:
      0.35,

    "deterministic-coverage":
      0.15,

    "independent-critic":
      0.10,

    "model-routing":
      0.08,

    orchestration:
      0.08,

    "model-history":
      0.08,

    "evidence-quality":
      0.08,

    disagreement:
      0.04,

    "task-novelty":
      0.02,

    uncertainty:
      0.02,
  };

function clamp01(
  value:
    number,
  field:
    string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a finite number.`,
    );
  }

  if (
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function optionalUnit(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field:
    string,
): number | undefined {
  return value ===
    undefined
    ? undefined
    : clamp01(
        value,
        field,
      );
}

function nonNegativeInteger(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field:
    string,
): number {
  const normalized =
    value ??
    0;

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      normalized,
    ) ||
    normalized < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a non-negative integer.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function bandForScore(
  score: number,
): ConfidenceBand {
  if (
    score >=
    0.90
  ) {
    return "very-high";
  }

  if (
    score >=
    0.75
  ) {
    return "high";
  }

  if (
    score >=
    0.55
  ) {
    return "moderate";
  }

  if (
    score >=
    0.30
  ) {
    return "low";
  }

  return "very-low";
}

function verificationValue(
  verdict:
    VerificationVerdict,
): number {
  switch (
    verdict
  ) {
    case "pass":
      return 1;

    case "revise":
      return 0.52;

    case "reject":
      return 0.08;

    case "blocked":
      return 0;
  }
}

function deterministicCoverageValue(
  verification:
    CriticVerificationResult,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const summary =
    verification
      .deterministic;

  const explicitChecks =
    summary.checksRun +
    summary.constraintChecksRun +
    summary.factualChecksRun;

  const reasons:
    string[] =
      [];

  if (
    explicitChecks ===
    0
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "No explicit deterministic, constraint, or factual checks were supplied.",
    );

    return {
      value:
        0.45,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons,
    };
  }

  if (
    summary.checksFailed >
    0
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      `${summary.checksFailed} deterministic checks failed.`,
    );

    return {
      value:
        0,
      quality:
        "strong",
      reasons,
    };
  }

  const coverageKinds =
    [
      summary.checksRun >
        0,
      summary.constraintChecksRun >
        0,
      summary.factualChecksRun >
        0,
    ].filter(
      Boolean,
    ).length;

  const value =
    coverageKinds ===
      3
      ? 1
      : coverageKinds ===
          2
        ? 0.85
        : 0.7;

  reasons.push(
    `${coverageKinds} deterministic verification categories supplied with no recorded deterministic failures.`,
  );

  return {
    value,
    quality:
      coverageKinds >=
        2
        ? "strong"
        : "moderate",
    reasons,
  };
}

function criticValue(
  verification:
    CriticVerificationResult,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const critic =
    verification
      .independentCritic;

  if (
    !critic.required
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0.72,
      quality:
        "moderate",
      reasons: [
        "Independent critic was not required by the active reasoning contract.",
      ],
    };
  }

  if (
    !critic.attempted ||
    !critic.parsed
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons: [
        "Independent critic was required but did not produce a parsed verification result.",
      ],
    };
  }

  switch (
    critic.verdict
  ) {
    case "pass":
      return {
        value:
          critic
            .independentFromFinalSource
            ? 1
            : 0.35,
        quality:
          critic
            .independentFromFinalSource
            ? "strong"
            : "weak",
        reasons: [
          critic
            .independentFromFinalSource
            ? "Independent critic passed the candidate."
            : "Critic passed, but independence from the final-source model was not established.",
        ],
      };

    case "revise":
      return {
        value:
          0.42,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "Independent critic requested revision.",
        ],
      };

    case "reject":
      return {
        value:
          0,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "Independent critic rejected the candidate.",
        ],
      };

    default:
      return {
        value:
          0,
        quality:
          "missing",
        reasons: [
          "Independent critic verdict is unavailable.",
        ],
      };
  }
}

function routeValue(
  modelRoute:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  if (
    modelRoute.status !==
      "selected" ||
    !modelRoute.selected
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons: [
        "No eligible model was selected by OH-4M.",
      ],
    };
  }

  return {
    value:
      clamp01(
        modelRoute
          .selected
          .confidence,
        "modelRoute.selected.confidence",
      ),
    quality:
      "moderate",
    reasons: [
      `OH-4M route confidence=${modelRoute.selected.confidence.toFixed(2)}.`,
    ],
  };
}

function orchestrationValue(
  orchestration:
    MultiModelOrchestrationResult,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  switch (
    orchestration.status
  ) {
    case "completed":
      return {
        value:
          1,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "OH-4S completed the selected strategy.",
        ],
      };

    case "partial":
      return {
        value:
          0.5,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "OH-4S returned only a partial strategy result.",
        ],
      };

    case "blocked":
      return {
        value:
          0,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "OH-4S orchestration was blocked.",
        ],
      };

    case "failed":
      return {
        value:
          0,
        quality:
          "strong",
        reasons: [
          "OH-4S orchestration failed.",
        ],
      };
  }
}

function historyValue(
  history:
    ConfidenceModelHistory
    | undefined,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  if (
    !history
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0.5,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons: [
        "No empirical model-history evidence was supplied.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const sampleCount =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      history.sampleCount,
      "modelHistory.sampleCount",
    );

  const success =
    optionalUnit(
      history.successRate,
      "modelHistory.successRate",
    );

  const verificationPass =
    optionalUnit(
      history.verificationPassRate,
      "modelHistory.verificationPassRate",
    );

  const recentFailure =
    optionalUnit(
      history.recentFailureRate,
      "modelHistory.recentFailureRate",
    );

  const calibrated =
    optionalUnit(
      history.calibratedScore,
      "modelHistory.calibratedScore",
    );

  const signals =
    [
      success,
      verificationPass,
      calibrated,
      recentFailure ===
        undefined
        ? undefined
        : 1 -
          recentFailure,
    ].filter(
      (
        value,
      ): value is number =>
        value !==
        undefined,
    );

  if (
    signals.length ===
    0
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0.5,
      quality:
        sampleCount >
          0
          ? "weak"
          : "missing",
      reasons: [
        "Model-history record contains no usable reliability rates.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const mean =
    signals.reduce(
      (
        total,
        value,
      ) =>
        total +
        value,
      0,
    ) /
    signals.length;

  const sampleFactor =
    Math.min(
      1,
      sampleCount /
        25,
    );

  const value =
    0.5 +
    (
      mean -
      0.5
    ) *
      sampleFactor;

  return {
    value:
      clamp01(
        value,
        "modelHistory.value",
      ),
    quality:
      sampleCount >=
        25
        ? "strong"
        : sampleCount >=
            8
          ? "moderate"
          : "weak",
    reasons: [
      `Model history uses ${sampleCount} samples across ${signals.length} reliability signals.`,
    ],
  };
}

function evidenceValue(
  context:
    ConfidenceContextEvidence,
  verification:
    CriticVerificationResult,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  const freshness =
    context
      .evidenceFreshness ??
    "unknown";

  const freshnessScore =
    freshness ===
      "fresh"
      ? 1
      : freshness ===
          "aging"
        ? 0.72
        : freshness ===
            "stale"
          ? 0.2
          : 0.5;

  const confidence =
    optionalUnit(
      context.evidenceConfidence,
      "evidenceConfidence",
    );

  const referenceFactor =
    verification
      .deterministic
      .evidenceReferenceCount >
    0
      ? 1
      : 0.55;

  const value =
    (
      freshnessScore *
        0.45 +
      (
        confidence ??
        0.5
      ) *
        0.35 +
      referenceFactor *
        0.20
    );

  return {
    value:
      clamp01(
        value,
        "evidenceQuality.value",
      ),
    quality:
      freshness ===
        "fresh" &&
      confidence !==
        undefined &&
      verification
        .deterministic
        .evidenceReferenceCount >
        0
        ? "strong"
        : freshness ===
            "stale"
          ? "weak"
          : "moderate",
    reasons: [
      `Evidence freshness=${freshness}.`,
      confidence ===
        undefined
        ? "No aggregate evidence-confidence signal supplied."
        : `Aggregate evidence confidence=${confidence.toFixed(2)}.`,
      `${verification.deterministic.evidenceReferenceCount} evidence references recorded by OH-4T.`,
    ],
  };
}

function inverseSignal(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field:
    string,
  missingReason:
    string,
  suppliedReason:
    (
      normalized: number,
    ) => string,
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0.5,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons: [
        missingReason,
      ],
    };
  }

  const normalized =
    clamp01(
      value,
      field,
    );

  return {
    value:
      1 -
      normalized,
    quality:
      "moderate",
    reasons: [
      suppliedReason(
        normalized,
      ),
    ],
  };
}

function uncertaintyValue(
  signals:
    ConfidenceContextEvidence[
      "uncertaintySignals"
    ],
): {
  value: number;
  quality:
    ConfidenceComponent[
      "evidenceQuality"
    ];
  reasons: string[];
} {
  if (
    !signals
  ) {
    return {
      value:
        0.5,
      quality:
        "missing",
      reasons: [
        "No structured uncertainty-signal counts were supplied.",
      ],
    };
  }

  const assumptions =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      signals.assumptions,
      "uncertaintySignals.assumptions",
    );

  const unresolved =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      signals.unresolvedQuestions,
      "uncertaintySignals.unresolvedQuestions",
    );

  const contradictions =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      signals.contradictions,
      "uncertaintySignals.contradictions",
    );

  const explicitUncertainties =
    nonNegativeInteger(
      signals.explicitUncertainties,
      "uncertaintySignals.explicitUncertainties",
    );

  const penalty =
    Math.min(
      1,
      assumptions *
        0.05 +
      unresolved *
        0.16 +
      contradictions *
        0.28 +
      explicitUncertainties *
        0.08,
    );

  return {
    value:
      1 -
      penalty,
    quality:
      "moderate",
    reasons: [
      `Uncertainty counts: assumptions=${assumptions}, unresolved=${unresolved}, contradictions=${contradictions}, explicit=${explicitUncertainties}.`,
    ],
  };
}

function component(
  kind:
    ConfidenceComponentKind,
  assessed: {
    value: number;
    quality:
      ConfidenceComponent[
        "evidenceQuality"
      ];
    reasons: string[];
  },
): ConfidenceComponent {
  const weight =
    WEIGHTS[kind];

  return {
    kind,
    weight,
    value:
      assessed.value,
    weightedValue:
      assessed.value *
      weight,
    evidenceQuality:
      assessed.quality,
    reasons:
      assessed.reasons,
  };
}

function confidenceCaps(
  input: {
    verification:
      CriticVerificationResult;
    orchestration:
      MultiModelOrchestrationResult;
    contract:
      ReasoningContract;
  },
): ConfidenceCap[] {
  const caps:
    ConfidenceCap[] =
      [];

  switch (
    input.verification
      .verdict
  ) {
    case "blocked":
      caps.push({
        id:
          "verification-blocked",
        maximum:
          0.05,
        reason:
          "OH-4T verification is blocked.",
      });
      break;

    case "reject":
      caps.push({
        id:
          "verification-rejected",
        maximum:
          0.15,
        reason:
          "OH-4T rejected the candidate.",
      });
      break;

    case "revise":
      caps.push({
        id:
          "verification-revision-required",
        maximum:
          0.64,
        reason:
          "OH-4T requires revision before the conclusion can be trusted.",
      });
      break;

    case "pass":
      break;
  }

  if (
    input.orchestration
      .status ===
      "partial"
  ) {
    caps.push({
      id:
        "partial-orchestration",
      maximum:
        0.68,
      reason:
        "OH-4S produced only a partial orchestration result.",
    });
  }

  if (
    input.orchestration
      .status ===
        "blocked" ||
    input.orchestration
      .status ===
        "failed"
  ) {
    caps.push({
      id:
        "orchestration-unusable",
      maximum:
        0.05,
      reason:
        "OH-4S did not produce a completed or partial usable result.",
    });
  }

  if (
    input.contract
      .validation
      .required &&
    !input.verification
      .trustedConclusion
  ) {
    caps.push({
      id:
        "required-validation-not-trusted",
      maximum:
        0.64,
      reason:
        "The reasoning contract requires validation and OH-4T has not marked the conclusion trusted.",
    });
  }

  return caps;
}

function finalRunModelConfidence(
  orchestration:
    MultiModelOrchestrationResult,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    !orchestration
      .finalSourceRunId
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const value =
    orchestration.runs
      .find(
        (run) =>
          run.id ===
          orchestration
            .finalSourceRunId,
      )
      ?.confidence;

  return value ===
    undefined
    ? undefined
    : clamp01(
        value,
        "modelReportedConfidence",
      );
}

function dataQuality(
  components:
    readonly ConfidenceComponent[],
): {
  score: number;
  missingSignals: string[];
} {
  const qualityScore = {
    strong:
      1,
    moderate:
      0.75,
    weak:
      0.45,
    missing:
      0.2,
  } as const;

  const weighted =
    components.reduce(
      (
        total,
        item,
      ) =>
        total +
        qualityScore[
          item.evidenceQuality
        ] *
          item.weight,
      0,
    );

  const missingSignals =
    components
      .filter(
        (item) =>
          item.evidenceQuality ===
          "missing",
      )
      .map(
        (item) =>
          item.kind,
      );

  return {
    score:
      clamp01(
        weighted,
        "dataQuality.score",
      ),
    missingSignals,
  };
}

export class ConfidenceEngine {
  assess(
    input: {
      verification:
        CriticVerificationResult;

      orchestration:
        MultiModelOrchestrationResult;

      modelRoute:
        ModelRouteDecision;

      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;

      context?:
        ConfidenceContextEvidence;
    },
  ): ConfidenceAssessment {
    const context =
      input.context ??
      {};

    const verificationComponent =
      component(
        "verification",
        {
          value:
            verificationValue(
              input.verification
                .verdict,
            ),
          quality:
            "strong",
          reasons: [
            `OH-4T verdict=${input.verification.verdict}.`,
            input.verification
              .trustedConclusion
              ? "OH-4T marked the conclusion trusted."
              : "OH-4T did not mark the conclusion trusted.",
          ],
        },
      );

    const deterministicComponent =
      component(
        "deterministic-coverage",
        deterministicCoverageValue(
          input.verification,
        ),
      );

    const criticComponent =
      component(
        "independent-critic",
        criticValue(
          input.verification,
        ),
      );

    const routingComponent =
      component(
        "model-routing",
        routeValue(
          input.modelRoute,
        ),
      );

    const orchestrationComponent =
      component(
        "orchestration",
        orchestrationValue(
          input.orchestration,
        ),
      );

    const historyComponent =
      component(
        "model-history",
        historyValue(
          context.modelHistory,
        ),
      );

    const evidenceComponent =
      component(
        "evidence-quality",
        evidenceValue(
          context,
          input.verification,
        ),
      );

    const disagreementComponent =
      component(
        "disagreement",
        inverseSignal(
          context.disagreement,
          "disagreement",
          "No cross-model/result disagreement signal was supplied.",
          (value) =>
            `Observed disagreement=${value.toFixed(2)}; higher disagreement lowers confidence.`,
        ),
      );

    const noveltyComponent =
      component(
        "task-novelty",
        inverseSignal(
          context.taskNovelty,
          "taskNovelty",
          "No task-novelty signal was supplied.",
          (value) =>
            `Task novelty=${value.toFixed(2)}; unfamiliar tasks reduce confidence.`,
        ),
      );

    const uncertaintyComponent =
      component(
        "uncertainty",
        uncertaintyValue(
          context
            .uncertaintySignals,
        ),
      );

    const components = [
      verificationComponent,
      deterministicComponent,
      criticComponent,
      routingComponent,
      orchestrationComponent,
      historyComponent,
      evidenceComponent,
      disagreementComponent,
      noveltyComponent,
      uncertaintyComponent,
    ];

    const rawScore =
      components.reduce(
        (
          total,
          item,
        ) =>
          total +
          item.weightedValue,
        0,
      );

    const caps =
      confidenceCaps({
        verification:
          input.verification,
        orchestration:
          input.orchestration,
        contract:
          input
            .reasoningContract,
      });

    const maximum =
      caps.length >
        0
        ? Math.min(
            ...caps.map(
              (cap) =>
                cap.maximum,
            ),
          )
        : 1;

    const score =
      clamp01(
        Math.min(
          rawScore,
          maximum,
        ),
        "confidence.score",
      );

    const positive =
      components
        .filter(
          (item) =>
            item.value >=
            0.75,
        )
        .sort(
          (
            left,
            right,
          ) =>
            right
              .weightedValue -
            left
              .weightedValue,
        )
        .flatMap(
          (item) =>
            item.reasons
              .map(
                (entry) =>
                  `${item.kind}: ${entry}`,
              ),
        )
        .slice(
          0,
          5,
        );

    const negative =
      [
        ...components
          .filter(
            (item) =>
              item.value <
              0.45,
          )
          .sort(
            (
              left,
              right,
            ) =>
              left.value -
              right.value,
          )
          .flatMap(
            (item) =>
              item.reasons
                .map(
                  (entry) =>
                    `${item.kind}: ${entry}`,
                ),
          ),
        ...caps.map(
          (cap) =>
            `cap:${cap.id}: ${cap.reason}`,
        ),
      ].slice(
        0,
        7,
      );

    const quality =
      dataQuality(
        components,
      );

    const reported =
      finalRunModelConfidence(
        input.orchestration,
      );

    const warnings:
      string[] =
        [];

    if (
      quality.score <
      0.6
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Confidence assessment is based on incomplete or weak supporting signals.",
      );
    }

    if (
      caps.length >
      0
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "One or more hard confidence caps were applied from verification/orchestration state.",
      );
    }

    if (
      reported !==
      undefined
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Model-reported confidence is recorded for audit only and contributes no direct weight to OH-4U confidence.",
      );
    }

    return {
      version:
        CONFIDENCE_ENGINE_VERSION,
      assessedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      score,
      percentage:
        Math.round(
          score *
          100,
        ),
      band:
        bandForScore(
          score,
        ),
      verificationVerdict:
        input.verification
          .verdict,
      trustedByVerification:
        input.verification
          .trustedConclusion,
      components:
        structuredClone(
          components,
        ),
      caps:
        structuredClone(
          caps,
        ),
      strongestPositiveSignals:
        positive,
      strongestNegativeSignals:
        negative,
      dataQuality:
        quality,
      modelReportedConfidence:
        reported ===
          undefined
          ? undefined
          : {
              value:
                reported,
              advisoryOnly:
                true,
              reason:
                "Self-reported/model-pass confidence is not authoritative and receives zero direct score weight.",
            },
      warnings,
      provenance: {
        criticVerificationVersion:
          input.verification
            .version,
        orchestratorVersion:
          input.orchestration
            .version,
        modelRouterVersion:
          input.modelRoute
            .version,
        reasoningContractVersion:
          input
            .reasoningContract
            .version,
      },
      invariants: {
        modelSelfConfidenceAuthoritative:
          false,
        escalationDecided:
          false,
        toolsExecuted:
          false,
        actionsExecuted:
          false,
        authorityBypassed:
          false,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultConfidenceEngine =
  new ConfidenceEngine();

export function getConfidenceEngine():
  ConfidenceEngine {
  return defaultConfidenceEngine;
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\escalationEngine.ts
============================================================
import type {
  ConfidenceAssessment,
  ConfidenceBand,
  ConfidenceComponentKind,
} from "./confidenceEngine";

import type {
  CriticVerificationResult,
} from "./criticVerificationLayer";

import type {
  ModelRouteDecision,
  RankedModelCandidate,
} from "./modelDecisionRouter";

import type {
  MultiModelOrchestrationResult,
} from "./multiModelOrchestrator";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const ESCALATION_ENGINE_VERSION =
  "oh4v.v1";

export type EscalationActionKind =
  | "none"
  | "retry"
  | "enrich-context"
  | "switch-model"
  | "increase-reasoning-effort"
  | "invoke-critic"
  | "request-human-clarification"
  | "stop-safely";

export type EscalationReasonCode =
  | "confidence-sufficient"
  | "blocking-ambiguity"
  | "context-insufficient"
  | "verification-critic-needed"
  | "transient-failure"
  | "model-underperforming"
  | "reasoning-depth-insufficient"
  | "low-confidence-retry"
  | "attempt-budget-exhausted"
  | "no-safe-recovery-path";

export type EscalationReasoningEffort =
  | "standard"
  | "deep"
  | "very-deep"
  | "maximum";

export interface EscalationQuestion {
  question: string;
  blocking: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

export interface EscalationContext {
  attemptCount?: number;
  maxAttempts?: number;

  unresolvedQuestions?:
    EscalationQuestion[];

  humanClarificationNeeded?:
    boolean;

  contextCanBeEnriched?:
    boolean;

  contextGapCount?:
    number;

  criticAvailable?:
    boolean;

  criticCandidateId?:
    string;

  transientFailureCount?:
    number;

  modelFailureCount?:
    number;

  currentReasoningEffort?:
    EscalationReasoningEffort;

  maxReasoningEffort?:
    EscalationReasoningEffort;

  allowRetry?:
    boolean;

  allowContextEnrichment?:
    boolean;

  allowModelSwitch?:
    boolean;

  allowReasoningIncrease?:
    boolean;

  allowCritic?:
    boolean;

  allowHumanClarification?:
    boolean;
}

export interface EscalationPolicy {
  acceptanceThreshold: number;

  contextEnrichmentThreshold:
    number;

  modelSwitchThreshold:
    number;

  reasoningIncreaseThreshold:
    number;

  retryThreshold:
    number;

  maxAttempts: number;

  maxClarificationQuestions:
    number;
}

export interface EscalationDirective {
  kind:
    EscalationActionKind;

  reasonCode:
    EscalationReasonCode;

  summary: string;

  prerequisites: string[];

  preservesCurrentArtifact:
    boolean;

  targetModelId?: string;

  nextReasoningEffort?:
    EscalationReasoningEffort;

  criticCandidateId?: string;

  clarificationQuestions?:
    string[];

  attemptNumber?: number;

  mayExecute: false;
  authorityGranted: false;
}

export interface EscalationDecision {
  version: string;
  decidedAt: string;

  required: boolean;

  confidence: {
    score: number;
    percentage: number;
    band:
      ConfidenceBand;
    acceptanceThreshold:
      number;
  };

  verificationVerdict:
    CriticVerificationResult[
      "verdict"
    ];

  selected:
    EscalationDirective;

  alternatives:
    EscalationDirective[];

  attemptBudget: {
    current: number;
    maximum: number;
    exhausted: boolean;
  };

  signals: {
    contextGapDetected:
      boolean;

    criticGapDetected:
      boolean;

    transientFailureDetected:
      boolean;

    modelUnderperformanceDetected:
      boolean;

    reasoningDepthGapDetected:
      boolean;

    blockingAmbiguityDetected:
      boolean;
  };

  warnings: string[];

  provenance: {
    confidenceEngineVersion:
      string;
    criticVerificationVersion:
      string;
    orchestratorVersion:
      string;
    modelRouterVersion:
      string;
    reasoningContractVersion:
      string;
  };

  invariants: {
    escalationExecuted: false;
    toolsExecuted: false;
    actionsExecuted: false;
    authorityBypassed: false;
    modelRouteMutated: false;
    verificationMutated: false;
  };
}

export const DEFAULT_ESCALATION_POLICY:
  EscalationPolicy = {
    acceptanceThreshold:
      0.75,

    contextEnrichmentThreshold:
      0.68,

    modelSwitchThreshold:
      0.58,

    reasoningIncreaseThreshold:
      0.64,

    retryThreshold:
      0.52,

    maxAttempts:
      3,

    maxClarificationQuestions:
      5,
  };

const REASONING_ORDER:
  EscalationReasoningEffort[] = [
    "standard",
    "deep",
    "very-deep",
    "maximum",
  ];

function clamp01(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function nonNegativeInteger(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  fallback:
    number,
  field:
    string,
): number {
  const normalized =
    value ??
    fallback;

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      normalized,
    ) ||
    normalized < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a non-negative integer.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function positiveInteger(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  fallback:
    number,
  field:
    string,
): number {
  const normalized =
    value ??
    fallback;

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      normalized,
    ) ||
    normalized < 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a positive integer.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function policyWithOverrides(
  policy:
    Partial<
      EscalationPolicy
    >
    | undefined,
): EscalationPolicy {
  const merged = {
    ...DEFAULT_ESCALATION_POLICY,
    ...policy,
  };

  clamp01(
    merged.acceptanceThreshold,
    "acceptanceThreshold",
  );

  clamp01(
    merged.contextEnrichmentThreshold,
    "contextEnrichmentThreshold",
  );

  clamp01(
    merged.modelSwitchThreshold,
    "modelSwitchThreshold",
  );

  clamp01(
    merged.reasoningIncreaseThreshold,
    "reasoningIncreaseThreshold",
  );

  clamp01(
    merged.retryThreshold,
    "retryThreshold",
  );

  positiveInteger(
    merged.maxAttempts,
    DEFAULT_ESCALATION_POLICY
      .maxAttempts,
    "maxAttempts",
  );

  positiveInteger(
    merged.maxClarificationQuestions,
    DEFAULT_ESCALATION_POLICY
      .maxClarificationQuestions,
    "maxClarificationQuestions",
  );

  return merged;
}

function componentValue(
  confidence:
    ConfidenceAssessment,
  kind:
    ConfidenceComponentKind,
): number | undefined {
  return confidence.components
    .find(
      (component) =>
        component.kind ===
        kind,
    )
    ?.value;
}

function componentQuality(
  confidence:
    ConfidenceAssessment,
  kind:
    ConfidenceComponentKind,
):
  | "strong"
  | "moderate"
  | "weak"
  | "missing"
  | undefined {
  return confidence.components
    .find(
      (component) =>
        component.kind ===
        kind,
    )
    ?.evidenceQuality;
}

function eligibleAlternates(
  modelRoute:
    ModelRouteDecision,
): RankedModelCandidate[] {
  const currentId =
    modelRoute.selected
      ?.candidate.id;

  return modelRoute.ranking
    .filter(
      (entry) =>
        entry.eligible &&
        entry.candidate.id !==
          currentId,
    )
    .sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) => {
        if (
          left.score !==
          right.score
        ) {
          return (
            right.score -
            left.score
          );
        }

        if (
          left.confidence !==
          right.confidence
        ) {
          return (
            right.confidence -
            left.confidence
          );
        }

        return left.candidate.id
          .localeCompare(
            right.candidate.id,
          );
      },
    );
}

function nextReasoningEffort(
  current:
    EscalationReasoningEffort,
  maximum:
    EscalationReasoningEffort,
):
  | EscalationReasoningEffort
  | undefined {
  const currentIndex =
    REASONING_ORDER
      .indexOf(
        current,
      );

  const maximumIndex =
    REASONING_ORDER
      .indexOf(
        maximum,
      );

  if (
    currentIndex <
      0 ||
    maximumIndex <
      0
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    currentIndex >=
    maximumIndex
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return REASONING_ORDER[
    currentIndex +
    1
  ];
}

function findingCodes(
  verification:
    CriticVerificationResult,
): Set<string> {
  return new Set(
    verification.findings
      .map(
        (finding) =>
          finding.code,
      ),
  );
}

function directive(
  input: Omit<
    EscalationDirective,
    "mayExecute"
    | "authorityGranted"
  >,
): EscalationDirective {
  return {
    ...input,
    mayExecute:
      false,
    authorityGranted:
      false,
  };
}

function uniqueAlternatives(
  values:
    EscalationDirective[],
  selected:
    EscalationDirective,
): EscalationDirective[] {
  const seen =
    new Set<string>([
      `${selected.kind}:${selected.targetModelId ?? ""}:${selected.nextReasoningEffort ?? ""}`,
    ]);

  const output:
    EscalationDirective[] =
      [];

  for (
    const value
    of values
  ) {
    const key =
      `${value.kind}:${value.targetModelId ?? ""}:${value.nextReasoningEffort ?? ""}`;

    if (
      seen.has(
        key,
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(
      key,
    );

    output.push(
      value,
    );
  }

  return output;
}

export class EscalationEngine {
  decide(
    input: {
      confidence:
        ConfidenceAssessment;

      verification:
        CriticVerificationResult;

      orchestration:
        MultiModelOrchestrationResult;

      modelRoute:
        ModelRouteDecision;

      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;

      context?:
        EscalationContext;

      policy?:
        Partial<
          EscalationPolicy
        >;
    },
  ): EscalationDecision {
    const policy =
      policyWithOverrides(
        input.policy,
      );

    const context =
      input.context ??
      {};

    const attemptCount =
      nonNegativeInteger(
        context.attemptCount,
        0,
        "attemptCount",
      );

    const maximumAttempts =
      positiveInteger(
        context.maxAttempts,
        policy.maxAttempts,
        "context.maxAttempts",
      );

    const attemptExhausted =
      attemptCount >=
      maximumAttempts;

    const allowRetry =
      context.allowRetry ??
      true;

    const allowContextEnrichment =
      context
        .allowContextEnrichment ??
      true;

    const allowModelSwitch =
      context.allowModelSwitch ??
      true;

    const allowReasoningIncrease =
      context
        .allowReasoningIncrease ??
      true;

    const allowCritic =
      context.allowCritic ??
      true;

    const allowHumanClarification =
      context
        .allowHumanClarification ??
      true;

    const unresolved =
      (
        context
          .unresolvedQuestions ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (question) =>
            question.question
              .trim()
              .length >
            0,
        );

    const blockingQuestions =
      unresolved.filter(
        (question) =>
          question.blocking,
      );

    const contextGapCount =
      nonNegativeInteger(
        context.contextGapCount,
        0,
        "contextGapCount",
      );

    const transientFailureCount =
      nonNegativeInteger(
        context.transientFailureCount,
        0,
        "transientFailureCount",
      );

    const modelFailureCount =
      nonNegativeInteger(
        context.modelFailureCount,
        0,
        "modelFailureCount",
      );

    const findingSet =
      findingCodes(
        input.verification,
      );

    const evidenceValue =
      componentValue(
        input.confidence,
        "evidence-quality",
      );

    const evidenceQuality =
      componentQuality(
        input.confidence,
        "evidence-quality",
      );

    const historyValue =
      componentValue(
        input.confidence,
        "model-history",
      );

    const disagreementValue =
      componentValue(
        input.confidence,
        "disagreement",
      );

    const noveltyValue =
      componentValue(
        input.confidence,
        "task-novelty",
      );

    const uncertaintyValue =
      componentValue(
        input.confidence,
        "uncertainty",
      );

    const criticGapDetected =
      (
        input.verification
          .independentCritic
          .required &&
        !input.verification
          .independentCritic
          .parsed
      ) ||
      findingSet.has(
        "independent-critic-executor-missing",
      ) ||
      findingSet.has(
        "independent-critic-candidate-missing",
      ) ||
      findingSet.has(
        "critic-unavailable",
      );

    const contextGapDetected =
      contextGapCount >
        0 ||
      (
        context.contextCanBeEnriched ===
          true &&
        (
          evidenceQuality ===
            "missing" ||
          evidenceQuality ===
            "weak" ||
          (
            evidenceValue !==
              undefined &&
            evidenceValue <
              policy
                .contextEnrichmentThreshold
          ) ||
          findingSet.has(
            "missing-evidence-references",
          ) ||
          findingSet.has(
            "constraint-coverage-missing",
          ) ||
          findingSet.has(
            "factual-coverage-missing",
          )
        )
      );

    const transientFailureDetected =
      transientFailureCount >
        0 ||
      input.orchestration
        .status ===
        "failed" ||
      input.orchestration
        .status ===
        "partial";

    const disagreementHigh =
      disagreementValue !==
        undefined &&
      disagreementValue <
        0.45;

    const historyWeak =
      historyValue !==
        undefined &&
      historyValue <
        0.45;

    const modelUnderperformanceDetected =
      modelFailureCount >
        0 ||
      disagreementHigh ||
      historyWeak;

    const noveltyHigh =
      noveltyValue !==
        undefined &&
      noveltyValue <
        0.45;

    const uncertaintyHigh =
      uncertaintyValue !==
        undefined &&
      uncertaintyValue <
        0.45;

    const currentReasoningEffort =
      context.currentReasoningEffort ??
      "standard";

    const maximumReasoningEffort =
      context.maxReasoningEffort ??
      "maximum";

    const increasedEffort =
      nextReasoningEffort(
        currentReasoningEffort,
        maximumReasoningEffort,
      );

    const reasoningDepthGapDetected =
      Boolean(
        increasedEffort,
      ) &&
      (
        noveltyHigh ||
        uncertaintyHigh ||
        (
          input.confidence.score <
            policy
              .reasoningIncreaseThreshold &&
          input.reasoningContract
            .taskProfile
            .reasoningDepth ===
            "very-deep"
        )
      );

    const blockingAmbiguityDetected =
      context.humanClarificationNeeded ===
        true ||
      blockingQuestions.length >
        0;

    const confidenceSufficient =
      input.confidence.score >=
        policy.acceptanceThreshold &&
      input.verification
        .verdict ===
        "pass" &&
      input.verification
        .trustedConclusion &&
      input.orchestration
        .status ===
        "completed";

    const candidates:
      EscalationDirective[] =
        [];

    if (
      blockingAmbiguityDetected &&
      allowHumanClarification
    ) {
      const questions =
        (
          blockingQuestions.length >
            0
            ? blockingQuestions
            : unresolved
        )
          .slice(
            0,
            policy
              .maxClarificationQuestions,
          )
          .map(
            (question) =>
              question.question
                .trim(),
          );

      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "request-human-clarification",
          reasonCode:
            "blocking-ambiguity",
          summary:
            "Request human clarification because unresolved ambiguity blocks reliable continuation.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Present only the unresolved questions needed to continue.",
            "Preserve the current cognitive artifact while waiting for clarification.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          clarificationQuestions:
            questions,
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      contextGapDetected &&
      allowContextEnrichment
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "enrich-context",
          reasonCode:
            "context-insufficient",
          summary:
            "Retrieve or assemble additional relevant context/evidence before reasoning again.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Use OH-4K context retrieval rules.",
            "Preserve source provenance and authority boundaries.",
            "Do not treat retrieved context as permission to act.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      criticGapDetected &&
      allowCritic &&
      context.criticAvailable ===
        true
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "invoke-critic",
          reasonCode:
            "verification-critic-needed",
          summary:
            "Run the missing independent critic verification required by the active reasoning contract.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Critic candidate must be independent from the final-source model.",
            "Critic candidate must satisfy privacy and availability gates.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          criticCandidateId:
            context
              .criticCandidateId,
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      transientFailureDetected &&
      allowRetry &&
      !attemptExhausted
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "retry",
          reasonCode:
            "transient-failure",
          summary:
            "Retry the bounded cognitive operation because the current failure pattern may be transient.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Retry remains inside the configured attempt budget.",
            "Reuse authoritative inputs unless fresher context is explicitly gathered.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          attemptNumber:
            attemptCount +
            1,
        }),
      );
    }

    const alternate =
      eligibleAlternates(
        input.modelRoute,
      )[0];

    if (
      modelUnderperformanceDetected &&
      allowModelSwitch &&
      alternate &&
      input.confidence.score <
        policy.modelSwitchThreshold
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "switch-model",
          reasonCode:
            "model-underperforming",
          summary:
            "Switch to the next eligible OH-4M-ranked model because current-model evidence indicates underperformance or disagreement.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Use only an OH-4M eligible alternate candidate.",
            "Re-run the applicable strategy and verification steps after switching.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          targetModelId:
            alternate
              .candidate.id,
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      reasoningDepthGapDetected &&
      allowReasoningIncrease &&
      increasedEffort &&
      input.confidence.score <
        policy
          .reasoningIncreaseThreshold
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "increase-reasoning-effort",
          reasonCode:
            "reasoning-depth-insufficient",
          summary:
            "Increase bounded reasoning effort because novelty, uncertainty, or required reasoning depth exceeds the current setting.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Remain inside the active reasoning contract pass budget.",
            "Do not treat increased reasoning effort as authority to act.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          nextReasoningEffort:
            increasedEffort,
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      allowRetry &&
      !attemptExhausted &&
      input.confidence.score <
        policy.retryThreshold
    ) {
      candidates.push(
        directive({
          kind:
            "retry",
          reasonCode:
            "low-confidence-retry",
          summary:
            "Retry the cognitive pass because confidence remains below the bounded retry threshold and no stronger prerequisite necessarily blocks continuation.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Retry remains inside the configured attempt budget.",
            "Re-run downstream verification after retry.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
          attemptNumber:
            attemptCount +
            1,
        }),
      );
    }

    let selected:
      EscalationDirective;

    if (
      confidenceSufficient
    ) {
      selected =
        directive({
          kind:
            "none",
          reasonCode:
            "confidence-sufficient",
          summary:
            "No escalation is required; confidence and verification satisfy the acceptance threshold.",
          prerequisites:
            [],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
        });
    } else if (
      candidates.length >
        0
    ) {
      selected =
        candidates[0];
    } else if (
      attemptExhausted
    ) {
      selected =
        directive({
          kind:
            "stop-safely",
          reasonCode:
            "attempt-budget-exhausted",
          summary:
            "Stop safely because the configured cognitive attempt budget is exhausted and no higher-priority recovery path is available.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Surface the unresolved limitation and preserve the last verified/auditable artifacts.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
        });
    } else {
      selected =
        directive({
          kind:
            "stop-safely",
          reasonCode:
            "no-safe-recovery-path",
          summary:
            "Stop safely because confidence is insufficient and no permitted recovery path is currently available.",
          prerequisites: [
            "Surface the unresolved limitation instead of fabricating certainty.",
          ],
          preservesCurrentArtifact:
            true,
        });
    }

    const alternatives =
      uniqueAlternatives(
        candidates.slice(
          selected.kind ===
            "none"
            ? 0
            : 1,
        ),
        selected,
      );

    const warnings:
      string[] =
        [];

    if (
      input.confidence
        .dataQuality
        .missingSignals
        .length >
      0
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        `Confidence assessment is missing signals: ${input.confidence.dataQuality.missingSignals.join(", ")}.`,
      );
    }

    if (
      selected.kind ===
        "stop-safely"
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "Escalation cannot recover automatically under the current evidence, permissions, or attempt budget.",
      );
    }

    if (
      selected.kind ===
        "switch-model" &&
      selected.targetModelId
    ) {
      warnings.push(
        "OH-4V selects an alternate model directive only; it does not mutate OH-4M routing state or execute the model.",
      );
    }

    return {
      version:
        ESCALATION_ENGINE_VERSION,
      decidedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      required:
        selected.kind !==
        "none",
      confidence: {
        score:
          input.confidence
            .score,
        percentage:
          input.confidence
            .percentage,
        band:
          input.confidence
            .band,
        acceptanceThreshold:
          policy
            .acceptanceThreshold,
      },
      verificationVerdict:
        input.verification
          .verdict,
      selected,
      alternatives,
      attemptBudget: {
        current:
          attemptCount,
        maximum:
          maximumAttempts,
        exhausted:
          attemptExhausted,
      },
      signals: {
        contextGapDetected,
        criticGapDetected,
        transientFailureDetected,
        modelUnderperformanceDetected,
        reasoningDepthGapDetected,
        blockingAmbiguityDetected,
      },
      warnings,
      provenance: {
        confidenceEngineVersion:
          input.confidence
            .version,
        criticVerificationVersion:
          input.verification
            .version,
        orchestratorVersion:
          input.orchestration
            .version,
        modelRouterVersion:
          input.modelRoute
            .version,
        reasoningContractVersion:
          input
            .reasoningContract
            .version,
      },
      invariants: {
        escalationExecuted:
          false,
        toolsExecuted:
          false,
        actionsExecuted:
          false,
        authorityBypassed:
          false,
        modelRouteMutated:
          false,
        verificationMutated:
          false,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultEscalationEngine =
  new EscalationEngine();

export function getEscalationEngine():
  EscalationEngine {
  return defaultEscalationEngine;
}

============================================================
FILE: packages\models\src\authorityBridge.ts
============================================================
import type {
  CognitiveActionEffect,
  CognitiveActionRisk,
  CognitiveAutonomyMode,
  CognitiveGovernanceSnapshot,
  CognitivePermissionState,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/cognition/actionTypes";

import type {
  ExecutionRiskLevel,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/execution/types";

import type {
  ExecutionApprovalMode,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/execution/riskPolicy";

import type {
  ConfidenceAssessment,
} from "./confidenceEngine";

import type {
  CriticVerificationResult,
} from "./criticVerificationLayer";

import type {
  EscalationDecision,
} from "./escalationEngine";

import type {
  ReasoningContract,
} from "./reasoningContracts";

export const AUTHORITY_BRIDGE_VERSION =
  "oh4w.v1";

export type AuthorityDecisionStatus =
  | "permitted"
  | "requires-approval"
  | "denied"
  | "blocked"
  | "advisory";

export type ResponsibilityOwner =
  | "user"
  | "chernobog"
  | "shared"
  | "external"
  | "unknown";

export type AuthorityApprovalStatus =
  | "not-required"
  | "pending"
  | "approved"
  | "denied";

export interface AuthorityResponsibility {
  owner:
    ResponsibilityOwner;

  ownerId?: string;

  delegatedToChernobog?:
    boolean;

  sourceRef?: string;
}

export interface AuthorityApprovalSnapshot {
  status:
    AuthorityApprovalStatus;

  approvalId?: string;

  approvedAt?: string;
  deniedAt?: string;
}

export interface CognitiveAuthorityProposal {
  id: string;

  description: string;

  capability?: string;

  effect:
    CognitiveActionEffect;

  risk:
    CognitiveActionRisk;

  reversible:
    boolean;

  requiresUserInput?:
    boolean;

  requestedExecutionRisk?:
    ExecutionRiskLevel;

  responsibility?:
    AuthorityResponsibility;
}

export type AuthorityReasonCode =
  | "verification-not-trusted"
  | "escalation-unresolved"
  | "safety-block"
  | "permission-denied"
  | "autonomy-disabled"
  | "advisory-only"
  | "requires-user-input"
  | "responsibility-not-delegated"
  | "critical-risk"
  | "high-risk"
  | "irreversible-side-effect"
  | "execution-policy-blocked"
  | "permission-confirmation"
  | "execution-approval-required"
  | "approval-pending"
  | "approval-denied"
  | "approval-satisfied"
  | "notice-required"
  | "bounded-authority-permits"
  | "reasoning-cannot-self-authorize";

export interface AuthorityReason {
  code:
    AuthorityReasonCode;

  detail: string;

  source:
    | "verification"
    | "escalation"
    | "safety"
    | "governance"
    | "responsibility"
    | "risk"
    | "execution-policy"
    | "approval"
    | "reasoning-contract";
}

export interface AuthorityBridgeDecision {
  version: string;
  decidedAt: string;

  proposalId: string;

  status:
    AuthorityDecisionStatus;

  permittedToPropose:
    boolean;

  permittedToExecute:
    boolean;

  executionRisk:
    ExecutionRiskLevel;

  executionApprovalMode:
    ExecutionApprovalMode;

  approval: {
    required: boolean;
    satisfied: boolean;
    status:
      AuthorityApprovalStatus;
    approvalId?: string;
  };

  governance: {
    permission:
      CognitivePermissionState;

    autonomy:
      CognitiveAutonomyMode;

    userInteractionAvailable:
      boolean;
  };

  responsibility: {
    owner:
      ResponsibilityOwner;

    delegatedToChernobog:
      boolean;

    ownerId?: string;
    sourceRef?: string;
  };

  reasons:
    AuthorityReason[];

  safetyBlocks:
    string[];

  confidence: {
    score: number;
    percentage: number;
    verificationTrusted:
      boolean;
  };

  unresolvedEscalation?:
    EscalationDecision[
      "selected"
    ]["kind"];

  provenance: {
    confidenceEngineVersion:
      string;

    criticVerificationVersion:
      string;

    escalationEngineVersion:
      string;

    reasoningContractVersion:
      string;

    responsibilitySourceRef?:
      string;
  };

  invariants: {
    cognitionCanSelfAuthorize:
      false;

    confidenceCanUpgradePermission:
      false;

    approvalCanOverrideDenial:
      false;

    approvalCanOverrideSafetyBlock:
      false;

    actionExecuted:
      false;

    executionTaskCreated:
      false;
  };
}

const EXECUTION_RISK_ORDER:
  Record<
    ExecutionRiskLevel,
    number
  > = {
    safe:
      0,

    notice:
      1,

    approval_required:
      2,

    blocked:
      3,
  };

function maxExecutionRisk(
  left:
    ExecutionRiskLevel,
  right:
    ExecutionRiskLevel,
): ExecutionRiskLevel {
  return (
    EXECUTION_RISK_ORDER[
      left
    ] >=
    EXECUTION_RISK_ORDER[
      right
    ]
  )
    ? left
    : right;
}

function executionModeForRisk(
  risk:
    ExecutionRiskLevel,
): ExecutionApprovalMode {
  switch (
    risk
  ) {
    case "safe":
      return "auto";

    case "notice":
      return "notice";

    case "approval_required":
      return "approval";

    case "blocked":
      return "blocked";
  }
}

function derivedRiskForProposal(
  proposal:
    CognitiveAuthorityProposal,
): ExecutionRiskLevel {
  if (
    proposal.risk ===
    "critical"
  ) {
    return "blocked";
  }

  if (
    proposal.risk ===
    "high"
  ) {
    return "approval_required";
  }

  if (
    (
      proposal.effect ===
        "write" ||
      proposal.effect ===
        "external"
    ) &&
    !proposal.reversible
  ) {
    return "approval_required";
  }

  if (
    proposal.effect ===
      "write"
  ) {
    return "approval_required";
  }

  if (
    proposal.effect ===
      "external"
  ) {
    return "notice";
  }

  return "safe";
}

function effectiveExecutionRisk(
  proposal:
    CognitiveAuthorityProposal,
): ExecutionRiskLevel {
  const derived =
    derivedRiskForProposal(
      proposal,
    );

  return proposal
    .requestedExecutionRisk
    ? maxExecutionRisk(
        derived,
        proposal
          .requestedExecutionRisk,
      )
    : derived;
}

function reason(
  reasons:
    AuthorityReason[],
  code:
    AuthorityReasonCode,
  detail:
    string,
  source:
    AuthorityReason[
      "source"
    ],
): void {
  reasons.push({
    code,
    detail,
    source,
  });
}

function normalizedResponsibility(
  responsibility:
    AuthorityResponsibility
    | undefined,
): AuthorityBridgeDecision[
  "responsibility"
] {
  const owner =
    responsibility
      ?.owner ??
    "unknown";

  return {
    owner,
    delegatedToChernobog:
      responsibility
        ?.delegatedToChernobog ??
      owner ===
        "chernobog",
    ownerId:
      responsibility
        ?.ownerId,
    sourceRef:
      responsibility
        ?.sourceRef,
  };
}

function approvalStatus(
  approval:
    AuthorityApprovalSnapshot
    | undefined,
): AuthorityApprovalStatus {
  return approval
    ?.status ??
    "not-required";
}

export class AuthorityBridge {
  decide(
    input: {
      proposal:
        CognitiveAuthorityProposal;

      governance:
        CognitiveGovernanceSnapshot;

      reasoningContract:
        ReasoningContract;

      verification:
        CriticVerificationResult;

      confidence:
        ConfidenceAssessment;

      escalation:
        EscalationDecision;

      approval?:
        AuthorityApprovalSnapshot;

      safetyBlocks?:
        string[];
    },
  ): AuthorityBridgeDecision {
    const reasons:
      AuthorityReason[] =
        [];

    const proposal =
      structuredClone(
        input.proposal,
      );

    const governance =
      structuredClone(
        input.governance,
      );

    const safetyBlocks =
      [
        ...new Set(
          (
            input.safetyBlocks ??
            []
          )
            .map(
              (entry) =>
                entry.trim(),
            )
            .filter(Boolean),
        ),
      ];

    const responsibility =
      normalizedResponsibility(
        proposal
          .responsibility,
      );

    const approval =
      input.approval;

    const currentApprovalStatus =
      approvalStatus(
        approval,
      );

    let executionRisk =
      effectiveExecutionRisk(
        proposal,
      );

    let hardBlocked =
      false;

    let denied =
      false;

    let advisoryOnly =
      false;

    let approvalRequired =
      executionRisk ===
      "approval_required";

    if (
      !input.reasoningContract
        .authority
        .reasoningMayAuthorizeActions
    ) {
      reason(
        reasons,
        "reasoning-cannot-self-authorize",
        "The active reasoning contract explicitly prevents cognition from authorizing its own proposed actions.",
        "reasoning-contract",
      );
    }

    if (
      !input.verification
        .trustedConclusion ||
      input.verification
        .verdict !==
        "pass"
    ) {
      hardBlocked =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "verification-not-trusted",
        `OH-4T verdict=${input.verification.verdict}; untrusted cognitive conclusions cannot cross into execution authority.`,
        "verification",
      );
    }

    if (
      input.escalation
        .required ||
      input.escalation
        .selected.kind !==
        "none"
    ) {
      hardBlocked =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "escalation-unresolved",
        `OH-4V still requires "${input.escalation.selected.kind}" before the cognitive result is ready for authority consideration.`,
        "escalation",
      );
    }

    if (
      safetyBlocks.length >
      0
    ) {
      hardBlocked =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "safety-block",
        `Safety policy blocks this proposal: ${safetyBlocks.join("; ")}.`,
        "safety",
      );
    }

    if (
      governance.permission ===
      "deny"
    ) {
      denied =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "permission-denied",
        "The existing Chernobog governance snapshot denies this capability.",
        "governance",
      );
    }

    if (
      governance.autonomy ===
      "disabled"
    ) {
      advisoryOnly =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "autonomy-disabled",
        "Autonomous execution is disabled by the existing governance snapshot.",
        "governance",
      );
    } else if (
      governance.autonomy ===
      "advisory"
    ) {
      advisoryOnly =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "advisory-only",
        "The existing governance snapshot allows recommendations but not autonomous execution.",
        "governance",
      );
    }

    if (
      proposal.requiresUserInput
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "requires-user-input",
        "The proposal requires user-supplied information before execution can proceed.",
        "governance",
      );
    }

    if (
      !responsibility
        .delegatedToChernobog &&
      responsibility.owner !==
        "chernobog"
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "responsibility-not-delegated",
        `Responsibility is owned by ${responsibility.owner}; execution authority has not been delegated to Chernobog.`,
        "responsibility",
      );
    }

    if (
      proposal.risk ===
      "critical"
    ) {
      hardBlocked =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "critical-risk",
        "Critical-risk cognitive proposals are blocked from autonomous execution.",
        "risk",
      );
    } else if (
      proposal.risk ===
      "high"
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        maxExecutionRisk(
          executionRisk,
          "approval_required",
        );

      reason(
        reasons,
        "high-risk",
        "High-risk cognitive proposals require explicit approval.",
        "risk",
      );
    }

    if (
      (
        proposal.effect ===
          "write" ||
        proposal.effect ===
          "external"
      ) &&
      !proposal.reversible
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        maxExecutionRisk(
          executionRisk,
          "approval_required",
        );

      reason(
        reasons,
        "irreversible-side-effect",
        "Irreversible write/external effects require explicit approval.",
        "risk",
      );
    }

    if (
      proposal
        .requestedExecutionRisk ===
        "blocked"
    ) {
      hardBlocked =
        true;

      executionRisk =
        "blocked";

      reason(
        reasons,
        "execution-policy-blocked",
        "The existing execution-risk classification marks this action blocked.",
        "execution-policy",
      );
    } else if (
      proposal
        .requestedExecutionRisk ===
        "approval_required"
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "execution-approval-required",
        "The existing execution-risk classification requires approval.",
        "execution-policy",
      );
    }

    if (
      governance.permission ===
      "confirm"
    ) {
      approvalRequired =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "permission-confirmation",
        "The existing governance permission state requires confirmation before execution.",
        "governance",
      );
    }

    let approvalSatisfied =
      !approvalRequired;

    if (
      approvalRequired
    ) {
      if (
        currentApprovalStatus ===
        "approved"
      ) {
        approvalSatisfied =
          true;

        reason(
          reasons,
          "approval-satisfied",
          "The required explicit approval is recorded as approved.",
          "approval",
        );
      } else if (
        currentApprovalStatus ===
        "denied"
      ) {
        denied =
          true;

        reason(
          reasons,
          "approval-denied",
          "The required approval was explicitly denied.",
          "approval",
        );
      } else {
        reason(
          reasons,
          "approval-pending",
          "Explicit approval is required and has not yet been satisfied.",
          "approval",
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      executionRisk ===
      "notice"
    ) {
      reason(
        reasons,
        "notice-required",
        "The existing execution-risk vocabulary requires notice before execution.",
        "execution-policy",
      );
    }

    const boundedAutonomy =
      governance.autonomy ===
      "bounded";

    const permissionAllows =
      governance.permission ===
        "allow" ||
      (
        governance.permission ===
          "confirm" &&
        approvalSatisfied
      );

    let status:
      AuthorityDecisionStatus;

    let permittedToExecute =
      false;

    if (
      hardBlocked
    ) {
      status =
        "blocked";
    } else if (
      denied
    ) {
      status =
        "denied";
    } else if (
      advisoryOnly ||
      !boundedAutonomy
    ) {
      status =
        "advisory";
    } else if (
      !permissionAllows
    ) {
      status =
        approvalRequired
          ? "requires-approval"
          : "denied";
    } else if (
      approvalRequired &&
      !approvalSatisfied
    ) {
      status =
        "requires-approval";
    } else {
      status =
        "permitted";

      permittedToExecute =
        true;

      reason(
        reasons,
        "bounded-authority-permits",
        "Existing governance, responsibility, risk, verification, escalation, and approval gates permit this proposal to cross the authority boundary.",
        "governance",
      );
    }

    if (
      status ===
        "blocked" ||
      status ===
        "denied"
    ) {
      executionRisk =
        "blocked";
    } else if (
      status ===
        "requires-approval"
    ) {
      executionRisk =
        maxExecutionRisk(
          executionRisk,
          "approval_required",
        );
    }

    return {
      version:
        AUTHORITY_BRIDGE_VERSION,
      decidedAt:
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
      proposalId:
        proposal.id,
      status,
      permittedToPropose:
        !hardBlocked &&
        !denied,
      permittedToExecute,
      executionRisk,
      executionApprovalMode:
        executionModeForRisk(
          executionRisk,
        ),
      approval: {
        required:
          approvalRequired,
        satisfied:
          approvalSatisfied,
        status:
          currentApprovalStatus,
        approvalId:
          approval
            ?.approvalId,
      },
      governance: {
        permission:
          governance
            .permission,
        autonomy:
          governance
            .autonomy,
        userInteractionAvailable:
          governance
            .userInteractionAvailable,
      },
      responsibility,
      reasons:
        structuredClone(
          reasons,
        ),
      safetyBlocks,
      confidence: {
        score:
          input.confidence
            .score,
        percentage:
          input.confidence
            .percentage,
        verificationTrusted:
          input.verification
            .trustedConclusion,
      },
      unresolvedEscalation:
        input.escalation
          .selected.kind ===
          "none"
          ? undefined
          : input.escalation
              .selected.kind,
      provenance: {
        confidenceEngineVersion:
          input.confidence
            .version,
        criticVerificationVersion:
          input.verification
            .version,
        escalationEngineVersion:
          input.escalation
            .version,
        reasoningContractVersion:
          input
            .reasoningContract
            .version,
        responsibilitySourceRef:
          responsibility
            .sourceRef,
      },
      invariants: {
        cognitionCanSelfAuthorize:
          false,
        confidenceCanUpgradePermission:
          false,
        approvalCanOverrideDenial:
          false,
        approvalCanOverrideSafetyBlock:
          false,
        actionExecuted:
          false,
        executionTaskCreated:
          false,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultAuthorityBridge =
  new AuthorityBridge();

export function getAuthorityBridge():
  AuthorityBridge {
  return defaultAuthorityBridge;
}

FILE: packages\models\src\actionBridge.ts
