import {
  getToolCatalogSnapshot,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/execution/toolCapabilities";

import type {
  CreateExecutionTaskInput,
  ExecutionRiskLevel,
  ExecutionStepKind,
  ExecutionTaskCategory,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/execution/types";

import {
  getTool,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/tools/registry";

import type {
  AuthorityBridgeDecision,
} from "./authorityBridge";

import type {
  ConfidenceAssessment,
} from "./confidenceEngine";

import type {
  CriticVerificationResult,
} from "./criticVerificationLayer";

export const ACTION_BRIDGE_VERSION =
  "oh4x.v1";

export type ActionBridgeTargetKind =
  | "tool"
  | "agent";

export interface ActionBridgeTarget {
  kind: ActionBridgeTargetKind;
  name: string;
}

export interface ActionBridgeIntent {
  id: string;
  proposalId: string;
  target: ActionBridgeTarget;
  input: unknown;
  goal: string;
  description?: string;
  category?: ExecutionTaskCategory;
  stepKind?: ExecutionStepKind;
  postActionVerification?: {
    required: boolean;
    checks?: string[];
    expectedOutcome?: string;
  };
  idempotencyMaterial?: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export type ActionBridgeStatus =
  | "ready"
  | "approval-required"
  | "blocked";

export interface ActionBridgeExecutionBlueprint {
  category: ExecutionTaskCategory;
  input: string;
  goal: string;
  steps: NonNullable<CreateExecutionTaskInput["steps"]>;
  context: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export interface ActionBridgePacket {
  version: string;
  createdAt: string;
  status: ActionBridgeStatus;
  readyForExecution: boolean;
  idempotencyKey: string;
  proposalId: string;
  actionIntentId: string;
  target: ActionBridgeTarget;
  normalizedInput: unknown;
  authorityStatus: AuthorityBridgeDecision["status"];
  executionRisk: ExecutionRiskLevel;
  executionTask: ActionBridgeExecutionBlueprint;
  toolRequest?: {
    toolName: string;
    input: unknown;
    origin: "execution-task";
    executionHandlerAvailable: boolean;
  };
  agentRequest?: {
    agentId: string;
    input: unknown;
    handlerAction: string;
    handlerResolutionRequired: true;
  };
  postActionVerification: {
    required: boolean;
    checks: string[];
    expectedOutcome?: string;
    sourceVerificationTrusted: boolean;
    sourceConfidence: number;
  };
  provenance: {
    authorityBridgeVersion: string;
    criticVerificationVersion: string;
    confidenceEngineVersion: string;
    proposalId: string;
    actionIntentId: string;
  };
  warnings: string[];
  invariants: {
    toolInvoked: false;
    agentInvoked: false;
    executionStarted: false;
    authorityBypassed: false;
    riskReduced: false;
    approvalBypassed: false;
  };
}

function stableValue(value: unknown): unknown {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map(stableValue);
  }

  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
        .sort((left, right) => left[0].localeCompare(right[0]))
        .map(([key, entry]) => [key, stableValue(entry)]),
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function stableStringify(value: unknown): string {
  return JSON.stringify(stableValue(value));
}

function fnv1a32(text: string): string {
  let hash = 0x811c9dc5;

  for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index += 1) {
    hash ^= text.charCodeAt(index);
    hash = Math.imul(hash, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
  }

  return hash.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function normalizeTargetName(name: string): string {
  const normalized = name.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error("Action target name must be non-empty.");
  }

  return normalized;
}

function categoryFor(intent: ActionBridgeIntent): ExecutionTaskCategory {
  return intent.category ?? (intent.target.kind === "tool" ? "system_operation" : "unknown");
}

function stepKindFor(intent: ActionBridgeIntent): ExecutionStepKind {
  return intent.stepKind ?? (intent.target.kind === "tool" ? "tool" : "workflow");
}

function buildIdempotencyKey(
  authority: AuthorityBridgeDecision,
  intent: ActionBridgeIntent,
  normalizedTarget: string,
): string {
  return `oh4x:${fnv1a32(stableStringify({
    proposalId: authority.proposalId,
    actionIntentId: intent.id,
    target: {
      kind: intent.target.kind,
      name: normalizedTarget,
    },
    input: stableValue(intent.input),
    executionRisk: authority.executionRisk,
    extra: intent.idempotencyMaterial ?? null,
  }))}`;
}

function statusForAuthority(authority: AuthorityBridgeDecision): ActionBridgeStatus {
  if (authority.status === "permitted" && authority.permittedToExecute) {
    return "ready";
  }

  if (authority.status === "requires-approval") {
    return "approval-required";
  }

  return "blocked";
}

function validateAuthorityBinding(
  authority: AuthorityBridgeDecision,
  intent: ActionBridgeIntent,
): void {
  if (authority.proposalId !== intent.proposalId) {
    throw new Error(
      "Action intent proposalId does not match the OH-4W authority decision.",
    );
  }

  if (authority.status === "permitted" && !authority.permittedToExecute) {
    throw new Error(
      "OH-4W authority decision is internally inconsistent: permitted status without execution permission.",
    );
  }
}

function validateToolTarget(name: string): { executionHandlerAvailable: boolean } {
  if (!getTool(name)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Action Bridge target "${name}" is not a registered Chernobog tool.`,
    );
  }

  const capability = getToolCatalogSnapshot().tools.find(
    (entry) => entry.name === name,
  );

  return {
    executionHandlerAvailable: capability?.executionTaskAvailable ?? false,
  };
}

export class ActionBridge {
  translate(input: {
    authority: AuthorityBridgeDecision;
    intent: ActionBridgeIntent;
    verification: CriticVerificationResult;
    confidence: ConfidenceAssessment;
  }): ActionBridgePacket {
    validateAuthorityBinding(input.authority, input.intent);

    const targetName = normalizeTargetName(input.intent.target.name);
    const status = statusForAuthority(input.authority);
    const warnings: string[] = [];
    const risk = input.authority.executionRisk;
    const idempotencyKey = buildIdempotencyKey(
      input.authority,
      input.intent,
      targetName,
    );

    let executionHandlerAvailable = false;

    if (input.intent.target.kind === "tool") {
      executionHandlerAvailable = validateToolTarget(targetName).executionHandlerAvailable;

      if (!executionHandlerAvailable) {
        warnings.push(
          `Registered tool "${targetName}" has no execution-task handler coverage. The packet remains non-executable until runtime support exists.`,
        );
      }
    } else {
      warnings.push(
        `Agent target "${targetName}" requires a downstream execution handler; OH-4X does not invoke agents directly.`,
      );
    }

    if (status === "approval-required") {
      warnings.push(
        "The action packet is materialized for approval workflow only; it is not ready for execution.",
      );
    }

    if (status === "blocked") {
      warnings.push(
        `OH-4W authority status "${input.authority.status}" prevents execution readiness.`,
      );
    }

    const actionName =
      input.intent.target.kind === "tool"
        ? targetName
        : `agent:${targetName}`;

    const executionTask: ActionBridgeExecutionBlueprint = {
      category: categoryFor(input.intent),
      input: input.intent.description ?? input.intent.goal,
      goal: input.intent.goal,
      steps: [
        {
          kind: stepKindFor(input.intent),
          label:
            input.intent.description ??
            `Execute ${input.intent.target.kind} ${targetName}`,
          action: actionName,
          input: structuredClone(input.intent.input),
          risk,
        },
      ],
      context: {
        oh4x: true,
        idempotencyKey,
        proposalId: input.authority.proposalId,
        actionIntentId: input.intent.id,
        authorityStatus: input.authority.status,
        authorityDecisionVersion: input.authority.version,
        sourceVerificationVersion: input.verification.version,
        sourceConfidenceVersion: input.confidence.version,
        postActionVerificationRequired:
          input.intent.postActionVerification?.required ?? false,
        postActionVerificationChecks: structuredClone(
          input.intent.postActionVerification?.checks ?? [],
        ),
        ...(input.intent.metadata ?? {}),
      },
    };

    // Tools may become runtime-ready only when authority permits and the
    // existing execution-task layer has handler coverage. Agent targets stay
    // fail-closed until a concrete downstream agent handler exists.
    const readyForExecution =
      status === "ready" &&
      risk !== "blocked" &&
      input.intent.target.kind === "tool" &&
      executionHandlerAvailable;

    if (status === "ready" && !readyForExecution) {
      warnings.push(
        "Authority permits the proposal, but runtime target coverage is incomplete; readyForExecution remains false.",
      );
    }

    return {
      version: ACTION_BRIDGE_VERSION,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status,
      readyForExecution,
      idempotencyKey,
      proposalId: input.authority.proposalId,
      actionIntentId: input.intent.id,
      target: {
        kind: input.intent.target.kind,
        name: targetName,
      },
      normalizedInput: structuredClone(input.intent.input),
      authorityStatus: input.authority.status,
      executionRisk: risk,
      executionTask,
      toolRequest:
        input.intent.target.kind === "tool"
          ? {
              toolName: targetName,
              input: structuredClone(input.intent.input),
              origin: "execution-task",
              executionHandlerAvailable,
            }
          : undefined,
      agentRequest:
        input.intent.target.kind === "agent"
          ? {
              agentId: targetName,
              input: structuredClone(input.intent.input),
              handlerAction: actionName,
              handlerResolutionRequired: true,
            }
          : undefined,
      postActionVerification: {
        required: input.intent.postActionVerification?.required ?? false,
        checks: structuredClone(input.intent.postActionVerification?.checks ?? []),
        expectedOutcome: input.intent.postActionVerification?.expectedOutcome,
        sourceVerificationTrusted: input.verification.trustedConclusion,
        sourceConfidence: input.confidence.score,
      },
      provenance: {
        authorityBridgeVersion: input.authority.version,
        criticVerificationVersion: input.verification.version,
        confidenceEngineVersion: input.confidence.version,
        proposalId: input.authority.proposalId,
        actionIntentId: input.intent.id,
      },
      warnings,
      invariants: {
        toolInvoked: false,
        agentInvoked: false,
        executionStarted: false,
        authorityBypassed: false,
        riskReduced: false,
        approvalBypassed: false,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultActionBridge = new ActionBridge();

export function getActionBridge(): ActionBridge {
  return defaultActionBridge;
}

import {
  createLearningExperience,
  createLearningFeedbackObservation,
  createLearningOutcomeObservation,
  getChernobogLearningRuntime,
  type EvaluatedLearningExperience,
  type LearningExperience,
  type LearningExperienceSource,
  type LearningFeedbackKind,
  type LearningOutcomeStatus,
  type LearningPatternCandidate,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/learning";

export const COGNITIVE_MEMORY_VERSION =
  "oh4y.v1";

export type CognitiveMemoryOutcomeStatus =
  LearningOutcomeStatus;

export type CognitiveMemoryFeedbackKind =
  Exclude<
    LearningFeedbackKind,
    "none"
  >;

export interface CognitiveMemoryOutcome {
  status:
    CognitiveMemoryOutcomeStatus;

  score?: number;

  confidence?: number;

  detail?: string;

  evidenceEventIds?: string[];

  evidenceWorldStateKeys?:
    string[];
}

export interface CognitiveMemoryFeedback {
  kind:
    CognitiveMemoryFeedbackKind;

  confidence?: number;

  detail: string;
}

export interface CognitiveMemoryExecutionSummary {
  actionIntentId?: string;

  idempotencyKey?: string;

  targetKind?:
    "tool"
    | "agent";

  targetName?: string;

  status?:
    "not-run"
    | "completed"
    | "failed"
    | "partial"
    | "blocked";

  failureKind?: string;

  detail?: string;
}

  taskId: string;

  projectId?: string;

  taskKind: string;

  domain?: string;

  modelId?: string;

  strategy?: string;

  verificationVerdict?: string;

  confidenceScore?: number;

  escalationKind?: string;

export interface CognitiveMemoryRecordInput {
  occurredAt: string;

  recordedAt?: string;

  source?:
    LearningExperienceSource;

    CognitiveMemoryOutcome;

  feedback?:

export interface CognitiveMemoryRecordResult {

  recordedAt: string;

  experience:
    LearningExperience;

  evaluation:
    EvaluatedLearningExperience;

    totalPatterns: number;

    activeProjectPatterns:
      LearningPatternCandidate[];

    globalPatterns:
      LearningPatternCandidate[];

    rawExperienceCanOverrideGovernance:

function normalizeOptional(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
): string | undefined {
  const normalized =
    value?.trim();

  return normalized ||
    undefined;
}

function uniqueText(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (
        values ??
        []
      )
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ].sort();
}

function requireTimestamp(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const parsed =
    new Date(
      value,
    );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(
      parsed.getTime(),
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a valid timestamp.`,
    );
  }

  return parsed.toISOString();
}

function unitInterval(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  fallback:
    number,
  field: string,
): number {
  const normalized =
    value ??
    fallback;

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      normalized,
    ) ||
    normalized < 0 ||
    normalized > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function outcomeScore(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < -1 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "cognitiveMemory.outcome.score must be between -1 and 1.",
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function jsonSafe(
  value:
    Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >
    | undefined,
): Record<
  string,
  unknown
> {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      JSON.stringify(
        value ??
        {},
      ),
    ) as Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >;
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "cognitiveMemory.metadata must be JSON-safe.",
    );
  }
}

function normalizeTaskId(
  value: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      "cognitiveMemory.artifacts.taskId must not be empty.",
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function normalizeTaskKind(
  value: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      "cognitiveMemory.artifacts.taskKind must not be empty.",
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function normalizeKeyPart(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
  fallback: string,
): string {
  return (
    value
      ?.trim()
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /[^a-z0-9._:-]+/g,
        "-",
      )
      .replace(
        /^-+|-+$/g,
        "",
      ) ||
    fallback
  );
}

function subjectFor(
  artifacts:
    CognitiveMemoryArtifactRefs,
): string {
  const taskKind =
    normalizeKeyPart(
      artifacts.taskKind,
      "task",
    );

  const domain =
    normalizeKeyPart(
      artifacts.domain,
      "general",
    );

  const strategy =
    normalizeKeyPart(
      artifacts.strategy,
      "unknown-strategy",
    );

  const model =
    normalizeKeyPart(
      artifacts.modelId,
      "unknown-model",
    );

  return [
    `cognitive-task:${taskKind}`,
    `domain=${domain}`,
    `strategy=${strategy}`,
    `model=${model}`,
  ].join(
    "|",
  );
}

function experienceIdFor(
  taskId: string,
  occurredAt: string,
): string {
  return [
    "oh4y",
    taskId,
    occurredAt,
  ].join(
    ":",
  );
}

function chooseSource(
  input:
    CognitiveMemoryRecordInput,
): LearningExperienceSource {
  if (
    input.source
  ) {
    return input.source;
  }

  if (
    input.execution ||
    input.outcome
  ) {
    return "action-outcome";
  }

  if (
    input.feedback
  ) {
    return "user-feedback";
  }

  return "cognitive-cycle";
}

function normalizedNotes(
  values:
    string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return uniqueText(
    values,
  );
}

function experienceContext(
  input:
    CognitiveMemoryRecordInput,
): Record<
  string,
  unknown
> {
  const artifacts =
    input.artifacts;

  const projectId =
    normalizeOptional(
      artifacts.projectId,
    );

  const context:
    Record<
      string,
      unknown
    > = {
      cognitiveMemoryVersion:
        COGNITIVE_MEMORY_VERSION,

      taskId:
        normalizeTaskId(
          artifacts.taskId,
        ),

      taskKind:
        normalizeTaskKind(
          artifacts.taskKind,
        ),

      domain:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts.domain,
        ),

      modelId:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts.modelId,
        ),

      strategy:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts.strategy,
        ),

      verificationVerdict:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts
            .verificationVerdict,
        ),

      confidenceScore:
        artifacts
          .confidenceScore ===
          undefined
          ? undefined
          : unitInterval(
              artifacts
                .confidenceScore,
              0.5,
              "cognitiveMemory.artifacts.confidenceScore",
            ),

      escalationKind:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts
            .escalationKind,
        ),

      authorityStatus:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts
            .authorityStatus,
        ),

      actionStatus:
        normalizeOptional(
          artifacts
            .actionStatus,
        ),

      sourceVersions:
        structuredClone(
          artifacts
            .sourceVersions ??
          {},
        ),

      execution:
        structuredClone(
          input.execution ??
          {},
        ),

      notes:
        normalizedNotes(
          input.notes,
        ),

      ...jsonSafe(
        input.metadata,
      ),
    };

  if (
    projectId
  ) {
    context.projectId =
      projectId;
  }

  return context;
}

function evidenceConfidence(
  input:
    CognitiveMemoryRecordInput,
): number {
  if (
    input.outcome
      ?.confidence !==
    undefined
  ) {
    return unitInterval(
      input.outcome
        .confidence,
      0.5,
      "cognitiveMemory.outcome.confidence",
    );
  }

  if (
    input.artifacts
      .confidenceScore !==
    undefined
  ) {
    return unitInterval(
      input.artifacts
        .confidenceScore,
      0.5,
      "cognitiveMemory.artifacts.confidenceScore",
    );
  }

  return 0.5;
}

export class CognitiveMemory {
  async record(
    input:
      CognitiveMemoryRecordInput,
  ): Promise<
    CognitiveMemoryRecordResult
  > {
    const occurredAt =
      requireTimestamp(
        input.occurredAt,
        "cognitiveMemory.occurredAt",
      );

    const recordedAt =
      requireTimestamp(
        input.recordedAt ??
        new Date()
          .toISOString(),
        "cognitiveMemory.recordedAt",
      );

    if (
      new Date(
        recordedAt,
      ).getTime() <
      new Date(
        occurredAt,
      ).getTime()
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "cognitiveMemory.recordedAt must not be earlier than occurredAt.",
      );
    }

    const taskId =
      normalizeTaskId(
        input.artifacts
          .taskId,
      );

    const experienceId =
      experienceIdFor(
        taskId,
        occurredAt,
      );

    const experience =
      createLearningExperience(
        {
          id:
            experienceId,

          occurredAt,

          recordedAt,

          source:
            chooseSource(
              input,
            ),

          subject:
            subjectFor(
              input.artifacts,
            ),

          confidence:
            evidenceConfidence(
              input,
            ),

          outcome: {
            status:
              "unknown",
          },

          feedback: {
            kind:
              "none",
          },

          evidence: {
            eventIds:
              uniqueText(
                input.evidence
                  ?.eventIds,
              ),

            worldStateKeys:
              uniqueText(
                input.evidence
                  ?.worldStateKeys,
              ),

            cognitiveDecisionIds:
              uniqueText(
                input.evidence
                  ?.cognitiveDecisionIds,
              ),
          },

          context:
            experienceContext(
              input,
            ),
        },
        new Date(
          recordedAt,
        ),
      );

    const runtime =
      await getChernobogLearningRuntime();

    runtime.experiences
      .upsert(
        experience,
      );

    if (
      input.outcome
    ) {
      runtime.addOutcome(
        createLearningOutcomeObservation({
          id:
            `outcome:${experienceId}`,

          experienceId,

          observedAt:
            recordedAt,

          status:
            input.outcome
              .status,

          score:
            outcomeScore(
              input.outcome
                .score,
            ),

          confidence:
            unitInterval(
              input.outcome
                .confidence,
              evidenceConfidence(
                input,
              ),
              "cognitiveMemory.outcome.confidence",
            ),

          detail:
            normalizeOptional(
              input.outcome
                .detail,
            ),

          evidenceEventIds:
            uniqueText(
              input.outcome
                .evidenceEventIds ??
              input.evidence
                ?.eventIds,
            ),

          evidenceWorldStateKeys:
            uniqueText(
              input.outcome
                .evidenceWorldStateKeys ??
              input.evidence
                ?.worldStateKeys,
            ),
        }),
      );
    }

    if (
      input.feedback
    ) {
      const feedbackDetail =
        input.feedback
          .detail
          .trim();

      if (
        !feedbackDetail
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "cognitiveMemory.feedback.detail must not be empty.",
        );
      }

      runtime.addFeedback(
        createLearningFeedbackObservation({
          id:
            `feedback:${experienceId}`,

          experienceId,

          observedAt:
            recordedAt,

          kind:
            input.feedback
              .kind,

          confidence:
            unitInterval(
              input.feedback
                .confidence,
              1,
              "cognitiveMemory.feedback.confidence",
            ),

          detail:
            feedbackDetail,
        }),
      );
    }

    const evaluation =
      runtime
        .evaluateExperience(
          experienceId,
        );

    if (
      !evaluation
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `OH-4Y failed to evaluate recorded experience ${experienceId}.`,
      );
    }

    runtime.refreshPatterns();

    const snapshot =
      runtime.snapshot();

    const projectId =
      normalizeOptional(
        input.artifacts
          .projectId,
      );

    const activeProjectPatterns =
      snapshot.patterns
        .filter(
          (pattern) =>
            pattern.scope ===
              "project" &&
            pattern.projectId ===
              projectId,
        );

    const globalPatterns =
      snapshot.patterns
        .filter(
          (pattern) =>
            pattern.scope ===
            "global",
        );

    return {
      version:
        COGNITIVE_MEMORY_VERSION,

      recordedAt,

      experience:
        structuredClone(
          experience,
        ),

      evaluation:
        structuredClone(
          evaluation,
        ),

      patternRefresh: {
        totalPatterns:
          snapshot.patterns
            .length,

        activeProjectPatterns:
          structuredClone(
            activeProjectPatterns,
          ),

        globalPatterns:
          structuredClone(
            globalPatterns,
          ),
      },

      lessonState: {
        totalLessons:
          snapshot.lessons
            .length,

        activeLessons:
          snapshot
            .activeLessons
            .length,

        lessonPromotionPerformed:
          false,
      },

      invariants: {
        directLearnedLessonWrite:
          false,

        automaticLessonPromotion:
          false,

        rawExperienceCanOverrideGovernance:
          false,

        preferenceSeparatedFromOutcome:
          true,

        projectScopePreserved:
          true,
      },
    };
  }
}

const defaultCognitiveMemory =
  new CognitiveMemory();

export function getCognitiveMemory():
  CognitiveMemory {
  return defaultCognitiveMemory;
}

FILE: packages\models\src\continuousCalibration.ts
============================================================
import {
  mkdir,
  readFile,
  rename,
  writeFile,
} from "node:fs/promises";

import {
  dirname,
  join,
} from "node:path";

export const CONTINUOUS_CALIBRATION_VERSION =
  "oh4z.v1";

export type CalibrationObservationSource =
  | "real-work"
  | "scheduled-evaluation";

export type CalibrationVerificationVerdict =
  | "pass"
  | "revise"
  | "reject"
  | "blocked"
  | "unknown";

export interface CalibrationTaskProfile {
  domain: string;

  complexity?: string;
  creativity?: string;
  factuality?: string;
  reasoningDepth?: string;
  privacySensitivity?: string;
  toolUse?: string;
}

export interface CalibrationObservation {
  id: string;

  observedAt: string;

  source:
    CalibrationObservationSource;

  modelId: string;
  modelVersion: string;

  strategy?: string;

  task:
    CalibrationTaskProfile;

  objective: {
    score: number;

    success?:
      boolean;

    verificationVerdict?:
      CalibrationVerificationVerdict;

    executionSucceeded?:
      boolean;

    evidenceConfidence?:
      number;
  };

  subjective?: {
    preferenceScore?: number;

    userRating?: number;

    source?: string;
  };

  telemetry?: {
    latencyMs?: number;

    cost?: number;

    retries?: number;

    fallbackCount?: number;
  };

  provenance?: {
    experienceId?: string;

    evaluationId?: string;

    taskId?: string;

    eventIds?: string[];
  };
}

export interface CalibrationSubject {
  modelId: string;
  modelVersion: string;

  strategy?: string;

  domains?: string[];
}

export interface ContinuousCalibrationPolicy {
  halfLifeDays: number;

  scheduledEvaluationIntervalDays:
    number;

  minimumEffectiveSamples:
    number;

  strongSampleCount:
    number;

  maximumObservations:
    number;

  sourceWeights: {
    realWork: number;
    scheduledEvaluation: number;
  };
}

export interface CapabilityEstimate {
  key: string;

  modelId: string;
  modelVersion: string;

  strategy?: string;

  domain: string;

  objectiveScore: number;

  confidence: number;

  sampleCount: number;
  effectiveSampleCount: number;

  sourceCounts: {
    realWork: number;
    scheduledEvaluation: number;
  };

  verification: {
    passRate?: number;
    rejectRate?: number;
  };

  executionSuccessRate?: number;

  telemetry: {
    weightedLatencyMs?: number;
    weightedCost?: number;
    weightedRetries?: number;
    weightedFallbackCount?: number;
  };

  subjective: {
    sampleCount: number;
    weightedPreferenceScore?: number;
    weightedUserRating?: number;
  };

  firstObservedAt: string;
  lastObservedAt: string;

  lastScheduledEvaluationAt?:
    string;

  recalibration: {
    due: boolean;

    reasons: string[];
  };
}

export interface CalibrationSnapshot {
  version: string;

  generatedAt: string;

  policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy;

  observationCount: number;

  estimates:
    CapabilityEstimate[];

  dueSubjects:
    Array<{
      modelId: string;
      modelVersion: string;
      strategy?: string;
      domain: string;
      reasons: string[];
    }>;

  invariants: {
    subjectivePreferenceAffectsObjectiveScore:
      false;

    crossVersionEvidenceMixed:
      false;

    routingMutated:
      false;

    registryMutated:
      false;

    scheduledEvaluationExecuted:
      false;
  };
}

interface CalibrationSnapshotFile {
  schemaVersion: 1;

  savedAt: string;

  observations:
    CalibrationObservation[];
}

const DAY_MS =
  24 *
  60 *
  60 *
  1000;

export const DEFAULT_CONTINUOUS_CALIBRATION_POLICY:
  ContinuousCalibrationPolicy = {
    halfLifeDays:
      30,

    scheduledEvaluationIntervalDays:
      14,

    minimumEffectiveSamples:
      2,

    strongSampleCount:
      12,

    maximumObservations:
      5000,

    sourceWeights: {
      realWork:
        0.9,

      scheduledEvaluation:
        1,
    },
  };

function requireFinite(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be finite.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function unitInterval(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  requireFinite(
    value,
    field,
  );

  if (
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function nonNegative(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field: string,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  requireFinite(
    value,
    field,
  );

  if (
    value < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be non-negative.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function nonNegativeInteger(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field: string,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a non-negative integer.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function requireTimestamp(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const parsed =
    new Date(
      value,
    );

  if (
    Number.isNaN(
      parsed.getTime(),
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a valid timestamp.`,
    );
  }

  return parsed.toISOString();
}

function normalizeText(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function optionalText(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
): string | undefined {
  const normalized =
    value?.trim();

  return normalized ||
    undefined;
}

function normalizeDomain(
  value: string,
): string {
  return normalizeText(
    value,
    "calibration.task.domain",
  )
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(
      /\s+/g,
      "-",
    );
}

function validatePolicy(
  policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy,
): ContinuousCalibrationPolicy {
  for (
    const [
      field,
      value,
    ]
    of [
      [
        "halfLifeDays",
        policy.halfLifeDays,
      ],
      [
        "scheduledEvaluationIntervalDays",
        policy.scheduledEvaluationIntervalDays,
      ],
      [
        "minimumEffectiveSamples",
        policy.minimumEffectiveSamples,
      ],
      [
        "strongSampleCount",
        policy.strongSampleCount,
      ],
      [
        "maximumObservations",
        policy.maximumObservations,
      ],
    ] as const
  ) {
    if (
      !Number.isFinite(
        value,
      ) ||
      value <= 0
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `continuousCalibration.${field} must be greater than zero.`,
      );
    }
  }

  unitInterval(
    policy.sourceWeights
      .realWork,
    "continuousCalibration.sourceWeights.realWork",
  );

  unitInterval(
    policy.sourceWeights
      .scheduledEvaluation,
    "continuousCalibration.sourceWeights.scheduledEvaluation",
  );

  return structuredClone(
    policy,
  );
}

function normalizeObservation(
  value:
    CalibrationObservation,
): CalibrationObservation {
  const observedAt =
    requireTimestamp(
      value.observedAt,
      "calibration.observedAt",
    );

  const objectiveScore =
    unitInterval(
      value.objective
        .score,
      "calibration.objective.score",
    );

  const evidenceConfidence =
    value.objective
      .evidenceConfidence ===
      undefined
      ? undefined
      : unitInterval(
          value.objective
            .evidenceConfidence,
          "calibration.objective.evidenceConfidence",
        );

  const preferenceScore =
    value.subjective
      ?.preferenceScore ===
      undefined
      ? undefined
      : unitInterval(
          value.subjective
            .preferenceScore,
          "calibration.subjective.preferenceScore",
        );

  const userRating =
    value.subjective
      ?.userRating ===
      undefined
      ? undefined
      : unitInterval(
          value.subjective
            .userRating,
          "calibration.subjective.userRating",
        );

  return {
    id:
      normalizeText(
        value.id,
        "calibration.id",
      ),

    observedAt,

    source:
      value.source,

    modelId:
      normalizeText(
        value.modelId,
        "calibration.modelId",
      ),

    modelVersion:
      normalizeText(
        value.modelVersion,
        "calibration.modelVersion",
      ),

    strategy:
      optionalText(
        value.strategy,
      ),

    task: {
      domain:
        normalizeDomain(
          value.task.domain,
        ),

      complexity:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .complexity,
        ),

      creativity:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .creativity,
        ),

      factuality:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .factuality,
        ),

      reasoningDepth:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .reasoningDepth,
        ),

      privacySensitivity:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .privacySensitivity,
        ),

      toolUse:
        optionalText(
          value.task
            .toolUse,
        ),
    },

    objective: {
      score:
        objectiveScore,

      success:
        value.objective
          .success,

      verificationVerdict:
        value.objective
          .verificationVerdict ??
        "unknown",

      executionSucceeded:
        value.objective
          .executionSucceeded,

      evidenceConfidence,
    },

    subjective: value.subjective
      ? {
          preferenceScore,

          userRating,

          source:
            optionalText(
              value.subjective
                .source,
            ),
        }
      : undefined,

    telemetry: value.telemetry
      ? {
          latencyMs:
            nonNegative(
              value.telemetry
                .latencyMs,
              "calibration.telemetry.latencyMs",
            ),

          cost:
            nonNegative(
              value.telemetry
                .cost,
              "calibration.telemetry.cost",
            ),

          retries:
            nonNegativeInteger(
              value.telemetry
                .retries,
              "calibration.telemetry.retries",
            ),

          fallbackCount:
            nonNegativeInteger(
              value.telemetry
                .fallbackCount,
              "calibration.telemetry.fallbackCount",
            ),
        }
      : undefined,

    provenance:
      value.provenance
        ? structuredClone(
            value.provenance,
          )
        : undefined,
  };
}

function estimateKey(
  input: {
    modelId: string;
    modelVersion: string;
    strategy?: string;
    domain: string;
  },
): string {
  return [
    input.modelId,
    input.modelVersion,
    input.strategy ??
      "any-strategy",
    input.domain,
  ].join(
    "::",
  );
}

function recencyWeight(
  observedAt: string,
  now: Date,
  halfLifeDays: number,
): number {
  const ageMs =
    Math.max(
      0,
      now.getTime() -
      new Date(
        observedAt,
      ).getTime(),
    );

  const ageDays =
    ageMs /
    DAY_MS;

  return Math.pow(
    0.5,
    ageDays /
    halfLifeDays,
  );
}

function sourceWeight(
  source:
    CalibrationObservationSource,
  policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy,
): number {
  return source ===
    "scheduled-evaluation"
    ? policy.sourceWeights
        .scheduledEvaluation
    : policy.sourceWeights
        .realWork;
}

function observationWeight(
  observation:
    CalibrationObservation,
  now: Date,
  policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy,
): number {
  const confidence =
    observation.objective
      .evidenceConfidence ??
    0.75;

  return (
    recencyWeight(
      observation.observedAt,
      now,
      policy.halfLifeDays,
    ) *
    sourceWeight(
      observation.source,
      policy,
    ) *
    confidence
  );
}

function weightedAverage(
  values:
    Array<{
      value: number;
      weight: number;
    }>,
): number | undefined {
  const usable =
    values.filter(
      (entry) =>
        Number.isFinite(
          entry.value,
        ) &&
        Number.isFinite(
          entry.weight,
        ) &&
        entry.weight >
        0,
    );

  if (
    usable.length ===
    0
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const totalWeight =
    usable.reduce(
      (
        total,
        entry,
      ) =>
        total +
        entry.weight,
      0,
    );

  if (
    totalWeight <=
    0
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return (
    usable.reduce(
      (
        total,
        entry,
      ) =>
        total +
        entry.value *
          entry.weight,
      0,
    ) /
    totalWeight
  );
}

function binaryRate(
  values:
    Array<{
      value: boolean;
      weight: number;
    }>,
): number | undefined {
  return weightedAverage(
    values.map(
      (entry) => ({
        value:
          entry.value
            ? 1
            : 0,

        weight:
          entry.weight,
      }),
    ),
  );
}

function estimateConfidence(
  effectiveSamples: number,
  policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy,
): number {
  const ratio =
    Math.min(
      1,
      effectiveSamples /
      policy.strongSampleCount,
    );

  return Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(
      1,
      0.25 +
      0.75 *
      ratio,
    ),
  );
}

function dueReasons(
  input: {
    lastScheduledEvaluationAt?:
      string;

    effectiveSampleCount:
      number;

    now: Date;

    policy:
      ContinuousCalibrationPolicy;
  },
): string[] {
  const reasons:
    string[] =
      [];

  if (
    input.effectiveSampleCount <
    input.policy
      .minimumEffectiveSamples
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "insufficient-effective-samples",
    );
  }

  if (
    !input
      .lastScheduledEvaluationAt
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "no-scheduled-evaluation-for-current-version",
    );

    return reasons;
  }

  const ageDays =
    Math.max(
      0,
      input.now.getTime() -
      new Date(
        input
          .lastScheduledEvaluationAt,
      ).getTime(),
    ) /
    DAY_MS;

  if (
    ageDays >
    input.policy
      .scheduledEvaluationIntervalDays
  ) {
    reasons.push(
      "scheduled-evaluation-stale",
    );
  }

  return reasons;
}

export class ContinuousCalibrationEngine {
  private readonly observations =
    new Map<
      string,
      CalibrationObservation
    >();

  private readonly policy:
    ContinuousCalibrationPolicy;

  private readonly filePath:
    string;

  private readonly clock:
    () => Date;

  constructor(
    options: {
      policy?:
        Partial<
          ContinuousCalibrationPolicy
        >;

      filePath?:
        string;

      clock?:
        () => Date;
    } = {},
  ) {
    this.policy =
      validatePolicy({
        ...DEFAULT_CONTINUOUS_CALIBRATION_POLICY,

        ...(
          options.policy ??
          {}
        ),

        sourceWeights: {
          ...DEFAULT_CONTINUOUS_CALIBRATION_POLICY
            .sourceWeights,

          ...(
            options.policy
              ?.sourceWeights ??
            {}
          ),
        },
      });

    this.filePath =
      options.filePath ??
      join(
        process.cwd(),
        ".chernobog",
        "cognition",
        "continuous-calibration.json",
      );

    this.clock =
      options.clock ??
      (() => new Date());
  }

  get size(): number {
    return this.observations
      .size;
  }

  list():
    CalibrationObservation[] {
    return [
      ...this.observations
        .values(),
    ]
      .sort(
        (
          left,
          right,
        ) =>
          left.observedAt
            .localeCompare(
              right.observedAt,
            ) ||
          left.id
            .localeCompare(
              right.id,
            ),
      )
      .map(
        (entry) =>
          structuredClone(
            entry,
          ),
      );
  }

  clear(): void {
    this.observations
      .clear();
  }

  ingest(
    observation:
      CalibrationObservation,
  ):
    CalibrationObservation {
    const normalized =
      normalizeObservation(
        observation,
      );

    this.observations
      .set(
        normalized.id,
        normalized,
      );

    this.trim();

    return structuredClone(
      normalized,
    );
  }

  ingestMany(
    observations:
      readonly CalibrationObservation[],
  ):
    CalibrationObservation[] {
    return observations
      .map(
        (observation) =>
          this.ingest(
            observation,
          ),
      );
  }

  estimate(
    subject:
      CalibrationSubject,
    domain: string,
    now =
      this.clock(),
  ):
    CapabilityEstimate {
    const modelId =
      normalizeText(
        subject.modelId,
        "calibrationSubject.modelId",
      );

    const modelVersion =
      normalizeText(
        subject.modelVersion,
        "calibrationSubject.modelVersion",
      );

    const strategy =
      optionalText(
        subject.strategy,
      );

    const normalizedDomain =
      normalizeDomain(
        domain,
      );

    const matching =
      this.list()
        .filter(
          (observation) =>
            observation.modelId ===
              modelId &&
            observation.modelVersion ===
              modelVersion &&
            (
              strategy ===
                undefined ||
              observation.strategy ===
                strategy
            ) &&
            observation.task.domain ===
              normalizedDomain,
        );

    const weighted =
      matching.map(
        (observation) => ({
          observation,

          weight:
            observationWeight(
              observation,
              now,
              this.policy,
            ),
        }),
      );

    const totalWeight =
      weighted.reduce(
        (
          total,
          entry,
        ) =>
          total +
          entry.weight,
        0,
      );

    const objectiveScore =
      weightedAverage(
        weighted.map(
          (entry) => ({
            value:
              entry.observation
                .objective
                .score,

            weight:
              entry.weight,
          }),
        ),
      ) ??
      0.5;

    const scheduled =
      matching.filter(
        (observation) =>
          observation.source ===
          "scheduled-evaluation",
      );

    const lastScheduledEvaluationAt =
      scheduled.length >
        0
        ? scheduled[
            scheduled.length -
            1
          ]?.observedAt
        : undefined;

    const reasons =
      dueReasons({
        lastScheduledEvaluationAt,

        effectiveSampleCount:
          totalWeight,

        now,

        policy:
          this.policy,
      });

    const subjectiveEntries =
      weighted.filter(
        (entry) =>
          entry.observation
            .subjective
            ?.preferenceScore !==
            undefined ||
          entry.observation
            .subjective
            ?.userRating !==
            undefined,
      );

    return {
      key:
        estimateKey({
          modelId,
          modelVersion,
          strategy,
          domain:
            normalizedDomain,
        }),

      modelId,
      modelVersion,
      strategy,
      domain:
        normalizedDomain,

      objectiveScore:
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            1,
            objectiveScore,
          ),
        ),

      confidence:
        estimateConfidence(
          totalWeight,
          this.policy,
        ),

      sampleCount:
        matching.length,

      effectiveSampleCount:
        totalWeight,

      sourceCounts: {
        realWork:
          matching.filter(
            (observation) =>
              observation.source ===
              "real-work",
          ).length,

        scheduledEvaluation:
          scheduled.length,
      },

      verification: {
        passRate:
          binaryRate(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .objective
                    .verificationVerdict !==
                    undefined &&
                  entry.observation
                    .objective
                    .verificationVerdict !==
                    "unknown",
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .objective
                      .verificationVerdict ===
                    "pass",

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),

        rejectRate:
          binaryRate(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .objective
                    .verificationVerdict !==
                    undefined &&
                  entry.observation
                    .objective
                    .verificationVerdict !==
                    "unknown",
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .objective
                      .verificationVerdict ===
                    "reject",

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),
      },

      executionSuccessRate:
        binaryRate(
          weighted
            .filter(
              (entry) =>
                entry.observation
                  .objective
                  .executionSucceeded !==
                undefined,
            )
            .map(
              (entry) => ({
                value:
                  entry.observation
                    .objective
                    .executionSucceeded ===
                  true,

                weight:
                  entry.weight,
              }),
            ),
        ),

      telemetry: {
        weightedLatencyMs:
          weightedAverage(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .telemetry
                    ?.latencyMs !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .telemetry
                      ?.latencyMs ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),

        weightedCost:
          weightedAverage(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .telemetry
                    ?.cost !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .telemetry
                      ?.cost ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),

        weightedRetries:
          weightedAverage(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .telemetry
                    ?.retries !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .telemetry
                      ?.retries ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),

        weightedFallbackCount:
          weightedAverage(
            weighted
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .telemetry
                    ?.fallbackCount !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .telemetry
                      ?.fallbackCount ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),
      },

      subjective: {
        sampleCount:
          subjectiveEntries
            .length,

        weightedPreferenceScore:
          weightedAverage(
            subjectiveEntries
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .subjective
                    ?.preferenceScore !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .subjective
                      ?.preferenceScore ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),

        weightedUserRating:
          weightedAverage(
            subjectiveEntries
              .filter(
                (entry) =>
                  entry.observation
                    .subjective
                    ?.userRating !==
                  undefined,
              )
              .map(
                (entry) => ({
                  value:
                    entry.observation
                      .subjective
                      ?.userRating ??
                    0,

                  weight:
                    entry.weight,
                }),
              ),
          ),
      },

      firstObservedAt:
        matching[0]
          ?.observedAt ??
        now.toISOString(),

      lastObservedAt:
        matching[
          matching.length -
          1
        ]?.observedAt ??
        now.toISOString(),

      lastScheduledEvaluationAt,

      recalibration: {
        due:
          reasons.length >
          0,

        reasons,
      },
    };
  }

  snapshot(
    subjects:
      readonly CalibrationSubject[],
    now =
      this.clock(),
  ):
    CalibrationSnapshot {
    const estimates:
      CapabilityEstimate[] =
        [];

    for (
      const subject
      of subjects
    ) {
      const domains =
        [
          ...new Set(
            (
              subject.domains ??
              []
            )
              .map(
                normalizeDomain,
              ),
          ),
        ];

      const observedDomains =
        this.list()
          .filter(
            (observation) =>
              observation.modelId ===
                subject.modelId &&
              observation.modelVersion ===
                subject.modelVersion &&
              (
                subject.strategy ===
                  undefined ||
                observation.strategy ===
                  subject.strategy
              ),
          )
          .map(
            (observation) =>
              observation.task
                .domain,
          );

      const allDomains =
        [
          ...new Set(
            [
              ...domains,
              ...observedDomains,
            ],
          ),
        ]
          .sort();

      for (
        const domain
        of allDomains
      ) {
        estimates.push(
          this.estimate(
            subject,
            domain,
            now,
          ),
        );
      }
    }

    estimates.sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) =>
        left.key
          .localeCompare(
            right.key,
          ),
    );

    return {
      version:
        CONTINUOUS_CALIBRATION_VERSION,

      generatedAt:
        now.toISOString(),

      policy:
        structuredClone(
          this.policy,
        ),

      observationCount:
        this.observations
          .size,

      estimates:
        structuredClone(
          estimates,
        ),

      dueSubjects:
        estimates
          .filter(
            (estimate) =>
              estimate
                .recalibration
                .due,
          )
          .map(
            (estimate) => ({
              modelId:
                estimate.modelId,

              modelVersion:
                estimate
                  .modelVersion,

              strategy:
                estimate.strategy,

              domain:
                estimate.domain,

              reasons:
                structuredClone(
                  estimate
                    .recalibration
                    .reasons,
                ),
            }),
          ),

      invariants: {
        subjectivePreferenceAffectsObjectiveScore:
          false,

        crossVersionEvidenceMixed:
          false,

        routingMutated:
          false,

        registryMutated:
          false,

        scheduledEvaluationExecuted:
          false,
      },
    };
  }

  async save(
    now =
      this.clock(),
  ):
    Promise<void> {
    const snapshot:
      CalibrationSnapshotFile = {
        schemaVersion:
          1,

        savedAt:
          now.toISOString(),

        observations:
          this.list(),
      };

    await mkdir(
      dirname(
        this.filePath,
      ),
      {
        recursive:
          true,
      },
    );

    const temp =
      `${this.filePath}.tmp`;

    await writeFile(
      temp,
      `${JSON.stringify(snapshot, null, 2)}\n`,
      "utf8",
    );

    await rename(
      temp,
      this.filePath,
    );
  }

  async load():
    Promise<void> {
    const raw =
      await readFile(
        this.filePath,
        "utf8",
      );

    const parsed =
      JSON.parse(
        raw,
      ) as Partial<
        CalibrationSnapshotFile
      >;

    if (
      parsed.schemaVersion !==
        1 ||
      !Array.isArray(
        parsed.observations,
      )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "Invalid continuous calibration snapshot.",
      );
    }

    const next =
      new Map<
        string,
        CalibrationObservation
      >();

    for (
      const rawObservation
      of parsed.observations
    ) {
      const normalized =
        normalizeObservation(
          rawObservation,
        );

      next.set(
        normalized.id,
        normalized,
      );
    }

    this.observations
      .clear();

    for (
      const [
        id,
        observation,
      ]
      of next
    ) {
      this.observations
        .set(
          id,
          observation,
        );
    }

    this.trim();
  }

  private trim():
    void {
    const maximum =
      Math.floor(
        this.policy
          .maximumObservations,
      );

    if (
      this.observations
        .size <=
      maximum
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const ordered =
      this.list();

    const removeCount =
      ordered.length -
      maximum;

    for (
      const observation
      of ordered.slice(
        0,
        removeCount,
      )
    ) {
      this.observations
        .delete(
          observation.id,
        );
    }
  }
}

type ContinuousCalibrationGlobals =
  typeof globalThis & {
    __chernobogContinuousCalibrationPromise?:
      Promise<
        ContinuousCalibrationEngine
      >;
  };

const calibrationGlobals =
  globalThis as
    ContinuousCalibrationGlobals;

export function getContinuousCalibrationEngine():
  Promise<
    ContinuousCalibrationEngine
  > {
  if (
    !calibrationGlobals
      .__chernobogContinuousCalibrationPromise
  ) {
    calibrationGlobals
      .__chernobogContinuousCalibrationPromise =
      Promise.resolve()
        .then(
          async () => {
            const engine =
              new ContinuousCalibrationEngine();

            try {
              await engine.load();
            } catch (
              error
            ) {
              const code =
                (
                  error as
                    NodeJS.ErrnoException
                ).code;

              if (
                code !==
                "ENOENT"
              ) {
                throw error;
              }
            }

            return engine;
          },
        )
        .catch(
          (error) => {
            delete calibrationGlobals
              .__chernobogContinuousCalibrationPromise;

            throw error;
          },
        );
  }

  return calibrationGlobals
    .__chernobogContinuousCalibrationPromise;
}

import {
  getChernobogEventBus,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/events";

import type {
  ChernobogEvent,
  ChernobogEventInput,
  ChernobogEventPublishResult,
  ChernobogEventQuery,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/events";

export const DECISION_PROVENANCE_VERSION =
  "oh4aa.v1";

export const DECISION_PROVENANCE_EVENT_TYPE =
  "cognition.decision-provenance.recorded";

export type DecisionProvenanceStage =
  | "interpret"
  | "context"
  | "prompt"
  | "model-route"
  | "strategy-route"
  | "reasoning-contract"
  | "decomposition"
  | "planning"
  | "attention"
  | "orchestration"
  | "verification"
  | "confidence"
  | "escalation"
  | "authority"
  | "action"
  | "memory"
  | "calibration";

export type DecisionProvenanceStageStatus =
  | "selected"
  | "accepted"
  | "rejected"
  | "blocked"
  | "revised"
  | "skipped"
  | "completed"
  | "failed"
  | "observed";

export type DecisionProvenanceOutcomeStatus =
  | "accepted"
  | "rejected"
  | "blocked"
  | "revise"
  | "ready"
  | "executed"
  | "failed";

export interface DecisionProvenanceContextRef {
  id: string;

  kind: string;

  sourceRef?: string;

  selected: boolean;

  reason?: string;

  confidence?: number;

  freshness?: string;
}

export interface DecisionProvenanceStageRecord {
  stage:
    DecisionProvenanceStage;

  version?: string;

  status:
    DecisionProvenanceStageStatus;

  decision?: string;

  reasons: string[];

  evidenceRefs: string[];

  artifactRef?: string;

  metadata?:
    Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >;
}

export interface DecisionProvenanceTask {
  id?: string;

  interpretedIntent:
    string;

  goal:
    string;

  desiredOutcome?:
    string;

  constraints?:
    string[];

  unresolvedQuestions?:
    string[];
}

export interface DecisionProvenanceInput {
  correlationId: string;

  causationId?: string;

  subject?: string;

  scope?: string;

  sensitive?: boolean;

  task:
    DecisionProvenanceTask;

  context:
    DecisionProvenanceContextRef[];

  stages:
    DecisionProvenanceStageRecord[];

  outcome: {
    status:
      DecisionProvenanceOutcomeStatus;

    reason:
      string;

    finalArtifactRef?: string;
  };

    Record<
      string,

    >;
}

export interface DecisionProvenanceRecord {
  version: string;

  id: string;

  recordedAt: string;

  correlationId: string;

  causationId?: string;

  subject?: string;

  scope?: string;

  sensitive: boolean;

  task:
    DecisionProvenanceTask;

  context:
    DecisionProvenanceContextRef[];

  stages:
    DecisionProvenanceStageRecord[];

  outcome:
    DecisionProvenanceInput[
      "outcome"
    ];

    Record<
      string,

    >;

      false;

      false;

export interface DecisionProvenanceEventAccess {

    input:
      ChernobogEventInput<TPayload>,

    ChernobogEventPublishResult
  >;

    query?:

    ChernobogEvent[]
  >;
}

const STAGE_ORDER:
  readonly DecisionProvenanceStage[] = [
    "interpret",
    "context",
    "prompt",
    "model-route",
    "strategy-route",
    "reasoning-contract",
    "decomposition",
    "planning",
    "attention",
    "orchestration",
    "verification",
    "confidence",
    "escalation",
    "authority",
    "action",
    "memory",
    "calibration",
  ] as const;

const FORBIDDEN_METADATA_KEY =
  /(?:chain.?of.?thought|hidden.?reasoning|scratch.?reasoning|private.?reasoning|reasoning.?tokens|cot)/i;

function normalizeRequiredText(
  value: string,
  field: string,
): string {
  const normalized =
    value.trim();

  if (!normalized) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must not be empty.`,
    );
  }

  return normalized;
}

function normalizeOptionalText(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
): string | undefined {
  const normalized =
    value?.trim();

  return normalized ||
    undefined;
}

function normalizeTextList(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (
        values ??
        []
      )
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function unitInterval(
  value:
    number
    | undefined,
  field: string,
): number | undefined {
  if (
    value ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 0 ||
    value > 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be between 0 and 1.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function sanitizeUnknown(
  value: unknown,
  depth = 0,
): unknown {
  if (
    depth >
    12
  ) {
    return "[depth-limit]";
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    return value.map(
      (entry) =>
        sanitizeUnknown(
          entry,
          depth +
            1,
        ),
    );
  }

  if (
    value &&
    typeof value ===
      "object"
  ) {
    const output:
      Record<
        string,
        unknown
      > = {};

    for (
      const [
        key,
        entry,
      ]
      of Object.entries(
        value as Record<
          string,
          unknown
        >,
      )
    ) {
      if (
        FORBIDDEN_METADATA_KEY
          .test(
            key,
          )
      ) {
        output[key] =
          "[redacted-by-oh4aa]";

        continue;
      }

      output[key] =
        sanitizeUnknown(
          entry,
          depth +
            1,
        );
    }

    return output;
  }

  if (
    typeof value ===
      "bigint"
  ) {
    return value.toString();
  }

  return value;
}

function jsonSafeObject(
  value:
    Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >
    | undefined,
): Record<
  string,
  unknown
> {
  const sanitized =
    sanitizeUnknown(
      value ??
      {},
    );

  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      JSON.stringify(
        sanitized,
      ),
    ) as Record<
      string,
      unknown
    >;
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "Decision provenance metadata must be JSON-safe.",
    );
  }
}

function normalizeContext(
  values:
    readonly DecisionProvenanceContextRef[],
): DecisionProvenanceContextRef[] {
  return values
    .map(
      (
        value,
        index,
      ) => ({
        id:
          normalizeRequiredText(
            value.id,
            `context[${index}].id`,
          ),

        kind:
          normalizeRequiredText(
            value.kind,
            `context[${index}].kind`,
          ),

        sourceRef:
          normalizeOptionalText(
            value.sourceRef,
          ),

        selected:
          Boolean(
            value.selected,
          ),

        reason:
          normalizeOptionalText(
            value.reason,
          ),

        confidence:
          unitInterval(
            value.confidence,
            `context[${index}].confidence`,
          ),

        freshness:
          normalizeOptionalText(
            value.freshness,
          ),
      }),
    )
    .sort(
      (
        left,
        right,
      ) =>
        left.id.localeCompare(
          right.id,
        ),
    );
}

function normalizeStages(
  values:
    readonly DecisionProvenanceStageRecord[],
): DecisionProvenanceStageRecord[] {
  const seen =
    new Set<
      DecisionProvenanceStage
    >();

  const normalized =
    values.map(
      (
        value,
        index,
      ) => {
        if (
          seen.has(
            value.stage,
          )
        ) {
          throw new Error(
            `Decision provenance stage "${value.stage}" appears more than once.`,
          );
        }

        seen.add(
          value.stage,
        );

        return {
          stage:
            value.stage,

          version:
            normalizeOptionalText(
              value.version,
            ),

          status:
            value.status,

          decision:
            normalizeOptionalText(
              value.decision,
            ),

          reasons:
            normalizeTextList(
              value.reasons,
            ),

          evidenceRefs:
            normalizeTextList(
              value.evidenceRefs,
            ),

          artifactRef:
            normalizeOptionalText(
              value.artifactRef,
            ),

          metadata:
            jsonSafeObject(
              value.metadata,
            ),
        };
      },
    );

  return normalized.sort(
    (
      left,
      right,
    ) =>
      STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(
        left.stage,
      ) -
      STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(
        right.stage,
      ),
  );
}

function stableValue(
  value: unknown,
): unknown {
  if (
    Array.isArray(
      value,
    )
  ) {
    return value.map(
      stableValue,
    );
  }

  if (
    value &&
    typeof value ===
      "object"
  ) {
    return Object.fromEntries(
      Object.entries(
        value as Record<
          string,
          unknown
        >,
      )
        .sort(
          (
            left,
            right,
          ) =>
            left[0]
              .localeCompare(
                right[0],
              ),
        )
        .map(
          (
            [
              key,
              entry,
            ],
          ) => [
            key,
            stableValue(
              entry,
            ),
          ],
        ),
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function stableStringify(
  value: unknown,
): string {
  return JSON.stringify(
    stableValue(
      value,
    ),
  );
}

function fnv1a64(
  text: string,
): string {
  let hash =
    BigInt(
      "0xcbf29ce484222325",
    );

  const prime =
    BigInt(
      "0x100000001b3",
    );

  const mask =
    BigInt(
      "0xffffffffffffffff",
    );

  for (
    const byte
    of new TextEncoder()
      .encode(
        text,
      )
  ) {
    hash ^=
      BigInt(
        byte,
      );

    hash =
      (
        hash *
        prime
      ) &
      mask;
  }

  return hash
    .toString(
      16,
    )
    .padStart(
      16,
      "0",
    );
}

function normalizeInput(
  input:
    DecisionProvenanceInput,
  now: Date,
): DecisionProvenanceRecord {
  const correlationId =
    normalizeRequiredText(
      input.correlationId,
      "correlationId",
    );

  const task:
    DecisionProvenanceTask = {
      id:
        normalizeOptionalText(
          input.task.id,
        ),

      interpretedIntent:
        normalizeRequiredText(
          input.task
            .interpretedIntent,
          "task.interpretedIntent",
        ),

      goal:
        normalizeRequiredText(
          input.task.goal,
          "task.goal",
        ),

      desiredOutcome:
        normalizeOptionalText(
          input.task
            .desiredOutcome,
        ),

      constraints:
        normalizeTextList(
          input.task
            .constraints,
        ),

      unresolvedQuestions:
        normalizeTextList(
          input.task
            .unresolvedQuestions,
        ),
    };

  const context =
    normalizeContext(
      input.context,
    );

  const stages =
    normalizeStages(
      input.stages,
    );

  const outcome = {
    status:
      input.outcome
        .status,

    reason:
      normalizeRequiredText(
        input.outcome
          .reason,
        "outcome.reason",
      ),

    finalArtifactRef:
      normalizeOptionalText(
        input.outcome
          .finalArtifactRef,
      ),
  };

  const identityMaterial = {
    correlationId,

    causationId:
      normalizeOptionalText(
        input.causationId,
      ),

    task,

    context,

    stages,

    outcome,
  };

  const id =
    `oh4aa:${fnv1a64(stableStringify(identityMaterial))}`;

  return {
    version:
      DECISION_PROVENANCE_VERSION,

    id,

    recordedAt:
      now.toISOString(),

    correlationId,

    causationId:
      normalizeOptionalText(
        input.causationId,
      ),

    subject:
      normalizeOptionalText(
        input.subject,
      ),

    scope:
      normalizeOptionalText(
        input.scope,
      ),

    sensitive:
      input.sensitive ??
      false,

    task,

    context,

    stages,

    outcome,

    metadata:
      jsonSafeObject(
        input.metadata,
      ),

    invariants: {
      hiddenScratchReasoningRequired:
        false,

      hiddenScratchReasoningPersisted:
        false,

      routingMutated:
        false,

      authorityMutated:
        false,

      executionStarted:
        false,
    },
  };
}

function isProvenancePayload(
  value: unknown,
): value is DecisionProvenanceRecord {
  if (
    !value ||
    typeof value !==
      "object"
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const record =
    value as Partial<
      DecisionProvenanceRecord
    >;

  return (
    record.version ===
      DECISION_PROVENANCE_VERSION &&
    typeof record.id ===
      "string" &&
    typeof record.correlationId ===
      "string" &&
    Array.isArray(
      record.stages,
    )
  );
}

export class DecisionProvenanceRecorder {
  private readonly access:
    DecisionProvenanceEventAccess;

  private readonly clock:
    () => Date;

  constructor(
    options: {
      access?:
        DecisionProvenanceEventAccess;

      clock?:
        () => Date;
    } = {},
  ) {
    this.access =
      options.access ??
      getChernobogEventBus();

    this.clock =
      options.clock ??
      (() => new Date());
  }

  async record(
    input:
      DecisionProvenanceInput,
  ): Promise<
    DecisionProvenanceWriteResult
  > {
    const record =
      normalizeInput(
        input,
        this.clock(),
      );

    const published =
      await this.access
        .publish({
          type:
            DECISION_PROVENANCE_EVENT_TYPE,

          occurredAt:
            record.recordedAt,

          source: {
            subsystem:
              "cognition.decision-provenance",
          },

          severity:
            record.outcome
              .status ===
                "failed" ||
            record.outcome
              .status ===
                "rejected" ||
            record.outcome
              .status ===
                "blocked"
              ? "warning"
              : "info",

          subject:
            record.subject,

          scope:
            record.scope,

          correlationId:
            record.correlationId,

          causationId:
            record.causationId,

          dedupeKey:
            record.id,

          payload:
            record,

          metadata: {
            confidence:
              undefined,

            tags: [
              "oh4",
              "oh4aa",
              "cognition",
              "decision-provenance",
            ],

            sensitive:
              record.sensitive,
          },
        });

    return {
      record,

      eventId:
        published.event.id,

      persisted:
        true,

      deduplicated:
        published.deduplicated,
    };
  }

  async findByCorrelationId(
    correlationId: string,
    limit = 100,
  ): Promise<
    DecisionProvenanceRecord[]
  > {
    const normalized =
      normalizeRequiredText(
        correlationId,
        "correlationId",
      );

    const events =
      await this.access.query({
        types: [
          DECISION_PROVENANCE_EVENT_TYPE,
        ],

        correlationId:
          normalized,

        newestFirst:
          false,

        limit:
          Math.max(
            1,
            Math.min(
              limit,
              1000,
            ),
          ),
      });

    return events
      .map(
        (event) =>
          event.payload,
      )
      .filter(
        isProvenancePayload,
      )
      .map(
        (record) =>
          structuredClone(
            record,
          ),
      );
  }
}

const defaultDecisionProvenanceRecorder =
  new DecisionProvenanceRecorder();

export function getDecisionProvenanceRecorder():
  DecisionProvenanceRecorder {
  return defaultDecisionProvenanceRecorder;
}

FILE: packages\models\src\gateway.ts

import type {
  ModelGateway,
  ModelRequest,
  ModelResult,
} from "@chernobog/contracts/models";

import {
  generateWithReliableOllama,
} from "./reliableOllama";
import {
  recordModelInvocationTelemetry,
  unavailableCostUsage,
  unavailableTokenUsage,
  type ModelInvocationTelemetryContext,
} from "./telemetry";

export type ModelGenerateOptions =
  Parameters<
    typeof generateWithReliableOllama
  >[0] & {
    telemetry?:
      ModelInvocationTelemetryContext;
  };

export type ModelGenerateResult =
  Awaited<
    ReturnType<
      typeof generateWithReliableOllama
    >
  >;

/**
 * Provider-neutral public generation entry point.
 * This preserves the accepted low-level generation semantics while hiding
 * the current provider from business/project callers.
 */
export async function generateWithModel(
  options: ModelGenerateOptions,
): Promise<ModelGenerateResult> {
  const {
    telemetry,
    ...generationOptions
  } = options;

  const startedAt =
    Date.now();

  try {
    const result =
      await generateWithReliableOllama(
        generationOptions,
      );

    const attempts =
      result.attempts ??
      1;

    await recordModelInvocationTelemetry(
      {
        taskType:
          telemetry?.taskType ??
          result.role ??
          "unspecified",
        provider:
          "ollama",
        model:
          result.model,
        requestedRole:
          generationOptions.role ??
          "default",
        selectedModel:
          result.selection
            ?.selectedInstalledModel ??
          result.selection
            ?.selectedModel ??
          result.model,
        selectionReason:
          result.selection
            ?.reason,
        outcome:
          result.ok
            ? "success"
            : "failure",
        latencyMs:
          result.durationMs ??
          Date.now() -
            startedAt,
        attempts,
        retries:
          Math.max(
            0,
            attempts - 1,
          ),
        fallbackCount:
          result.fallbackCount ??
          0,
        tokenUsage:
          unavailableTokenUsage(),
        cost:
          unavailableCostUsage(),
        validationExpected:
          telemetry
            ?.validationExpected ===
          true,
        error:
          result.error,
        failureKind:
          result.failureKind,
      },
      telemetry,
    );

    return result;
  } catch (error) {
    await recordModelInvocationTelemetry(
      {
        taskType:
          telemetry?.taskType ??
          generationOptions.role ??
          "unspecified",
        provider:
          "ollama",
        model:
          generationOptions
            .modelOverride ??
          "unresolved",
        requestedRole:
          generationOptions.role ??
          "default",
        outcome:
          "failure",
        latencyMs:
          Date.now() -
          startedAt,
        attempts:
          0,
        retries:
          0,
        fallbackCount:
          0,
        tokenUsage:
          unavailableTokenUsage(),
        cost:
          unavailableCostUsage(),
        validationExpected:
          telemetry
            ?.validationExpected ===
          true,
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : String(error),
        failureKind:
          "gateway-exception",
      },
      telemetry,
    );

    throw error;
  }
}

export const modelGateway: ModelGateway = {
  async execute<TOutput = unknown>(
    request: ModelRequest,
  ): Promise<ModelResult<TOutput>> {
    const metadata =
      request.metadata ?? {};

    const result =
      await generateWithModel({
        role:
          (request.role ?? "default") as any,
        ...(request.prompt
          ? { prompt: request.prompt }
          : {}),
        ...(request.messages
          ? { messages: request.messages as any }
          : {}),
        ...(typeof metadata.temperature === "number"
          ? { temperature: metadata.temperature }
          : {}),
        ...(typeof metadata.timeoutMs === "number"
          ? { timeoutMs: metadata.timeoutMs }
          : {}),
        ...(typeof metadata.numPredict === "number"
          ? { numPredict: metadata.numPredict }
          : {}),
        ...(typeof metadata.think === "boolean"
          ? { think: metadata.think }
          : {}),
      });

    return {
      ok: result.ok,
      ...(result.text !== undefined
        ? { output: result.text as TOutput }
        : {}),
      ...(result.error
        ? { error: result.error }
        : {}),
      provider: "ollama",
      model: result.model,
      metadata: {
        role: result.role,
      },
    };
  },
};
