export const DEFAULT_AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_LOOP_POLICY:
  AutonomousCognitiveLoopPolicy = {
    maxStageExecutions:
      80,

    maxRecoveryAttempts:
      8,
  };

const TERMINAL_STATUSES =
  new Set<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopStatus
  >([
    "completed",
    "failed",
    "stopped",
  ]);

function nowIso(
  clock:
    () => Date,
): string {
  return clock()
    .toISOString();
}

function normalizeText(
  value:
    string
    | undefined,
): string | undefined {
  const normalized =
    value?.trim();

  return normalized ||
    undefined;
}

function requirePositiveInteger(
  value: number,
  field: string,
): number {
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(
      value,
    ) ||
    value < 1
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      `${field} must be a positive integer.`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function normalizePolicy(
  value:
    Partial<
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopPolicy
    >
    | undefined,
): AutonomousCognitiveLoopPolicy {
  const merged = {
    ...DEFAULT_AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_LOOP_POLICY,
    ...(
      value ??
      {}
    ),
  };

  return {
    maxStageExecutions:
      requirePositiveInteger(
        merged
          .maxStageExecutions,
        "autonomousLoop.maxStageExecutions",
      ),

    maxRecoveryAttempts:
      requirePositiveInteger(
        merged
          .maxRecoveryAttempts,
        "autonomousLoop.maxRecoveryAttempts",
      ),
  };
}

function jsonSafe(
  value: unknown,
): unknown {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      JSON.stringify(
        value,
      ),
    );
  } catch {
    throw new Error(
      "OH-4AD loop state/artifacts must be JSON-safe.",
    );
  }
}

function stageIndex(
  stage:
    AutonomousCognitiveStage,
): number {
  return AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
    .indexOf(
      stage,
    );
}

function currentStage(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): AutonomousCognitiveStage {
  return (
    AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER[
      Math.min(
        state.currentStageIndex,
        AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
          .length -
        1,
      )
    ] ??
    "learn"
  );
}

function isTerminal(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): boolean {
  return TERMINAL_STATUSES
    .has(
      state.status,
    );
}

function cloneState(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): AutonomousCognitiveLoopState {
  return structuredClone(
    state,
  );
}

function normalizeQuestions(
  values:
    readonly string[],
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      values
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function normalizeNotes(
  values:
    readonly string[]
    | undefined,
): string[] {
  return [
    ...new Set(
      (
        values ??
        []
      )
        .map(
          (value) =>
            value.trim(),
        )
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  ];
}

function recoveryResumeToStage(
  resumeStage:
    RecoveryResumeStage,
  action:
    RecoveryActionKind,
  failedStage:
    AutonomousCognitiveStage,
): AutonomousCognitiveStage | undefined {
  if (
    action ===
      "retry-tool" ||
    action ===
      "resume-from-checkpoint"
  ) {
    return "act";
  }

  switch (
    resumeStage
  ) {
    case "context":
      return "contextualize";

    case "prompt":
      return "compile-prompt";

    case "model-route":
      return "choose-models";

    case "planning":
    case "orchestration":
      return "reason-plan";

    case "verification":
      return failedStage ===
        "verify"
        ? "verify"
        : "validate";

    case "authority":
      return "authorize";

    case "action":
      return "act";

    case "human":
    case "stop":
      return undefined;
  }
}

function recoveryAttemptsForEngine(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): RecoveryAttemptRecord[] {
  return state.recoveries
    .map(
      (
        recovery,
        index,
      ) => ({
        attempt:
          index +
          1,

        action:
          recovery.action,

        failureFingerprint:
          recovery
            .failureFingerprint,

        completed:
          true,

        succeeded:
          false,

        occurredAt:
          recovery.at,
      }),
    );
}

function createStageStates():
  AutonomousCognitiveStageState[] {
  return AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
    .map(
      (stage) => ({
        stage,

        componentBinding: [
          ...AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_BINDINGS[
            stage
          ],
        ],

        status:
          "pending",

        attempts:
          0,

        notes:
          [],
      }),
    );
}

function createLoopState(
  input:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopInput,
  clock:
    () => Date,
): AutonomousCognitiveLoopState {
  const createdAt =
    nowIso(
      clock,
    );

  return {
    version:
      AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_LOOP_VERSION,

    id:
      `oh4ad-${randomUUID()}`,

    createdAt,

    updatedAt:
      createdAt,

    status:
      "created",

    input: {
      ...jsonSafe(
        input,
      ) as
        AutonomousCognitiveLoopInput,

      subject:
        normalizeText(
          input.subject,
        ),

      scope:
        normalizeText(
          input.scope,
        ),

      correlationId:
        normalizeText(
          input.correlationId,
        ),
    },

    currentStageIndex:
      0,

    stages:
      createStageStates(),

    artifacts:
      {},

    supersededArtifacts:
      {},

    humanInterventions:
      [],

    recoveries:
      [],

    totalStageExecutions:
      0,

    invariants: {
      authorityBypassed:
        false,

      directToolExecutionByLoop:
        false,

      directModelExecutionByLoop:
        false,

      hiddenScratchReasoningRequired:
        false,

      completedWorkSilentlyDiscarded:
        false,

      unsafePostActionRewindAllowed:
        false,
    },
  };
}

function recordArtifact(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  stage:
    AutonomousCognitiveStage,
  artifact:
    unknown,
): void {
  if (
    artifact ===
    undefined
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const safe =
    jsonSafe(
      artifact,
    );

  if (
    state.artifacts[
      stage
    ] !==
    undefined
  ) {
    const history =
      state
        .supersededArtifacts[
          stage
        ] ??
      [];

    history.push(
      state.artifacts[
        stage
      ],
    );

    state.supersededArtifacts[
      stage
    ] =
      history;
  }

  state.artifacts[
    stage
  ] =
    safe;
}

function resetFromStage(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  resumeStage:
    AutonomousCognitiveStage,
): void {
  const start =
    stageIndex(
      resumeStage,
    );

  for (
    let index =
      start;
    index <
      state.stages.length;
    index +=
      1
  ) {
    const stageState =
      state.stages[
        index
      ];

    if (
      !stageState
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      state.artifacts[
        stageState.stage
      ] !==
      undefined
    ) {
      const history =
        state
          .supersededArtifacts[
            stageState.stage
          ] ??
        [];

      history.push(
        state.artifacts[
          stageState.stage
        ],
      );

      state
        .supersededArtifacts[
          stageState.stage
        ] =
        history;

      delete state
        .artifacts[
          stageState.stage
        ];
    }

    stageState.status =
      "pending";

    stageState.startedAt =
      undefined;

    stageState.completedAt =
      undefined;

    stageState.error =
      undefined;
  }

  state.currentStageIndex =
    start;
}

function hasPassedAct(
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): boolean {
  const act =
    state.stages[
      stageIndex(
        "act",
      )
    ];

  return (
    act?.status ===
      "completed" ||
    act?.status ===
      "skipped"
  );
}

function safePostActionRecovery(
  decision:
    FailureRecoveryDecision,
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  failedStage:
    AutonomousCognitiveStage,
  recoveryContext:
    Partial<
      FailureRecoveryContext
    >,
): boolean {
  if (
    !hasPassedAct(
      state,
    ) &&
    stageIndex(
      failedStage,
    ) <=
      stageIndex(
        "act",
      )
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  if (
    decision.selected
      .action ===
        "retry-tool" ||
    decision.selected
      .action ===
        "resume-from-checkpoint"
  ) {
    const partial =
      recoveryContext
        .partialExecution;

    return Boolean(
      partial
        ?.resumable &&
      partial
        .checkpointRef &&
      (
        !partial
          .sideEffectsObserved ||
        partial
          .idempotencyKey
      ),
    );
  }

  if (
    decision.selected
      .action ===
      "invoke-critic" &&
    failedStage ===
      "verify"
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return (
    decision.selected
      .resumeStage ===
      "human" ||
    decision.selected
      .resumeStage ===
      "stop"
  );
}

export class JsonAutonomousCognitiveLoopStore
  implements AutonomousCognitiveLoopStore {
  constructor(
    private readonly directory =
      join(
        process.cwd(),
        ".chernobog",
        "cognition",
        "autonomous-loop",
      ),
  ) {}

  async save(
    state:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const safe =
      jsonSafe(
        state,
      );

    const target =
      this.pathFor(
        state.id,
      );

    await mkdir(
      dirname(
        target,
      ),
      {
        recursive:
          true,
      },
    );

    const temporary =
      `${target}.${process.pid}.tmp`;

    await writeFile(
      temporary,
      `${JSON.stringify(safe, null, 2)}\n`,
      "utf8",
    );

    await rename(
      temporary,
      target,
    );
  }

  async load(
    id: string,
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const raw =
      await readFile(
        this.pathFor(
          id,
        ),
        "utf8",
      );

    const parsed =
      JSON.parse(
        raw,
      ) as
        AutonomousCognitiveLoopState;

    if (
      parsed.version !==
      AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_LOOP_VERSION
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unsupported OH-4AD loop state version: ${String(parsed.version)}`,
      );
    }

    return cloneState(
      parsed,
    );
  }

  private pathFor(
    id: string,
  ): string {
    if (
      !/^oh4ad-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+$/
        .test(
          id,
        )
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "Invalid OH-4AD loop id.",
      );
    }

    return join(
      this.directory,
      `${id}.json`,
    );
  }
}

async function defaultEventPublisher(
  event:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopEvent,
  state:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
): Promise<void> {
  await publishChernobogEventSafely({
    type:
      event.type,

    source: {
      subsystem:
        "cognition.autonomous-loop",
    },

    severity:
      event.status ===
        "failed" ||
      event.status ===
        "stopped"
        ? "warning"
        : event.status ===
            "waiting-human" ||
          event.status ===
            "interrupted"
          ? "notice"
          : "info",

    subject:
      state.input
        .subject ??
      state.id,

    scope:
      state.input
        .scope ??
      "cognition:oh4ad",

    correlationId:
      state.input
        .correlationId ??
      state.id,

    causationId:
      event.stage
        ? `${state.id}:${event.stage}`
        : undefined,

    payload: {
      loopId:
        state.id,

      stage:
        event.stage,

      status:
        event.status,

      detail:
        event.detail,

      currentStageIndex:
        state.currentStageIndex,

      totalStageExecutions:
        state
          .totalStageExecutions,

      recoveryCount:
        state.recoveries
          .length,
    },

    metadata: {
      tags: [
        "oh4",
        "oh4ad",
        "autonomous-cognitive-loop",
        ...(
          event.stage
            ? [
                event.stage,
              ]
            : []
        ),
      ],
    },
  });
}

export class AutonomousCognitiveLoop {
  private readonly policy:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopPolicy;

  private readonly store:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopStore;

  private readonly clock:
    () => Date;

  private readonly eventPublisher:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopEventPublisher;

  constructor(
    private readonly handlers:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopHandlers,

    options: {
      policy?:
        Partial<
          AutonomousCognitiveLoopPolicy
        >;

      store?:
        AutonomousCognitiveLoopStore;

      clock?:
        () => Date;

      eventPublisher?:
        AutonomousCognitiveLoopEventPublisher;
    } = {},
  ) {
    this.policy =
      normalizePolicy(
        options.policy,
      );

    this.store =
      options.store ??
      new JsonAutonomousCognitiveLoopStore();

    this.clock =
      options.clock ??
      (() => new Date());

    this.eventPublisher =
      options.eventPublisher ??
      defaultEventPublisher;

    for (
      const stage
      of AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
    ) {
      if (
        typeof this.handlers[
          stage
        ] !==
        "function"
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `OH-4AD requires a stage adapter for "${stage}".`,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  async create(
    input:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopInput,
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const state =
      createLoopState(
        input,
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    await this.emit(
      {
        type:
          "cognition.autonomous-loop.created",

        loopId:
          state.id,

        status:
          state.status,
      },
      state,
    );

    return cloneState(
      state,
    );
  }

  async start(
    input:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopInput,
    options:
      AutonomousCognitiveRunOptions =
      {},
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const state =
      await this.create(
        input,
      );

    return this.run(
      state.id,
      options,
    );
  }

  async load(
    id: string,
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    return this.store
      .load(
        id,
      );
  }

  async run(
    id: string,
    options:
      AutonomousCognitiveRunOptions =
      {},
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    let state =
      await this.store
        .load(
          id,
        );

    if (
      isTerminal(
        state,
      )
    ) {
      return state;
    }

    if (
      state.status ===
        "waiting-human"
    ) {
      return state;
    }

    if (
      state.status ===
        "interrupted"
    ) {
      return state;
    }

    state.status =
      "running";

    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    while (
      state.currentStageIndex <
      AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
        .length
    ) {
      if (
        state.totalStageExecutions >=
        this.policy
          .maxStageExecutions
      ) {
        state.status =
          "stopped";

        state.terminalReason =
          `OH-4AD exceeded max stage execution limit of ${this.policy.maxStageExecutions}.`;

        await this.persist(
          state,
        );

        await this.emit(
          {
            type:
              "cognition.autonomous-loop.stopped",

            loopId:
              state.id,

            status:
              "stopped",

            detail:
              state
                .terminalReason,
          },
          state,
        );

        return cloneState(
          state,
        );
      }

      if (
        await options
          .shouldInterrupt?.(
            cloneState(
              state,
            ),
          )
      ) {
        return this.interrupt(
          state.id,
          "External interruption requested.",
        );
      }

      const stage =
        AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER[
          state.currentStageIndex
        ];

      if (!stage) {
        break;
      }

      const stageState =
        state.stages[
          state.currentStageIndex
        ];

      if (!stageState) {
        throw new Error(
          `Missing OH-4AD stage state at index ${state.currentStageIndex}.`,
        );
      }

      if (
        stageState.status ===
          "completed" ||
        stageState.status ===
          "skipped"
      ) {
        state.currentStageIndex +=
          1;

        continue;
      }

      stageState.status =
        "running";

      stageState.attempts +=
        1;

      stageState.startedAt =
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        );

      stageState.error =
        undefined;

      state.totalStageExecutions +=
        1;

      state.updatedAt =
        stageState.startedAt;

      await this.persist(
        state,
      );

      await this.emit(
        {
          type:
            "cognition.autonomous-loop.stage.started",

          loopId:
            state.id,

          stage,

          status:
            "running",
        },
        state,
      );

      const controller =
        new AbortController();

      const humanInput =
        state
          .humanInterventions
          .filter(
            (entry) =>
              entry.stage ===
              stage,
          )
          .at(
            -1,
          )
          ?.input;

      let result:
        AutonomousCognitiveStageResult;

      try {
        result =
          await this.handlers[
            stage
          ]({
            loopId:
              state.id,

            stage,

            input:
              structuredClone(
                state.input,
              ),

            artifacts:
              structuredClone(
                state.artifacts,
              ),

            stageStates:
              structuredClone(
                state.stages,
              ),

            humanInput:
              humanInput ===
                undefined
                ? undefined
                : structuredClone(
                    humanInput,
                  ),

            recoveryHistory:
              structuredClone(
                state.recoveries,
              ),

            signal:
              controller.signal,
          });
      } catch (error) {
        const message =
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : String(
                error,
              );

        result = {
          status:
            "failed",

          error:
            message,

          failure: {
            kind:
              "unknown",

            message,
          },
        };
      }

      recordArtifact(
        state,
        stage,
        result.artifact,
      );

      if (
        result.status ===
          "completed" ||
        result.status ===
          "skipped"
      ) {
        stageState.status =
          result.status;

        stageState.completedAt =
          nowIso(
            this.clock,
          );

        stageState.notes = [
          ...stageState
            .notes,
          ...normalizeNotes(
            result.notes,
          ),
        ];

        state.currentStageIndex +=
          1;

        state.updatedAt =
          stageState
            .completedAt;

        await this.persist(
          state,
        );

        await this.emit(
          {
            type:
              "cognition.autonomous-loop.stage.completed",

            loopId:
              state.id,

            stage,

            status:
              result.status,
          },
          state,
        );

        continue;
      }

      if (
        result.status ===
        "waiting-human"
      ) {
        const questions =
          normalizeQuestions(
            result.questions,
          );

        stageState.status =
          "waiting-human";

        stageState.error =
          undefined;

        state.status =
          "waiting-human";

        state.waitingForHuman = {
          stage,

          reason:
            result.reason
              .trim() ||
            "Human intervention is required.",

          questions,

          recoveryDriven:
            false,
        };

        state.updatedAt =
          nowIso(
            this.clock,
          );

        await this.persist(
          state,
        );

        await this.emit(
          {
            type:
              "cognition.autonomous-loop.waiting-human",

            loopId:
              state.id,

            stage,

            status:
              "waiting-human",

            detail:
              state
                .waitingForHuman
                .reason,
          },
          state,
        );

        return cloneState(
          state,
        );
      }

      if (
        result.status !==
        "failed"
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `OH-4AD stage "${stage}" returned an unsupported result status.`,
        );
      }

      state =
        await this.handleFailure(
          state,
          stage,
          result,
        );

      if (
        state.status ===
          "waiting-human" ||
        state.status ===
          "stopped" ||
        state.status ===
          "failed"
      ) {
        return cloneState(
          state,
        );
      }
    }

    state.status =
      "completed";

    state.currentStageIndex =
      AUTONOMOUS_COGNITIVE_STAGE_ORDER
        .length;

    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    await this.emit(
      {
        type:
          "cognition.autonomous-loop.completed",

        loopId:
          state.id,

        status:
          "completed",
      },
      state,
    );

    return cloneState(
      state,
    );
  }

  async interrupt(
    id: string,
    reason:
      string,
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const state =
      await this.store
        .load(
          id,
        );

    if (
      isTerminal(
        state,
      )
    ) {
      return state;
    }

    const stage =
      currentStage(
        state,
      );

    state.status =
      "interrupted";

    state.interruption = {
      at:
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        ),

      reason:
        normalizeText(
          reason,
        ) ??
        "Interrupted.",

      stage,
    };

    state.updatedAt =
      state
        .interruption
        .at;

    const stageState =
      state.stages[
        stageIndex(
          stage,
        )
      ];

    if (
      stageState
        ?.status ===
        "running"
    ) {
      stageState.status =
        "pending";
    }

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    await this.emit(
      {
        type:
          "cognition.autonomous-loop.interrupted",

        loopId:
          state.id,

        stage,

        status:
          "interrupted",

        detail:
          state
            .interruption
            .reason,
      },
      state,
    );

    return cloneState(
      state,
    );
  }

  async resume(
    id: string,
    options:
      AutonomousCognitiveResumeOptions =
      {},
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const state =
      await this.store
        .load(
          id,
        );

    if (
      isTerminal(
        state,
      )
    ) {
      return state;
    }

    if (
      state.status ===
        "waiting-human"
    ) {
      if (
        options.humanInput ===
        undefined
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "OH-4AD loop is waiting for human intervention; humanInput is required to resume.",
        );
      }

      const stage =
        state
          .waitingForHuman
          ?.stage ??
        currentStage(
          state,
        );

      state
        .humanInterventions
        .push({
          at:
            nowIso(
              this.clock,
            ),

          stage,

          input:
            jsonSafe(
              options.humanInput,
            ),
        });

      const stageState =
        state.stages[
          stageIndex(
            stage,
          )
        ];

      if (
        stageState
      ) {
        stageState.status =
          "pending";

        stageState.error =
          undefined;
      }

      state.waitingForHuman =
        undefined;
    }

    if (
      state.status ===
        "interrupted"
    ) {
      const stage =
        state
          .interruption
          ?.stage ??
        currentStage(
          state,
        );

      const stageState =
        state.stages[
          stageIndex(
            stage,
          )
        ];

      if (
        stageState &&
        stageState.status !==
          "completed" &&
        stageState.status !==
          "skipped"
      ) {
        stageState.status =
          "pending";
      }

      state.interruption =
        undefined;
    }

    state.status =
      "running";

    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    return this.run(
      id,
      options,
    );
  }

  private async handleFailure(
    state:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
    failedStage:
      AutonomousCognitiveStage,
    result:
      Extract<
        AutonomousCognitiveStageResult,
        {
          status:
            "failed";
        }
      >,
  ): Promise<
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopState
  > {
    const failedState =
      state.stages[
        stageIndex(
          failedStage,
        )
      ];

    if (
      failedState
    ) {
      failedState.status =
        "failed";

      failedState.error =
        result.error;

      failedState.completedAt =
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        );
    }

    await this.emit(
      {
        type:
          "cognition.autonomous-loop.stage.failed",

        loopId:
          state.id,

        stage:
          failedStage,

        status:
          "failed",

        detail:
          result.error,
      },
      state,
    );

    if (
      state.recoveries
        .length >=
      this.policy
        .maxRecoveryAttempts
    ) {
      state.status =
        "stopped";

      state.terminalReason =
        `OH-4AD exceeded max recovery attempt limit of ${this.policy.maxRecoveryAttempts}.`;

      await this.persist(
        state,
      );

      return state;
    }

    const recoveryContext:
      FailureRecoveryContext = {
        attemptCount:
          state.recoveries
            .length,

        maxAttempts:
          this.policy
            .maxRecoveryAttempts,

        recentAttempts:
          recoveryAttemptsForEngine(
            state,
          ),

        humanAvailable:
          true,

        ...(
          result.recoveryContext ??
          {}
        ),
      };

    const failure:
      FailureSignal = {
        id:
          result.failure
            ?.id ??
          `${state.id}:${failedStage}:${failedState?.attempts ?? 1}`,

        kind:
          result.failure
            ?.kind ??
          "unknown",

        occurredAt:
          result.failure
            ?.occurredAt ??
          nowIso(
            this.clock,
          ),

        stage:
          result.failure
            ?.stage ??
          failedStage,

        source:
          result.failure
            ?.source ??
          "oh4ad",

        message:
          result.failure
            ?.message ??
          result.error,

        transient:
          result.failure
            ?.transient,

        retryable:
          result.failure
            ?.retryable,

        failureFingerprint:
          result.failure
            ?.failureFingerprint,

        modelId:
          result.failure
            ?.modelId,

        toolName:
          result.failure
            ?.toolName,

        evidenceRefs:
          result.failure
            ?.evidenceRefs,
      };

    const decision =
      getFailureRecoveryEngine()
        .decide({
          failure,

          context:
            recoveryContext,

          policy: {
            maxAttempts:
              this.policy
                .maxRecoveryAttempts,
          },
        });

    const safe =
      safePostActionRecovery(
        decision,
        state,
        failedStage,
        recoveryContext,
      );

    if (!safe) {
      state.status =
        "waiting-human";

      state.waitingForHuman = {
        stage:
          failedStage,

        reason:
          "Automatic recovery would rewind across a completed action without explicit safe replay/resume evidence.",

        questions: [
          "An external or side-effecting action may already have occurred. Review the current state and choose whether to continue, repair manually, or stop.",
        ],

        recoveryDriven:
          true,
      };

      state.updatedAt =
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        );

      await this.persist(
        state,
      );

      await this.emit(
        {
          type:
            "cognition.autonomous-loop.waiting-human",

          loopId:
            state.id,

          stage:
            failedStage,

          status:
            "waiting-human",

          detail:
            state
              .waitingForHuman
              .reason,
        },
        state,
      );

      return state;
    }

    const mappedStage =
      recoveryResumeToStage(
        decision.selected
          .resumeStage,
        decision.selected
          .action,
        failedStage,
      );

    state.recoveries
      .push({
        at:
          nowIso(
            this.clock,
          ),

        failedStage,

        failureFingerprint:
          decision.failure
            .fingerprint,

        action:
          decision.selected
            .action,

        resumeStage:
          decision.selected
            .resumeStage,

        resumedAtStage:
          mappedStage,

        summary:
          decision.selected
            .summary,
      });

    if (
      decision.selected
        .action ===
        "request-human-clarification"
    ) {
      state.status =
        "waiting-human";

      state.waitingForHuman = {
        stage:
          failedStage,

        reason:
          decision.selected
            .summary,

        questions: [
          decision.selected
            .summary,
        ],

        recoveryDriven:
          true,
      };

      state.updatedAt =
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        );

      await this.persist(
        state,
      );

      return state;
    }

    if (
      decision.selected
        .action ===
        "stop-safely" ||
      !mappedStage
    ) {
      state.status =
        "stopped";

      state.terminalReason =
        decision.selected
          .summary;

      state.updatedAt =
        nowIso(
          this.clock,
        );

      await this.persist(
        state,
      );

      return state;
    }

    resetFromStage(
      state,
      mappedStage,
    );

    state.status =
      "recovering";

    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    await this.emit(
      {
        type:
          "cognition.autonomous-loop.recovering",

        loopId:
          state.id,

        stage:
          mappedStage,

        status:
          "recovering",

        detail:
          `${decision.selected.action}: ${decision.selected.summary}`,
      },
      state,
    );

    state.status =
      "running";

    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.persist(
      state,
    );

    return state;
  }

  private async persist(
    state:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  ): Promise<void> {
    state.updatedAt =
      nowIso(
        this.clock,
      );

    await this.store
      .save(
        state,
      );
  }

  private async emit(
    event:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopEvent,
    state:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoopState,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.eventPublisher(
      event,
      cloneState(
        state,
      ),
    );
  }
}

type RuntimeGlobals =
  typeof globalThis & {
    __chernobogAutonomousCognitiveLoop?:
      AutonomousCognitiveLoop;
  };

const runtimeGlobals =
  globalThis as
    RuntimeGlobals;

export function configureAutonomousCognitiveLoopRuntime(
  handlers:
    AutonomousCognitiveLoopHandlers,

  options: ConstructorParameters<
    typeof AutonomousCognitiveLoop
  >[1] = {},
): AutonomousCognitiveLoop {
  const runtime =
    new AutonomousCognitiveLoop(
      handlers,
      options,
    );

  runtimeGlobals
    .__chernobogAutonomousCognitiveLoop =
    runtime;

  return runtime;
}

export function getAutonomousCognitiveLoopRuntime():
  AutonomousCognitiveLoop {
  const runtime =
    runtimeGlobals
      .__chernobogAutonomousCognitiveLoop;

  if (!runtime) {
    throw new Error(
      "OH-4AD autonomous cognitive loop runtime is not configured with stage adapters.",
    );
  }

  return runtime;
}

import type { RouteName } from "@/lib/chernobog/session/types";

export type WorkflowKind = "none" | "file";

export type FileWorkflowStep =
  | "idle"
  | "searching"
  | "awaiting_selection"
  | "selected"
  | "reading"
  | "completed"
  | "failed";

export type FileCandidate = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  path?: string;
  summary?: string;
  score?: number;
  source?: string;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;
};

export type FileSelectionReference =
  | { mode: "ordinal"; ordinal: number }
  | { mode: "candidateId"; candidateId: string }
  | { mode: "relative"; value: "first" | "last" | "next" | "previous" }
  | { mode: "label"; label: string };

export type FileWorkflowState = {
  kind: "file";
  step: FileWorkflowStep;
  query: string | null;
  root: string | null;
  candidates: FileCandidate[];
  selectedCandidateId: string | null;
  readCandidateId: string | null;
  awaitingDisambiguation: boolean;
  lastAction:
    | "search"
    | "select"
    | "read"
    | "open"
    | "change_root"
    | "refine_query"
    | null;
  lastUserReference?: FileSelectionReference | null;
  error?: string | null;
};

export type WorkflowState =
  | { kind: "none" }
  | FileWorkflowState;

export type FileWorkflowIntent =
  | { kind: "none" }
  | { kind: "search"; query: string; root?: string | null }
  | { kind: "refine"; query: string }
  | { kind: "change_root"; root: string | null }
  | { kind: "select"; reference: FileSelectionReference }
  | { kind: "read_selection"; reference: FileSelectionReference }
  | { kind: "open_selection"; reference: FileSelectionReference }
  | { kind: "read_selected" }
  | { kind: "open_selected" }
  | { kind: "needs_disambiguation"; message: string };

  export type ChatUiPayload = {
    route: RouteName;
    reply: string;
    sessionId: string;
    tool: string;
    toolSummary: string;
    searchQuery: string;
    searchRoot: string;
    selectedFile: string;
    readFile: string;
    pendingState: string;
    workflowKind: string;
    workflowStep: string;
    workflowCandidateCount: number;

    activePlan?: {
      id: string;
      title: string;
      status: string;
      stepCount: number;
      activeStep: string | null;
    } | null;
  
    debugTrace?: {
      id: string;
      route: string;
      tool: string;
      success: boolean;
      summary: string;
      steps: {
        type: string;
        label: string;
        detail?: string;
        timestamp: string;
      }[];
    };
  };

export type CommandPipelineResult = {
  payload: ChatUiPayload;
};

FILE: lib\chernobog\pipeline\payload.ts

import { saveMessage } from "@/lib/chernobog/memory";
import { getSessionContext } from "@/lib/chernobog/session/store";
import type { RouteName } from "@/lib/chernobog/session/types";
import { buildWorkflowSnapshot } from "@chernobog/trust/trust/sessionSnapshot";
import { saveTrustTrace } from "@chernobog/trust/trust/store";
import {
  addTraceStep,
  finishTrace,
  printTraceInDev,
  summarizeTrace,
} from "@chernobog/trust/trust/trace";
import type { TrustTrace } from "@chernobog/trust/trust/types";
import type { ChatUiPayload, CommandPipelineResult } from "./types";

export function buildUiPayload(
  sessionId: string,
  route: RouteName,
  reply: string,
  trace?: TrustTrace
): ChatUiPayload {
  const session = getSessionContext(sessionId);
  const workflow = session.workflow;

  const activePlan = session.activePlan
    ? {
        id: session.activePlan.id,
        title: session.activePlan.title,
        status: session.activePlan.status,
        stepCount: session.activePlan.steps.length,
        activeStep:
          session.activePlan.steps.find((step) => step.status === "active")
            ?.title ?? null,
      }
    : null;

  const selectedCandidate =
    workflow.kind === "file"
      ? workflow.candidates.find(
          (candidate) => candidate.id === workflow.selectedCandidateId
        )
      : null;

  const readCandidate =
    workflow.kind === "file"
      ? workflow.candidates.find(
          (candidate) => candidate.id === workflow.readCandidateId
        )
      : null;

  const debugTrace = trace
    ? {
        id: trace.id,
        route: trace.route,
        tool: trace.tool,
        success: trace.success,
        failureCategory: trace.failureCategory,
        summary: summarizeTrace(trace),
        steps: trace.steps.map((step) => ({
          type: step.type,
          label: step.label,
          detail: step.detail,
          timestamp: step.timestamp,
        })),
      }
    : undefined;

  return {
    route,
    reply: reply || "No response returned.",
    sessionId,
    tool: session.lastTool?.name ?? "none",
    toolSummary: session.lastToolResult?.summary ?? "No tool activity yet",
    searchQuery:
      workflow.kind === "file"
        ? workflow.query ?? "none"
        : session.fileContext?.lastSearch?.query ?? "none",
    searchRoot:
      workflow.kind === "file"
        ? workflow.root ?? "none"
        : session.fileContext?.lastSearch?.normalizedRoot ??
          session.fileContext?.lastSearch?.root ??
          "none",
    selectedFile:
      selectedCandidate?.path ??
      session.fileContext?.lastSelected?.path ??
      "none",
    readFile:
      readCandidate?.path ??
      session.fileContext?.lastRead?.path ??
      "none",
    pendingState:
      workflow.kind === "file" && workflow.awaitingDisambiguation
        ? "awaiting_file_selection"
        : session.pendingDisambiguation
          ? "awaiting_file_selection"
          : "none",
    workflowKind: workflow.kind,
    workflowStep: workflow.kind === "file" ? workflow.step : "none",
    workflowCandidateCount:
      workflow.kind === "file" ? workflow.candidates.length : 0,
    activePlan,
    debugTrace,
  };
}

export function finalizePipelinePayload(
  sessionId: string,
  route: RouteName,
  reply: string,
  trace: TrustTrace
): CommandPipelineResult {
  const endingSession = getSessionContext(sessionId);

  addTraceStep(
    trace,
    "workflow_update",
    "Workflow snapshot after command",
    undefined,
    buildWorkflowSnapshot(endingSession)
  );

  finishTrace(trace, route, endingSession.lastTool?.name ?? "none");
  saveTrustTrace(trace);
  printTraceInDev(trace);

  saveMessage("assistant", reply, route, sessionId);

  return {
    payload: buildUiPayload(sessionId, route, reply, trace),
  };
}


============================================================
FILE: lib\chernobog\pipeline\toolExecution.ts
============================================================
import { logToolCall } from "@/lib/chernobog/db";
import {
  clearPendingDisambiguation,
  getSessionContext,
  saveSessionContext,
} from "@/lib/chernobog/session/store";
import { setSelectedFileFromPath, updateSessionFromToolResult } from "@/lib/chernobog/session/update";
import { executeGovernedToolAction } from "@/lib/chernobog/execution/governedToolExecution";


type FindFilesResultData = {
  root: string;
  query: string;
  matches: {
    path: string;
    name: string;
    extension: string;
  }[];
};

type ReadTextFileResultData = {
  path: string;
  content: string;
  truncated: boolean;
};

export type ToolExecutionResult = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof executeGovernedToolAction>>["result"];
export type FileActionTool = "read_text_file" | "open_file";

export function formatToolReply(
  result: ToolExecutionResult,
  sessionId?: string
): string {
  if (!result.ok) {
    return `Tool failed: ${result.error}`;
  }

  switch (result.tool) {
    case "get_time": {
      const data = result.data as { local: string; timezone: string };
      return `The current time is ${data.local} (${data.timezone}).`;
    }

    case "list_files": {
      const data = result.data as {
        path: string;
        entries: { name: string; type: "file" | "directory" }[];
      };

      if (data.entries.length === 0) {
        return `That folder is empty: ${data.path}`;
      }

      const preview = data.entries
        .slice(0, 12)
        .map((entry) =>
          entry.type === "directory" ? `[DIR] ${entry.name}` : entry.name
        )
        .join(", ");

      const extraCount = data.entries.length - 12;
      const suffix = extraCount > 0 ? ` ...and ${extraCount} more.` : ".";

      return `I found ${data.entries.length} item(s) in ${data.path}: ${preview}${suffix}`;
    }

    case "read_text_file": {
      const data = result.data as ReadTextFileResultData;
      return data.truncated
        ? `Here is the start of ${data.path}:\n\n${data.content}\n\n[truncated]`
        : `Here is ${data.path}:\n\n${data.content}`;
    }

    case "open_file":
    case "open_folder":
    case "open_app":
    case "open_url": {
      const data = result.data as { message: string };
      return data.message;
    }

    case "find_files": {
      const data = result.data as FindFilesResultData;

      if (data.matches.length === 0) {
        return `I could not find any files matching "${data.query}" in ${data.root}.`;
      }

      const preview = data.matches
        .slice(0, 5)
        .map((match, index) => `${index + 1}. ${match.name} â€” ${match.path}`)
        .join("\n");

      const extraCount = data.matches.length - 5;
      const suffix = extraCount > 0 ? `\n...and ${extraCount} more.` : "";
      const sessionNote = sessionId
        ? `\nYou can now say things like "read the first one", "open the first one", or "search Documents instead".`
        : "";

      return `I found ${data.matches.length} file(s) matching "${data.query}" in ${data.root}:\n${preview}${suffix}${sessionNote}`;
    }

    default:
      return "Tool executed successfully.";
  }
}

export function looksLikeExplicitFilePath(value: string): boolean {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return false;

  if (/^[a-zA-Z]:\\/.test(trimmed)) return true;
  if (/^\\\\/.test(trimmed)) return true;
  if (trimmed.includes("\\") || trimmed.includes("/")) return true;
  if (/\.[a-zA-Z0-9]{1,10}$/.test(trimmed)) return true;

  return false;
}

function extractSearchQueryFromPathLikeValue(value: string): string {
  return value
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, "")
    .replace(/^(read|open|show)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/^(my|the)\s+/i, "")
    .replace(/\b(file|document|doc)\b/gi, "")
    .replace(/\.[a-zA-Z0-9]{1,10}$/, "")
    .trim();
}

export async function executeAndTrackTool(
  toolName: string,
  input: unknown,
  sessionId: string
): Promise<ToolExecutionResult> {
  const session = getSessionContext(sessionId);

  const governedToolExecution =
    await executeGovernedToolAction(
      toolName,
      input,
      {
        origin: "pipeline",
        sessionId,
        previousState:
          (
            session as typeof session & {
              executionState?: import("@/lib/chernobog/execution").ExecutionState;
            }
          ).executionState,
        context: {
          platform: process.platform,
          sessionId,
        },
      },
    );

  const result =
    governedToolExecution.result;

  (
    session as typeof session & {
      executionState?: import("@/lib/chernobog/execution").ExecutionState;
    }
  ).executionState =
    governedToolExecution.executionState;

  try {
    logToolCall({
      toolName,
      input,
      output: result,
      success: result.ok,
    });
  } catch (logError) {
    console.error("Failed to log tool call:", logError);
  }

  updateSessionFromToolResult(session, toolName, input, result);

  if (result.ok && toolName === "find_files") {
    if (
      !(
        session.workflow.kind === "file" &&
        session.workflow.awaitingDisambiguation
      )
    ) {
      clearPendingDisambiguation(session);
    }
  }

  if (result.ok && toolName === "read_text_file") {
    const data = result.data as ReadTextFileResultData;
    setSelectedFileFromPath(session, "recent_read", data.path);
    clearPendingDisambiguation(session);
  }

  if (result.ok && toolName === "open_file") {
    const data = result.data as { path?: string };

    if (data.path) {
      setSelectedFileFromPath(session, "recent_read", data.path);
    }

    clearPendingDisambiguation(session);
  }

  saveSessionContext(session);
  return result;
}

export async function tryFileSearchFallback(
  requestedPath: string,
  sessionId: string,
  action: FileActionTool
): Promise<string | null> {
  const query = extractSearchQueryFromPathLikeValue(requestedPath);
  if (!query) return null;

  const searchResult = await executeAndTrackTool(
    "find_files",
    { query, maxResults: 8 },
    sessionId
  );

  if (!searchResult.ok) {
    return `I could not search for "${query}".\n${searchResult.error}`;
  }

  const data = searchResult.data as FindFilesResultData;

  if (data.matches.length === 0) {
    return `I could not find any files matching "${query}".`;
  }

  if (data.matches.length === 1) {
    const chosen = data.matches[0];

    const actionResult = await executeAndTrackTool(
      action,
      { path: chosen.path },
      sessionId
    );

    if (!actionResult.ok) {
      return `I found ${chosen.name}, but I could not ${
        action === "read_text_file" ? "read" : "open"
      } it.\n${actionResult.error}`;
    }

    if (action === "read_text_file") {
      const readData = actionResult.data as ReadTextFileResultData;

      return readData.truncated
        ? `I found ${chosen.name} and read the start of it:\n\n${readData.content}\n\n[truncated]`
        : `I found ${chosen.name} and read it:\n\n${readData.content}`;
    }

    const openData = actionResult.data as { message: string };
    return openData.message;
  }

  const preview = data.matches
    .slice(0, 5)
    .map((match, index) => `${index + 1}. ${match.name} â€” ${match.path}`)
    .join("\n");

  return `I found multiple files matching "${query}". Tell me which one you want:\n${preview}`;
}

export function looksLikeVagueFileRequest(message: string): boolean {
  const lower = message.trim().toLowerCase();

  return (
    /^(read|open|show)\s+my\s+.+/.test(lower) ||
    /^(read|open|show)\s+.+\.(txt|md|json|csv|log|cs|ts|js|tsx|jsx|xml|ini|cfg)$/i.test(
      lower
    ) ||
    /\b(file|document|doc|notes|note)\b/.test(lower)
  );
}

FILE: lib\chernobog\router.ts
============================================================
import {
  generateWithModel as generateWithOllama,
} from "@chernobog/models/gateway";
import {
  OLLAMA_LIFECYCLE_DEPENDENCY,
  getLifecycleRuntime,
} from "@chernobog/runtime";
import type {
  OllamaChatMessage,
} from "@chernobog/models";
import type {
  ModelRole,
} from "@chernobog/models/modelRouter";
import {
  buildChernobogPersonalityPrompt,
  type ChernobogResponseMode,
} from "./personality";

import {
  buildWorldModelRepairPrompt,
  shouldValidateWorldModelResponse,
  validateWorldModelResponse,
} from "./worldModel/responseValidation";

const ROUTED_RESPONSE_NUM_PREDICT = 2048;

export type RouteName = "chat" | "planner" | "memory" | "tools" | "guardian";

export type OllamaMessage = OllamaChatMessage;

type ResponseContext = {
  memories?: string[];
  recentMessages?: OllamaMessage[];
  sessionSummary?: string;
  responseMode?: ChernobogResponseMode;
};


const WORLD_MODEL_CRITICAL_START =
  "WORLD MODEL CRITICAL DEPENDENCY BACKBONE";

const WORLD_MODEL_VERBOSE_START =
  "World Model entities (current evidence first; historical tail explicitly labelled):";

function extractCriticalWorldModelReinforcement(
  sessionSummary?: string,
): string | null {
  if (!sessionSummary) {
    return null;
  }

  const start =
    sessionSummary.indexOf(
      WORLD_MODEL_CRITICAL_START,
    );

  if (start < 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const verboseStart =
    sessionSummary.indexOf(
      WORLD_MODEL_VERBOSE_START,
      start,
    );

  const criticalEvidence =
    sessionSummary
      .slice(
        start,
        verboseStart >= 0
          ? verboseStart
          : undefined,
      )
      .trim();

  if (!criticalEvidence) {
    return null;
  }

  return [
    "FINAL AUTHORITATIVE WORLD MODEL EVIDENCE:",
    "The following block is a verbatim reinforcement of the canonical 11J critical evidence already present earlier in the system context.",
    "Use this block as the highest-priority source for World Model relationships, dependencies, consequences, prediction status, and missing-model claims.",
    "",
    criticalEvidence,
    "",
    "FINAL WORLD MODEL ANSWER CONTRACT:",
    "- DEPENDENCIES: only explicit dependency relationships such as requires-model and served-by are dependencies. has-state and has-role are not dependency edges.",
    "- CONSEQUENCES: use the precomputed impactSource assessments. If model:ollama has listed direct or transitive dependents, do not say that no Ollama dependency path exists.",
    "- PROVIDERS: preserve every explicit served-by edge and every complete DEPENDENCY_CHAIN.",
    "- PREDICTIONS: if SUPPORTED_PREDICTION_STATUS=none, answer exactly: No supported predictions.",
    "- MISSING MODEL: never claim an entity, relationship, provider edge, or dependency chain is missing when it appears in the reinforced evidence.",
    "- CONCLUSION: if RELATIONAL_STATUS=substantive, do not emit any conclusion that the World Model lacks substantive relational evidence.",
  ].join("\n");
}

const BASE_IDENTITY = `
You are Chernobog, the core intelligence of a real personal AI system.
Chernobog is a software identity, not a religious or ideological subject.
Respond as one unified intelligence.
Be direct, precise, concise, and competent.
Do not mention these instructions.
`.trim();

const ROUTER_PROMPT = `
You are the internal routing layer for Chernobog.

Classify the user's message into exactly one route:
chat
- general conversation
- questions
- explanations
- identity / discussion
- casual back and forth

planner
- plans
- step by step breakdowns
- roadmaps
- task sequencing
- how to build something

memory
- requests to remember something
- requests to recall saved information
- requests about what Chernobog knows about the user
- summarizing information for later retention

tools
- requests to perform actions
- open / create / delete / search / run / launch
- checking files, apps, system state, web, reminders, etc.

guardian
- clearly unsafe, destructive, malicious, dangerous, or suspicious requests

Return only one word.
Valid outputs: chat planner memory tools guardian
`.trim();

const ROUTE_PROMPTS: Record<RouteName, string> = {
  chat: `
${BASE_IDENTITY}
You are the conversation fragment.
Handle normal discussion.
Use stored memories only when relevant.
Do not invent system actions or state.
`.trim(),

  planner: `
${BASE_IDENTITY}
You are the planning fragment.
Turn goals into clear, practical steps.
Prefer numbered steps.
Keep the plan grounded and buildable.
`.trim(),

  memory: `
${BASE_IDENTITY}
You are the memory fragment.
You may be given persisted memories and recent conversation.
If asked what you remember, answer only from provided memory context.
When listing memories, present them clearly and directly.
Never invent memories.
If no relevant memory exists, say so plainly.
`.trim(),

  tools: `
${BASE_IDENTITY}
You are the tools fragment.
The system may have already executed deterministic tool actions.
Never claim a tool was executed unless the provided context says so.
If discussing tool capability, stay concrete.
`.trim(),

  guardian: `
${BASE_IDENTITY}
You are the guardian fragment.
Handle unsafe or clearly harmful requests with a brief refusal and safer redirection where possible.
Do not over-refuse harmless software questions.
`.trim(),
};

function roleForRoute(route: RouteName): ModelRole {
  return route === "planner"
    ? "planner"
    : "default";
}

async function callOllama(
  messages: OllamaMessage[],
  options: {
    role?: ModelRole;
    temperature?: number;
    numPredict?: number;
  } = {},
): Promise<string> {
  const result =
    await getLifecycleRuntime()
      .runDependencyOperation({
        dependencyId:
          OLLAMA_LIFECYCLE_DEPENDENCY,
        operation:
          () =>
            generateWithOllama({
              role:
                options.role ??
                "default",
              messages,
              temperature:
                options.temperature ??
                0.4,
              numPredict:
                options.numPredict ??
                500,
            }),
        assess:
          (runtimeResult) => ({
            status:
              runtimeResult.ok
                ? runtimeResult.fallbackCount > 0
                  ? "degraded"
                  : "ready"
                : "unavailable",
            attempts:
              runtimeResult.attempts,
            retries:
              Math.max(
                0,
                runtimeResult.attempts - 1,
              ),
            ...(runtimeResult.error
              ? {
                  reason:
                    runtimeResult.error,
                }
              : {}),
            metadata: {
              fallbackCount:
                runtimeResult.fallbackCount,
            },
          }),
      });

  if (!result.ok || !result.text) {
    throw new Error(
      result.error ??
        "No response returned from the local model.",
    );
  }

  return result.text;
}

function normalizeRoute(raw: string): RouteName {
  const match = raw.toLowerCase().match(/\b(chat|planner|memory|tools|guardian)\b/);
  return (match?.[1] as RouteName) ?? "chat";
}

export async function routeMessage(userMessage: string): Promise<RouteName> {
  const rawRoute = await callOllama(
    [
      { role: "system", content: ROUTER_PROMPT },
      { role: "user", content: userMessage },
    ],
    {
      role: "default",
    },
  );

  return normalizeRoute(rawRoute);
}

export async function respondForRoute(
  route: RouteName,
  userMessage: string,
  context: ResponseContext = {}
): Promise<string> {
  const messages: OllamaMessage[] = [
    {
      role: "system",
      content: ROUTE_PROMPTS[route],
    },
  ];

  if (context.memories && context.memories.length > 0) {
    messages.push({
      role: "system",
      content: [
        "Persisted user memories:",
        ...context.memories.map((memory) => `- ${memory}`),
        "Use these only when relevant.",
        "Never invent additional memories.",
      ].join("\n"),
    });
  }

  if (context.sessionSummary) {
    messages.push({
      role: "system",
      content: `Active short-term session context:\n${context.sessionSummary}`,
    });
  }

  if (context.recentMessages && context.recentMessages.length > 0) {
    messages.push(...context.recentMessages);
  }

  if (
    context.sessionSummary &&
    context.recentMessages &&
    context.recentMessages.length > 0
  ) {
    messages.push({
      role: "system",
      content: [
        "Authoritative context precedence:",
        "The current runtime/session context supplied above is newer and more authoritative than earlier assistant statements in conversation history.",
        "If an earlier assistant response conflicts with current runtime state, project state, scoped memory, or current user instructions, disregard the stale assistant response.",
        "Do not repeat an earlier claim that information is missing when the current authoritative context now supplies that information.",
      ].join("\n"),
    });
  }



  const worldModelReinforcement =
    extractCriticalWorldModelReinforcement(
      context.sessionSummary,
    );

  if (worldModelReinforcement) {
    messages.push({
      role: "system",
      content: worldModelReinforcement,
    });
  }


  messages.push({
    role: "system",
    content: buildChernobogPersonalityPrompt(
      context.responseMode ?? "text",
    ),
  });

  messages.push({
    role: "user",
    content: userMessage,
  });

  const initialReply =
    await callOllama(
      messages,
      {
        role:
          roleForRoute(route),
        numPredict:
          ROUTED_RESPONSE_NUM_PREDICT,
      },
    );

  if (
    !shouldValidateWorldModelResponse(
      userMessage,
      context.sessionSummary,
    )
  ) {
    return initialReply;
  }

  const validation =
    await validateWorldModelResponse(
      userMessage,
      initialReply,
    );

  if (validation.valid) {
    return initialReply;
  }

  try {
    const repairedReply =
      await callOllama(
        [
          {
            role: "system",
            content: [
              "You are the Chernobog grounded World Model response repair pass.",
              "Repair only semantic grounding errors identified by the validator.",
              "Canonical 11J evidence below is authoritative.",
              "Do not execute tools or claim that any tool was executed.",
            ].join("\n"),
          },
          {
            role: "system",
            content:
              validation.canonicalEvidenceText,
          },
          {
            role: "user",
            content:
              buildWorldModelRepairPrompt(
                userMessage,
                initialReply,
                validation,
              ),
          },
        ],
        {
          role:
            roleForRoute(route),
          temperature: 0.1,
          numPredict:
            ROUTED_RESPONSE_NUM_PREDICT,
        },
      );

    const repairedValidation =
      await validateWorldModelResponse(
        userMessage,
        repairedReply,
      );

    if (repairedValidation.valid) {
      return repairedReply;
    }

    return repairedValidation.fallbackText;
  } catch {
    return validation.fallbackText;
  }
}
